- **APP-039** Edit ▸ Undo (⌘Z) and Edit ▸ Redo (⇧⌘Z) shall revert and reapply curation choices — Like, Mark as Not Rated, Dislike — from every surface that submits them, and edits (APP-046), on the window's one undo stack. [verified]
  - The inverse of a user choice shall be registered as the choice is shown (APP-086), and restore the prior choice per asset the Store reports from the write's own transaction, so a batch whose members held mixed values is restored per asset.
  - If undo is pressed before the write answers, then the undo shall be written after it, from those priors.
  - Only rows the write actually changed shall be restored; a choice that changed nothing shall take its entry back off the stack when the Store says so.
  - Undo and redo shall travel the same serial curation lane as user choices, never advance the selection, and register their own inverse synchronously so redo works after undo.
  - When an undo or redo's items are not the current targets, Otos shall select them — the loupe moves to the first, the grid scrolls to it — so a change is never made out of sight (`hig-validation.md` F-18); edits shall reveal their photo the same way.
  - Menu titles shall name the act (`Undo Like`, `Redo Dislike`) and both items shall be disabled when their stack is empty.
  - Move to Trash shall be on the same stack (`Undo Move to Trash` / `Redo Move to Trash`): undo withdraws items still pending (they reappear with no filesystem call) and moves items already in the system Trash back to their original paths from the location `trashItem` reported, never over an existing file, showing each kept row again with everything it held — choice, edit, import batch, thumbnail.
  - If an item cannot be put back, then it shall fail truthfully with the `This item couldn’t be put back.` / `Some items couldn’t be put back.` alert.
  - Redo shall hide the items again under a new command.
  - `Undo Move to Trash` shall be registered as the items leave the window, before the Store records the move (APP-085); the mark, undo, redo and the answers to the trash prompts shall reach the queue through one lossless ordered stream, so an undo is always applied after the mark it undoes and before a redo that follows it.
  - Trash undo entries shall live in memory for the session; after a relaunch Finder's Put Back returns an item with its overlay, because its row is kept while the file is in the Trash (ARC-058, ADR 0022).
  - People's acts shall be on the same stack (APP-072), registered as each act is shown (APP-087): each undo or redo registers its opposite synchronously inside the manager's undo or redo, so redoing one of several undone acts keeps the rest to redo, and writes back the state the write before it replaced (`PeopleUndoRecord`), so a redone act returns the catalog to the state the act left — a person it created comes back under the same ID and later acts naming them redo too.
  - The ViewModel shall own the one `UndoManager` and publish `UndoMenuState` for the menu.
  Example: Given two selected items and Like, ⌘Z returns both to Not Rated and ⇧⌘Z likes them again; given items previously Liked, Disliked and Not Rated all marked Not Rated, ⌘Z restores each to its own prior value; given Mark as Not Rated on an already Not Rated item, Undo stays disabled; given advance-after-choice on, ⌘Z leaves the selection where the advance put it; given Move to Trash, ⌘Z puts the files back with their curation choice and ⇧⌘Z moves them again; given a file now occupies a trashed item's path, undo leaves both files untouched and reports the failure.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelCuration.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModel.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/OtosApp.swift`

- **APP-040** The loupe image shall zoom: pinch and spread about the cursor, two-finger pan when zoomed, smart magnify, ⌘= / ⌘− (View ▸ Zoom In / Zoom Out — the same menu items that change grid density in grid mode and do nothing in compare), double-click on the image and Z (View ▸ Actual Size at fit, Zoom to Fit otherwise — the item names the act it does) toggling fit and 100 percent about the click point, and Esc returning to fit before a second Esc leaves the loupe. [verified]
  - The zoom shall be an `NSScrollView` with `allowsMagnification` whose document has the shape of the decode on screen at the original's resolution (its native long edge) over the backing scale, so magnification 1 is one original pixel per device pixel and a catalog size that disagrees with the decode (a HEIF turned by its `irot` property, cataloged with its stored size) never stretches the picture.
  - The range shall be fit to 100 percent, or fit alone when a small image cannot exceed it.
  - Every path shall resolve its target through `LoupeZoomPolicy`.
  - The ViewModel shall own zoom intent as a generation-stamped command and receive what the view measured, taking a report only when it names the focused photo (the loupe keeps showing the previous photo until the next one's decode lands, and a report measured on it is not the next one's).
  - A focus change and a mode exit shall reset to fit.
  - When a report shows the held decode is short of what the magnification displays, Otos shall request one full-resolution decode for the focused asset through the same `PreviewImageLoader` and the same live materialization gate (ARC-034); the fit-size decode shall stay painted until the sharper one arrives and never be blanked.
  - Zoom shall animate 0.2 s and be immediate under Reduce Motion (DES-075).
  - Concurrent requests for one asset shall share a decode only when it is at least as large as the one requested, and a smaller decode finishing late shall never replace a larger cached one.
  - Short of full resolution, the loupe and compare shall decode the still's stored preview without touching the original, cloud-only or not, sizing the 100% document from the original's oriented size in stored metadata.
  - A still with no stored preview yet shall be rendered from its original at the stored preview's size, short of full resolution, through the interactive `ImageRenderer` (a RAW never as its embedded JPEG), and shown from an IOSurface — except an upright, unedited JPEG that Image I/O decodes whole at that size anyway (a 12 MP JPEG), which shall be shown whole as Image I/O returns it, its pixels in the JPEG's own YCbCr layout (`OriginalRoute.wholeShown`).
  - Full resolution shall render the original (ADR 0035).
  Example: Given a 6000-px image in a 1500-pt pane on a 2× display, fit is 0.5 and the ceiling is 1; given a double-click at fit, magnification becomes 1 about the click point and a full-resolution decode is requested once; given ⌘= at fit, magnification steps by 1.25×; given Esc while zoomed, the image returns to fit and the loupe stays; given Esc at fit, the grid returns; given ⌘= in grid, the column count changes and no zoom command is issued; given a focus move, the next image opens at fit.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/LoupeZoomPolicy.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/LoupeZoomView.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelLoupeZoom.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelViewMode.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/PreviewImageLoader.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/PanePreview.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/LoupeView.swift`, `OtosKit/OtosApp.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogQueryGateway.swift`

- **APP-041** I, View ▸ Show Info / Hide Info and the toolbar's Info toggle shall show the inspector's Info pane (DES-088) — the trailing column it shares with Edit, one pane at a time: I and E each show their pane, switch the column to it, or hide the column when it is the pane shown — in every view mode. [verified]
  - For exactly one target, the pane shall show the file name, when it was taken (with the file's UTC offset when it records one), and the sections, in this order: a still's Description (APP-077), People, In This Photo, Location, File, Camera, and Exposure for a still or Video for a movie.
  - People shall show each face as its crop with its person's name or `Unidentified`, and the face acts of DES-113.
  - With no faces listed, People shall show `No Faces` only once the catalog says the photo was read for faces and holds none, `Not Checked Yet` before it was read, and `—` while that is unknown, for several photos, and when its only faces are a hidden person's — read by `CatalogStore.facesRead(for:)` off the main actor and read again for the photo shown at every new target and People state, so a photo read while shown says so without being reselected.
  - A hidden person's face shall be left out (DES-111).
  - An unidentified face's buttons shall appear only once it is ranked, their row's height kept meanwhile; another target, folding the section or hiding the pane shall let go of a row's outline.
  - In This Photo (DES-088) shall show the scene labels of its search reading that pass Vision's high-precision test, most confident first, without one a shown descendant scores within 0.05 of (`SceneLabelHierarchy.tsv`, ADR 0050).
  - Each scene label shall be named in the Mac's language where Otos ships a name that read back into English as the same label (`SceneLabelNames`, ADR 0050) and in English otherwise, each name shown once.
  - Each scene label shall be a button that searches for its label itself (by its English name, with no language recognised and no facts read) while the toolbar field shows the chip's name, laid out no wider than the pane, a long name truncated.
  - In This Photo shall then show `Text` and the lines Vision read with full confidence, each once, without a phone screenshot's status bar (`SearchTextReading.shown`), selectable, its first 8 lines and a Show More button when it is longer (Show Less once open; the next photo's text starts closed), or `Text` over `Not Checked Yet` while a still's preview is unread.
  - In This Photo shall show `Not Checked Yet` before the thumbnail is read, and `Nothing Recognized` only when both readings are made and found nothing (a movie has no text reading); a thumbnail or preview that does not decode is no reading and shall stay `Not Checked Yet`.
  - In This Photo shall be re-read for the item shown whenever search's index announces new readings and again after each Info read lands, so a reading announced while that read was in flight is not lost.
  - Location shall show place name, a map, coordinates, altitude.
  - File shall show kind, dimensions with megapixels, size, library and folder, whether the original is on this Mac, created, modified, import, companions, edits.
  - Camera shall show camera, lens, serial number.
  - Exposure for a still shall show shutter speed, aperture, ISO, focal length with its 35 mm equivalent, compensation, program, metering, flash, white balance — as MediaMetadata reports them — and Video for a movie shall show duration, frame rate, codec.
  - With several targets, People shall list each visible person in them with their photo count and the unidentified faces, the three people those most likely are offered as buttons; hovering one face shall outline it on the loupe's photo, through the photo's saved crop and straighten.
  - Every value but the place name shall be something the catalog or the original's locality already holds, read together off the main actor; nothing shall read the original's bytes.
  - The File section's `Original` (`On This Mac`, `Not Downloaded`, `Unavailable`) shall be read again each time a download Otos ran makes an original local (`MaterializationCoordinator.landings()`), so it never keeps saying `Not Downloaded` for a photo Otos just fetched.
  - A fact the catalog lacks shall read `Unknown` rather than being omitted (APP-001); with no single target the title shall be `No Selection` or the item count and every value shall read `—`.
  - The place name shall be looked up from the coordinate through MapKit for the item shown, never stored, and read `Unknown` until it arrives or when the lookup fails (ADR 0050).
  - If a read fails, then it shall not be treated as a lacking fact: nothing reads `Unknown` for it, the pane keeps what it showed for the same asset and drops another asset's, and the next refresh reads again.
  - The pane shall be session-only and follow every writer of the target.
  - Leaving the Edit pane shall let go of the photo it edits.
  Example: Given one photo selected in the grid and I, the Info pane shows its file name and facts; given an asset with no capture date, that line reads `Unknown`; given the focus moves in the loupe, the pane shows the new asset; given E, the column switches to Edit and Info is dropped; given E again, the column hides; given the focus moves and the asset read fails, the previous asset's info is dropped and the next refresh shows the new asset's facts; given a still with Exif exposure, its rows show it as photographers write it (`1/250 s`, `ƒ/2.8`); given a movie, Video replaces Exposure; given three items selected, the title reads `3 Items` and every value `—`; given a photo with Sam's face and an unidentified one, People lists `Sam` and `Unidentified`, Sam's face left out once she is hidden; given a face hovered on a cropped photo, its outline is the box mapped through the crop; given a still whose reading holds the confident labels document and screenshot and the certain line `BRIDGE CLOSED AHEAD` beside an unsure one, In This Photo shows the two labels and only the certain line, and before its thumbnail is read it reads `Not Checked Yet`; given its labels read and none found while its text is unread, it is not `Nothing Recognized`; given a movie whose labels are read and empty, it is; given Finnish names for dog and canine that are both `koira`, one chip. Given an SUV read `machine 0.535, vehicle 0.523, automobile 0.523, outdoor 0.514, land 0.514, license_plate 0.339`, the chips are Automobile, Land and License plate; given a phone screenshot, its clock and battery lines are not shown, and a line read twice is shown once. Given a 40-line reading, Text shows 8 lines and Show More; given a 2-line sign, both lines and no button.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/PhotoInfo.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/SceneLabelNames.swift`, `OtosKit/Resources/SceneLabelNames.tsv`, `OtosKit/Services/SearchLabelGate.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/SearchTextReading.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/EditGeometry.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/LoupeZoomView.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelPeople.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/AssetMetadata.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/InfoPaneView.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelInspector.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MediaEnricher.swift`, `OtosKit/ContentView.swift`, `OtosKit/OtosApp.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MaterializationCoordinator.swift`

- **APP-042** When the filter or the sort changes, Otos shall keep the user's place in the same collection; only a sidebar scope change shall start over at the first item with nothing selected. [verified]
  - Selected items that survive the filter shall stay selected.
  - The grid shall keep the first visible tile that is still selected at its on-screen position, even at the top of the grid.
  - Without one, the item at the viewport's top edge shall keep its position, and if the filter removed that item, its nearest survivor shall take its place — the first later survivor in the order the user was looking at, else the closest earlier one.
  - At the top of the grid with nothing selected visible, the grid shall stay at the top.
  - The loupe shall stay on the focused item if it survives and otherwise move to its nearest survivor; compare shall keep surviving pair members.
  Example: Given a scrolled grid and a filter that keeps the top item, that item stays at the top edge at the same offset; given a filter that removes it, the nearest later survivor in the previous order takes its place; given the same filtered snapshot applied without the anchor, the top item changes (negative control); given a visible selected tile and a sort that reverses the order, that tile keeps its on-screen offset; given the grid at the top with nothing selected, a filter leaves it at the top; given a filter change, surviving selected items stay selected and a sort change keeps the whole selection; given the loupe focused on an item a filter keeps, focus stays, and on one it drops, focus moves to the nearest later survivor; given the same identities under a new scope, the gate scrolls to top.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/BrowserViewMode.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModel.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelViewMode.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/MediaGridMutationGate.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/MediaGridBridge.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/MediaGridStructuralApply.swift`

- **APP-043** Otos shall reopen where the user left it. [verified]
  - At quit the ViewModel shall save one browser place — sidebar scope (every kind, Places and an area under it included), filter, sort, view mode, the selection in display order, and the item to bring back into view (the loupe's focused item, or the grid's middle visible item) — as a versioned preference value.
  - At launch the first query shall use the saved scope, filter and sort.
  - Once the catalog has loaded its roots, a saved scope that still exists shall replace the launch default, including a card connected at launch; one that is gone (including All Media when fewer than two fixed libraries exist) shall fall back to the launch default, keep the saved sort, start that row unfiltered (APP-013), and drop the rest of the place.
  - When the first non-empty result for the saved query arrives, the surviving selection shall be restored, then the grid shall center on the saved middle item, the loupe reopen on the saved focus, or compare reopen on the saved pair.
  - A scope with one presentation (the Places map, the duplicates list, where a Duplicates group reopens) shall restore no view mode.
  - Saved items that no longer exist shall be dropped.
  - If a stored value is missing, unreadable, of another version, or outside today's vocabulary, then it shall mean no place.
  - Any query change before the restore shall drop it, and quitting before it has applied shall keep the stored place.
  - A read-only tour session (ADR 0020) shall save nothing of Otos's: its preferences are session-only, so its place, zoom, the panes' folded sections and settings never reach the stored ones, whether or not it put the window back before quitting.
  Example: Given a saved place, the save/load round trip is lossless for every scope kind; given a missing, malformed, other-version, or unknown-vocabulary value, nothing loads; given a stored place, the new ViewModel's first descriptor and sidebar selection are the saved ones; given the first loaded result, grid restores surviving selection and centers on the saved item, loupe reopens on its focus, and compare reopens on the saved pair; given the saved library is gone, or the saved All Media is hidden with one library, the default applies with the saved sort, no filter, and no selection or mode restored; given a sort change before load, the pending place is dropped; given a grid with a selection and a viewport report, quit saves the query, mode, display-ordered selection, and middle visible item; given quit before the restore applied, the stored place is unchanged; given a tour session's filter, sort, search, Compare, inspector, zoom and Info and Edit section-fold intents and a quit with or without its restore, the stored preferences are byte-identical, while an ordinary session's same intents write them.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/BrowserPlace.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModel.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelBrowserPlace.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/SidebarSelectionPolicy.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/SidebarView.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/PreferenceStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Support/SmokeTour.swift`

- **APP-044** The window shall claim nothing about the catalog before its initial load has applied. [verified]
  - Until then the window title shall claim no catalog name (APP-090), the grid shall show no items and no empty-state message (`.loadingCatalog`, drawn as nothing), the sidebar shall draw no row and no section header until a frame has been presented while the catalog opens, and then only the fixed rows of APP-090, the duplicates pane shall draw nothing (and keep drawing nothing, with the Duplicates row uncounted, until the groups are first built), and Projection shall run no catalog query.
  - The catalog shall start loading when the app finishes launching, before the main window is built, so its load overlaps the window's first frame.
  - The first publication after the load shall not wait for the background cadence, and shall read the filter's options beside the order.
  - The load's maintenance writes (abandoned claims, unrendered edits, undecodable stills, awaiting-presence marks) shall each find their rows through a partial index, never a walk of every row.
  - A catalog that is loaded and empty shall still say No Media.
  Example: Given a projection subscribed to an unstarted store, its first state is `.loadingCatalog` with an empty title; given the store then loads an empty catalog, every state before the first claim is `.loadingCatalog` and the first claim is `.noMedia`; given a one-minute cadence, the state carrying the loaded library arrives within the ten-second test wait, so only an immediate publication passes.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/CatalogProjection.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/CatalogWindowState.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/MediaGridView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/CatalogMediaEmptyStatePresentation.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/DuplicatesView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/SidebarView.swift`, `OtosKit/OtosApp.swift`

- **APP-045** If a Move to Trash's cloud-only items need more than 100 MB downloaded before they can go to the system Trash, then Otos shall first ask `Download <size> to move N items to the Trash?` (or `… “name” …` for one item; DES-090), counting only the catalog sizes of members that are dataless at that moment and reading no file bytes. [verified]
  - A member whose size the catalog does not know shall never be counted as zero: the known part is compared with the threshold and stated as a lower bound (`Download at least <size> …`), and the trash status bar states `at least <size> to download`, or no figure when no size is known.
  - `Download and Move to Trash` (default, not destructive-styled) shall move the items the user was asked about; `Cancel` shall move nothing.
  - The alert shall carry the system suppression checkbox (`Do not ask again`), and Settings ▸ General shall carry `Ask before downloading more than 100 MB to move items to the Trash`; both shall edit the one preference `trash.asksBeforeLargeDownload` (default on) through one ViewModel writer.
  - With the preference off, or at or below 100 MB, Move to Trash shall act at once.
  - A command whose items' own files are all local shall be sized from their files' flags without reading the catalog; only the items whose own file is cloud-only shall be read for their members' sizes, so a cloud-only companion of a local file is downloaded by the queue without being counted toward the question.
  - The question shall be answered from the targets' files as read when the targets last changed (`docs/research/2026-11-15-trash-question-at-selection.md`): every change of the targets reads, off the main actor, the command they would make, and a command of exactly those files asks or moves in the step that applies it.
  - A command made before its targets' reading finished, or one that is not the current targets' (Duplicates' Move All), shall read for itself before anything is hidden.
  - A file evicted after the reading shall be moved without the question, under the queue's free-space floor and failed-download question; one downloaded after it shall still be asked about.
  Example: Given 1.2 GB of cloud-only members and the preference on, the question is asked and nothing is hidden until it is answered; given only local items, nothing is asked; given the preference turned off in either place, it stays off after relaunch.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/TrashPrompts.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelTrash.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelOpenWith.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModel.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MediaTrashCoordinator.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/SettingsView.swift`, `OtosKit/ContentView.swift`, `OtosKit/OtosApp.swift`
  Not covered by tests: alert and suppression checkbox rendering need UI automation

- **APP-046** The edit panel (DES-092; shown and hidden by View ▸ Show Edit / Hide Edit, E and the toolbar button) shall edit the current target when it is exactly one image or RAW, with the Light section's Exposure, Contrast, Highlights (−100…100, positive lifting the upper tones through a tone curve) and Shadows plus Reset. [verified]
  - When the target resolves, the photo shall be opened (a cloud-only original downloads at interactive priority) and its facts published; the controls shall act only once it is open.
  - How the open went shall be the target's state (opening, opened, failed).
  - If the original cannot be made local or read, then the open shall fail: the controls stay disabled with the tooltip `Can’t read the original right now` and the histogram's well says the same (DES-100).
  - Whichever comes first shall resolve and open it again: the next target refresh for that photo, the next publication whose rows or roots changed (a file written again, a root that came back), or a change FSEvents reports at the original's own path or any folder above it (`OriginalPathWatch`: the file put back, its folder restored, its volume mounted again), which reopens an original that returns exactly as it was and so changes no row.
  - An original that was not local shall be looked at once more as soon as that watch is armed, so a return between the failed read and the watch is not missed.
  - A refresh of a photo that is open or opening shall leave it alone, and nothing shall retry on a timer (ADR 0033).
  - Dragging a slider shall render the draft live from the original on `LiveEditRenderer`, at preview size, in the loupe or compare pane, and save nothing.
  - Letting go of the slider, a keyboard step on it, or Reset shall save the recipe once through one ordered lane under the next edit revision (ADR 0036), counted on from the photo's last revision even when it holds no edit, so a revision never names two edits.
  - The save shall register one undo step named for the control (`Undo Exposure`, `Undo Reset Light`).
  - The save shall then render the saved recipe's 2560 px preview and 256 px thumbnail, which the store promotes only while that revision is current and the original's catalog size and mtime are unchanged.
  - The pane shall keep the live render until its own preview carries the save, raising no progress indicator while that preview loads (DES-086), and the grid tile shall repaint with the edited thumbnail (APP-015).
  - A save's preview and thumbnail shall be encoded off the live renderer, so they do not hold up the next live render.
  - Edit ▸ Undo and Redo shall restore recipes through the same lane and move the panel's values with them.
  - If a render cannot happen, then the photo shall be handed to enrichment, which renders with the saved recipe.
  - With no single photo to edit, the controls shall be disabled.
  - Each control's value shall also be a field: a typed value (with sign, degree mark or kelvin unit) is saved as one step on Return or leaving the field, an unparsable one is put back, and Escape restores it; while a field is edited, Edit ▸ Cut, Copy, Paste and Select All act on its text (DES-093).
  - Sections shall fold under disclosure triangles, open by default and remembered across launches.
  - If the target's tile-detail read fails, then the panel shall let go of the photo it showed and hold no target until the next refresh reads it (ARC-059).
  - Hiding the panel shall let go of the photo.
  - Until the photo is open, each value field shall show `—`, never the control's name.
  Example: Given a drag on Exposure, a live image appears and nothing is saved until release; given release, the recipe is saved at revision 1 and the tile detail's rendered revision becomes 1; given a photo at revision 2 holding no edit, a new edit is saved at revision 3; given ⌘Z then ⇧⌘Z, the recipe goes to neutral and back with the panel; given two photos selected, there is no edit target; given the next photo's read fails, there is no edit target and the next refresh opens it; given its original's status fails, the pane says the original can't be read, and the next file event's publication, a change at its path with no catalog change, or opening it in the loupe, opens it and its histogram arrives; given the original back before its watch started, it opens with no event; given a watch on a path whose folder does not exist yet, putting the file back there is reported; given a superseded revision's products, they are refused and their staged files deleted.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/EditRecipe.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/LiveEditRenderer.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/ImageRenderer.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/EditSession.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/OriginalPathWatch.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/EditPanelView.swift`, `OtosKit/ContentView.swift`, `OtosKit/OtosApp.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`

- **APP-047** The edit panel's Color section shall offer White Balance (Temperature, Tint, and a picker), Vibrance and Saturation, with Reset (ADR 0036). [verified]
  - A RAW's white balance shall be absolute (2000–50000 K on a slider even in mireds, 50 K steps; tint −150…150) and read its camera's as-shot values until moved.
  - Any other still's white balance shall be a relative −100…100 through `CITemperatureAndTint`, zero meaning as shot.
  - A RAW's temperature slider shall carry labels at 2500, 4000 and 10000 K under its track.
  - The picker (also Photo ▸ Pick White Balance, W) shall be enabled in the loupe; while armed, the loupe cursor shall be a crosshair and one click shall make that point neutral, saved as one `White Balance` undo step.
  - A RAW shall use `CIRAWFilter.neutralLocation` in output coordinates; another still shall solve for the relative values that gray the sampled color.
  - Escape shall disarm it first.
  - Each section's Reset shall return only its own controls to neutral.
  Example: Given a warm-cast still, picking its center saves a negative temperature and renders it gray within 2%; given +50 temperature on a gray still, it renders warmer; given saturation −100, the color is all but removed; given Reset Color, Light values are kept; given a RAW at as shot, the panel reads its camera kelvin; given the grid, the picker is disabled; given Escape with the picker armed, the loupe stays open.

- **APP-048** The edit panel's Crop tool (DES-092) shall crop, straighten, turn and flip the photo (ADR 0036). [verified]
  - Photo ▸ Crop, C and the panel's Crop button shall turn it on, first showing the panel and switching to the loupe if needed.
  - The loupe shall then show the photo with every edit but its crop, with the crop drawn over it.
  - The crop's frame shall be the original's upright size as the decode the photo is edited from reports it, never the catalog's.
  - Dragging inside shall move the crop; a handle shall resize it about the opposite corner or edge; a constrained aspect shall keep its shape and shrink to stay in the frame.
  - A drag shall save one `Crop` undo step when let go and render nothing while it moves.
  - The tool shall start with the aspect Custom, so a crop takes whatever shape suits the photo.
  - Choosing an aspect shall fit the largest crop of that shape around the current center; Rotate Aspect shall turn it.
  - A constrained aspect shall be portrait or landscape as the opened photo's upright shape is, until Rotate Aspect chooses.
  - Done, Return, C, Escape (after a picker), leaving the loupe, another photo or hiding the panel shall end the tool.
  - With Custom, Shift during a handle drag shall keep the crop's shape.
  - While the tool is on, its canvas shall hold first responder and return it when the tool ends; arrow keys shall nudge the crop one frame pixel (ten with Shift) and a held key shall save one step on release.
  - Rotate Left (⌘[), Rotate Right (⌘]), Flip Horizontal and Flip Vertical shall save at once, with the crop turning or mirroring with the photo.
  - Straighten (−45…45°, clockwise positive) shall rotate about the center and scale to keep the frame covered.
  - The Crop section's rows shall stand in three groups, 12 pt apart (DES-092): Straighten with `Level`, Rotate & Flip, and Aspect with Suggested.
  - `Level ▸ Auto` shall straighten the photo by its level reading as one `Auto Straighten` step (ADR 0045): the camera's gravity reading, which MediaMetadata reports and the photo's opening reads from the original, else GeoCalib's roll of the unedited photo when its uncertainty is at most 3°.
  - The reading shall be reversed when the photo is flipped once, and the button shall be available only once a level is trusted.
  - With the tool on, a line drawn on the photo — ⌘-drag, or a drag with the ruler armed (arming it turns the tool on) — shall turn it so that line is level, or plumb when drawn nearer vertical, as one `Straighten` step.
  - `Suggested ▸ Crop` shall apply FocalNet's crop of the frame as saved, in any shape, as one `Auto Crop` step (ADR 0045).
  - The frame shall be read when the photo opens and again when a turn, flip or straighten changes it; the button shall be available only for the frame the draft shows and only when FocalNet suggests a crop.
  - Both shall read only the photo being edited: a read for a photo already left develops nothing.
  - A cropped photo shall be developed from a frame large enough for its crop to reach the requested size.
  - The pane's native size shall be the edited photo's, and the white balance picker shall sample the developed pixel under the click through the geometry.
  Example: Given a crop drag released, the recipe holds the crop and `Undo Crop` is offered; given Escape, the tool ends in the loupe; given leaving the loupe, it ends; given Rotate Right then Undo, the turns go 1 then 0; given four turns or two flips, the recipe returns to start; given a split red/blue photo, a turn puts red on top, a flip puts blue on the left, and a left-half crop is all red at 48×64; given straighten 12°, the size is unchanged and the corners are filled; given an iPhone back camera's gravity reading for −3°, Auto saves −3° as `Undo Auto Straighten`, and +3° once flipped; given that reading from a front camera, Auto never uses it; given a crop reading for the frame shown, Suggested ▸ Crop saves it as `Undo Auto Crop`, and a turn makes the button wait for the turned frame's reading; given a read for a photo no longer open, nothing is developed and the open photo still reads; given fields drawn from a known camera, the solver recovers its roll within 0.01°; given the ruler pressed with the tool off, the tool turns on armed; given a reading for another turn, or none, nothing changes; given a line drawn rising 4° to the right, the straighten is +4° as `Undo Straighten`; given a line nearer vertical, it is made plumb; given a half-width crop rendered at long edge 64, the output's long edge is 64; given a flipped photo, a picker click on its left half samples the original's right half.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/EditRecipe.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/EditGeometry.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/ImageRenderer.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/LiveEditRenderer.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/AutoCropModel.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/AutoLevelModel.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/PerspectiveFieldSolver.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/EditSessionCrop.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/EditSession.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/CropEditorView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/EditPanelView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/LoupeView.swift`, `OtosKit/OtosApp.swift`

- **APP-049** The edit panel shall offer Effects (Vignette amount −100…100 with negative darkening, Midpoint, Feather; applied after the crop as an ellipse fitted to the frame), Detail (Sharpening and Noise Reduction for every still: a RAW's through `CIRAWFilter`, starting at its decoder's own amounts read when it opens; any other still's through `CISharpenLuminance` and `CINoiseReduction`; Color Noise, Detail and Moiré only where the RAW decoder supports them) and Optics (Lens Correction, only for a RAW with a lens profile, on by its decoder's default) (ADR 0036). - **APP-050** When the video shown in the loupe is prepared there, Photo ▸ Trim Video… (⌘T) shall show `AVPlayerView`'s trim UI on it, and the menu item shall be enabled only while one is prepared there (ADR 0036). [verified]
  - Trim shall save the chosen range — read from the player item's reverse and forward playback end times — as the video's `trimStart` / `trimEnd` in one `Trim` undo step.
  - Cancel shall save nothing.
  - A prepared video shall play only its trimmed range, and undo and redo shall move the player's range with the recipe.
  - Saving a video's recipe shall mark its products current in the same transaction, so nothing is rendered or downloaded for it.
  Example: Given a trim of 0.5–1.25 s applied to the player, the player item reports that range back, and an untrimmed recipe clears it; given a saved trim on a video row, the save reports a video and its rendered revision equals its edit revision.

- **APP-051** Copy, Share, Open, Open With and drag shall hand another app one full-size file per photo, as Finder does (ADR 0037), on this Mac (ADR 0012). [verified]
  - For an unedited still that is not a RAW, or an untrimmed video, the file handed over shall be the original — a cloud-only original downloaded first at interactive priority, an original a source stages and has not yet copied fetched first, an original missing from its folder never handed over.
  - For an edited still or any RAW, the file handed over shall be a full-size JPEG rendered with the recipe (keeping the original's Exif, GPS, TIFF and IPTC metadata, orientation reset).
  - For a trimmed video, the file handed over shall be the trimmed range, by passthrough.
  - One answer shall say whether a photo's file is ready (`HandOffPreparer.readyURL(for:as:)`).
  - Prepared files shall live in Application Support's `HandOff` folder, in a folder of the launch's own, at a path fixed by the photo and its recipe; they are reused within a launch, never taken from an earlier one, and the earlier launches' folders are cleared before the first preparation of the next writes.
  - When two asks for one photo's file are made, they shall join one preparation, each able to stop waiting at once, and the preparation shall be cancelled when its last waiter leaves.
  - Copy, Share and Open shall travel one ordered lane, each request's work a task of its own.
  - While photos are prepared, a bottom status bar shall read `Preparing N photos to copy` (or share, open) with M of N and Cancel (DES-094), counting each photo whose file is not ready.
  - When Cancel is chosen, it shall cancel the work, stopping the download or render in flight, hand nothing over, and the lane shall go on to the next request.
  - A hand-off shall be all or nothing: the first photo that cannot be prepared ends it with `Couldn’t Prepare Photos`, whose message names that photo's cause, classified by type and never by text, then `Nothing was handed over.`
  - The causes named shall be: the disk full (`FreeSpaceFloor.refusedForSpace`: Foundation's out-of-space code, POSIX `ENOSPC` in the error chain, or AVFoundation's `diskFull`); a source that could not send an original by its sidebar name (`“Family” isn’t answering, so one photo couldn’t be downloaded.`, `“EOS R5” isn’t connected, so one photo couldn’t be copied from it.`); a folder's provider that did not deliver a cloud-only original (`One photo couldn’t be downloaded from “Pictures”.`); an original missing from its folder (`One photo’s original isn’t where Otos last saw it.`); macOS's missing support for a RAW's camera (`macOS couldn’t download what it needs to develop one RAW photo.`); a render that failed (`One photo couldn’t be rendered from its original.`); or the targets' details unread (`Otos couldn’t read these photos from its catalog.`).
  - Open and Open With shall list apps for an original not on disk yet by its file type.
  - Reveal in Finder shall travel the same lane, as `Preparing N photos to reveal`, and reveal each photo's original — never a rendered file — fetching a staged original not yet copied first.
  - If a source cannot send an original for Reveal in Finder, or an original is missing from its folder, then Reveal in Finder shall fail with the same alert ending `Nothing was revealed.`
  - Reveal in Finder shall reveal a cloud placeholder as it is, never materialized.
  - A drag shall write a ready file's `public.file-url` item — a cloud placeholder as it is, since a drag cannot wait — or a file promise (Finder, Mail, Messages and Safari wait; Chrome receives only ready files) that downloads and prepares the photo's file when the receiver asks and copies it on the promise's own queue.
  - A staged original not yet copied shall be promised.
  - A tile whose details the grid does not hold shall be promised as pending, its details read at once off the main actor, and the promise shall name its file only once they are read, declaring an image or a movie until then.
  - If a promise cannot be kept, then it shall fail with the reason as its error.
  Example: Given three photos selected with the middle one edited, ⌘C then paste yields three file URLs in display order: the two originals and the edited one's rendered `edit-1.jpg` in the hand-off folder; given the same edited photo prepared twice, the same file is returned and a file promise copies it to the drop destination; given two asks for one edited photo at once, both get the same file and nothing is left beside it; given one of them cancelled, it stops waiting at once and the other still receives the file from the one preparation; given a trim of 0.2–0.6 s, the handed-over movie lasts about 0.4 s; given an unedited JPEG, a RAW, an edited JPEG and a trimmed and an untrimmed video, the forms are original, rendered, rendered, trimmed and original; given a cloud placeholder, Copy downloads it before handing it over and a drag or a reveal takes it as it is, and a download the provider does not deliver fails with its own message; given Cancel while a download is stuck, nothing is handed over and the next Copy is written; given a missing original, every action fails with its message; given each cause, the alert says it in its own terms; given an account's server that does not answer, or a camera that is not connected, the alert names it and the promise fails with that reason; given the original once staged, it is handed over as itself and fetched no more; given a pending promise for an edited HEIC, it declares an image and names the file `IMG_1.jpg` once read, and a failed read fails it; given a JPEG not on disk yet, Open and Open With still list apps; given an edited photo, Reveal in Finder reveals its original and renders nothing; given an edited camera original not yet staged, a reveal while the camera is disconnected fails with that reason and, once it answers, reveals the staged original.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/HandOffPreparer.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MediaFileWriter.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/EditRecipe.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelHandOff.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelSelection.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/ProgressStatusBar.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/MediaGridClipboard.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/MediaGridSelectionController.swift`, `OtosKit/ContentView.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/WorkspaceOpening.swift`

- **APP-052** When File ▸ Export… (⇧⌘E) is chosen, Otos shall open a sheet for the current targets titled `Export N Photos`, with Format (JPEG, HEIC, Original), Size (Full Size or Long Edge in pixels, 64–32768), Quality, Remove location and Folder (Choose…), remembered across launches (DES-053, DES-095). [verified]
  - Original shall disable Size, Quality and Remove location, and every row shall stay shown so the sheet keeps one height (DES-053, DES-095).
  - Export shall write one new file per target into the folder, in display order, through one ordered lane (ADR 0038).
  - A photo as JPEG or HEIC shall be rendered with its edit, scaled down to the long edge and never up, keeping the original's Exif, TIFF, IPTC and (unless removed) GPS metadata.
  - A photo as Original shall be the original's bytes.
  - A video shall be the original's bytes, or its range by passthrough when trimmed or when location is removed.
  - Each file shall be written in the system's scratch folder on the destination's volume and moved in by `ExclusivePublication`; a taken name gets `_001`, `_002`…, and no file is ever overwritten.
  - The 5 GB free-space floor shall be checked before each download and write.
  - While the export runs, the bottom bar shall read `Exporting N photos` with M of N and Cancel (DES-094).
  - If a photo fails, then the export shall not stop the others.
  - If the floor is reached, a write is refused for space (reported as a full disk), or the folder vanishes, then the export shall stop the job.
  - If an export did not write everything, then it shall show an alert (`Exported M of N Photos` or `Couldn’t Export`, with why) offering Show in Finder for what it wrote.
  - A complete or cancelled export shall show nothing more.
  - While the sheet is up, Edit ▸ Cut, Copy, Paste and Select All shall act on its text.
  Example: Given three photos (one edited) exported as JPEG into a folder already holding `edit-0.jpg`, the folder ends with `edit-0.jpg` untouched, `edit-0_001.jpg`, `edit-1.jpg` and `edit-2.jpg` and nothing else, and no report is shown; given a 96×64 photo and a long edge of 48 or 4000, the files are 48×32 and 96×64; given a photo with GPS exported as HEIC, the file is HEIC and keeps GPS, and with Remove location it has Exif but no GPS; given Original, the file's bytes equal the original's; given a located video trimmed to 0.2–0.6 s, the export lasts about 0.4 s and keeps its location unless removed; given a folder that no longer exists, the report reads `Couldn’t Export` naming it; given the floor after one of three, the report reads `Exported 1 of 3 Photos`; given settings saved with an out-of-range long edge and quality, they are clamped and reload equal.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/ExportSettings.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MediaExporter.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MediaFileWriter.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelExport.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/ExportSheetView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/ProgressStatusBar.swift`, `OtosKit/ContentView.swift`, `OtosKit/OtosApp.swift`

- **APP-095** Every thumbnail outside the grid — a Places stack, an On This Day cover, a duplicate, and the loupe, compare and story panes' fallback — shall load through one loader (`ThumbnailLoad`) that answers a picture, unavailable, or not yet. [verified]

- **APP-096** The sidebar's foot shall show the status of the window's background work in one Activity line and its popover (ADR 0058, DES-124, DES-125), answering what a user asks of it — is everything here, is something wrong that I can fix, is Otos busy — and nothing else. [verified]
  - One board (`ActivityBoard`) shall read every watched owner's status and what stops its work (`QuiescentOwner.troubles`, `OwnerTrouble`) whenever the owner's activity or the machine's conditions change.
  - Each kind shall be an outcome the user would notice missing, named as a verb and the thing Otos makes (DES-035): `Making thumbnails` (with the background downloads it waits on as its line; never an Immich account's thumbnails, which its sync brings), `Reading photo info` (the details the Info pane shows, and a metadata refresh), `Scanning for faces` (stills' previews and videos' frames), `Looking up place names` (counted in the map's areas, `locations`), `Indexing for search`, `Writing descriptions` and `Finding duplicates` (render signatures and the digests that tell exact copies apart).
  - Each owed row of enrichment's walk shall be filed under one of its four outcomes (`EnrichmentPurpose`), alike in Swift and in SQL, and counted and stopped there alone.
  - A kind counted by several owners shall sum their counts.
  - A kind shall work while an owner of it moves its work — busy, with items left that are not parked (`WorkProgress.counted`'s `parked`: items that wait out a failure or are being tried again) — and shall be paused while every such owner waits on a hold.
  - A kind that cannot move shall wait while something stops it or items it has left are parked, and keep waiting, with what stopped it still said, through its owners' retries until it has finished an item more than when it began to wait, or nothing works or waits; else it is up to date.
  - A backoff wake alone shall be no trouble; an owner that is a kind's detail only (its downloads) shall never make it work; no time shall be shown.
  - The popover shall list what works, is paused or waits, each row named by what it does, with how many items it has left in its unit from the one owner that counts the kind's work (`QuiescentOwner.progress(for:)`: the items it finished, counted at each write the catalog accepted — for enrichment the rows a fully applied batch wrote, by the outcome each was claimed for — and a COUNT over its walk's own predicate, read again only when its work moved; done is what it finished since the stretch began and total that plus what is left, so a fall no work made lowers the total and is never done; a failed count publishes nothing).
  - Each popover row shall show a determinate bar only while it works, and one indicator: the bar, a spinner only with nothing counted, a warning while it needs the user, none while it waits.
  - A problem shall be said only when the user would notice it (`OwnerTrouble.Standing`): what the user can fix (a full disk, Apple Intelligence off, a signed-out account with `Open Settings…`) first, on the line too, whatever works; then a source or service that is not available, a cloud provider named as the sidebar names its library.
  - One item failing and being tried again shall never be said — a row whose items all wait says only `Otos will try these again later.` — and an item only the user's work can read (ADR 0046 §4) shall be no work left at all.
  - Then the popover shall say `Everything else is up to date`.
  - An Immich account's row, `Syncing “<name>”`, shall say how far its running pass is in counts the pass reports as it goes: the assets its stream has listed so far, after each applied batch, with no bar (the stream names no total ahead), then the rows tried of those owed the server's images when the fetch began, an exact COUNT over the walk's own index, with a bar.
  - If the count fails, then the account's row shall show what was tried with no bar.
  - When a pass has ended, the account's row shall show only what stops the account, and the assets its server has not read yet, which Otos does not show until it has: `The server hasn’t processed 943 photos and videos yet. They appear once it has.`, counted after each pass that applied the stream to its end, which makes an otherwise current account wait.
  - The line shall name what needs the user, else the work that has run longest, else what waits with its cause when it waits alone, else `Up to date` once the owners are watched.
  - The duplicates bar shall say `Still looking for duplicates` while the groups are built and nothing would move.
  - A row whose items are done while only its grouping owners (`ActivityBoard.Watched.Role.grouping`: People's assignment, the duplicate groups) still work shall say `Grouping faces into people` or `Grouping duplicates` with a spinner and no bar, and its count only while items are left.
  Example: Given a faces owner that begins a pass, the board publishes `scanningFaces` working since that moment; given it settles, up to date; given two owners of one kind, paused only when both are held; given the room turning Low Power on under a description's wait, paused by `lowPower`; given enrichment at rest reporting its provider not answering and places only backing off, making thumbnails waits on it and place names are up to date; given every item left parked while the owner retries, the kind waits with what stopped it still said, and works again once an item finishes; given only the downloads owner busy, the kind does not work; given 11 failed downloads and 114 unanswered items with all 125 parked, `Making thumbnails` reads `125 photos and videos left` and `Otos will try these again later.`, up-to-date kinds are not listed and the footer says the rest is up to date; given a counting owner at 100 left, then 40 finished and 60 left, then 30 left with nothing finished, then 45, its row's progress is 0 of 100, 40 of 100, 40 of 70, 40 of 85, and none once up to date; given each kind's progress, its row reads what is left in its unit with an exact bar while it works and no bar while paused or waiting, and the photos to show say the downloads they wait on with their bytes; given three new stills, enrichment's count is 3 rows owed thumbnails, and once their thumbnails are written none owes a thumbnail and 3 owe their digest; and the signature and search counts equal their walks, falling by one with a written signature, and the signature owner that signed all three reports 3 finished and none left; given enrichment's schedule, its paced rows count as held back, by outcome, until their read lands, a row only the user's work can read counts apart, and what stops one outcome's rows stops no other's; given two owners counting one kind, the kind sums what each finished and has left; given descriptions alone waiting on Apple Intelligence not responding, the line reads `Writing descriptions` over `Apple Intelligence isn’t responding`; given Apple Intelligence off or a signed-out account beside work, the line warns of what needs the user; given every kind working, the rows read `Making thumbnails`, `Reading photo info`, `Scanning for faces`, `Looking up place names`, `Indexing for search`, `Writing descriptions`, `Finding duplicates`; given photo info held back by a server, its row waits saying only that they are tried again; given every cause, only those the user would notice read as distinct sentences, and a provider is named; given a model off or a service failure, the description writer reports `appleIntelligenceOff` or `appleIntelligenceNotResponding`. Given an account's pass, its row reads `18,400 photos and videos so far` while it lists and `2,100 of 6,000 thumbnails` with a bar while it fetches images, and a pass over two batches reports its listing after each and its images from 0 to the rows owed.; given faces' grouping owner busy and its scanning owner settled, the row groups, and works on its items again while the scan moves; given a grouping faces row with nothing left, it reads `Grouping faces into people` with no bar, and with 3 parked stills it also reads `3 photos and videos to scan`.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/ActivityBoard.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/OwnerQuiescence.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/BackgroundOwnerSet.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentSchedule.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/PhotoDescriptionWriter.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/PhotoDescriber.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/PreviewFaceReader.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/PlaceNameReader.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/PhotoSearchIndex.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/PeopleAssignment.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MaterializationCoordinator.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/ActivityPresentation.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/ActivityView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/DuplicatesPresentation.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/RenderSignatureRefresher.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/ImmichCatalogSync.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/ImmichAccounts.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSQLTypes.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinator.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinatorCompletion.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/EnrichmentClaimGateway.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelActivity.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/AssetMetadata.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/CatalogProjection.swift`

- **APP-097** The installed app shall update itself (ADR 0059): only the product identity `net.melisma.Otos`, carrying Otos's EdDSA update key, outside a test host, starts Sparkle. [verified]
  - Sparkle shall check the feed every hour, download a newer build in the background without asking, and install it only once no import or reorganization is in flight, as the Store reports from its journals' own writes (`CatalogStore.operationalWorkChanges`).
  - When Otos quits before then, it shall install the update as Otos quits.
  - App ▸ Check for Updates… shall be offered only by a build that updates itself, and always answer when chosen.
  - When chosen, Check for Updates… shall run Sparkle's check, which shows what it finds.
  - While Otos holds a downloaded update, Check for Updates… shall show an alert naming its version and what it waits for, `Otos 1.0.9 Is Ready to Install`, since Sparkle answers a check with nothing while its session is open.
  - While Sparkle's own check or download runs (its `canCheckForUpdates`), Check for Updates… shall be disabled.
  Example: Given a downloaded update while an import's journal row is in flight, the install is held, and it runs once, the newest one, when the journal is gone; given a dev build or a build without the key, Sparkle never starts. Given an update held, choosing Check for Updates… names that version; given none held, it is Sparkle's check while Sparkle can check and disabled while it cannot; given a held install that runs, nothing is held any more.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/AppUpdater.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/UpdateInstallGate.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStoreOperationalWork.swift`, `OtosKit/OtosApp.swift`, `Config/Otos-Info.plist`

- **APP-098** When Help ▸ Export Diagnostics… is chosen, Otos shall ask for a folder and write one new folder into it, `Otos Diagnostics <yyyy-MM-dd HH.mm.ss>` (ADR 0060). [verified]
  - The folder shall hold about.json (build, Mac, when, and the log lines' scope, start and count or why there are none).
  - The folder shall hold `log.ndjson` (Otos's log lines of the last 24 hours at info and above, one JSON object a line).
  - The folder shall hold `crash-reports/` (the system's reports named `Otos-…` or `Otos_…`, no other app's).
  - The folder shall hold `metrickit/` (the MetricKit diagnostic reports the app kept as they arrived).
  - The folder shall hold storage.json (each entry of Otos's Application Support folder with its bytes and file count).
  - The export shall be written in the scratch folder on the destination's volume and moved in without replacing anything; a taken name gets ` 2`, ` 3`….
  - A written export shall be shown in Finder, selected.
  - If an export could not be written, then Otos shall show `Couldn’t Export Diagnostics` with why (DES-037).
  - If a log cannot be read, then it shall be named in about.json and the rest shall be written.
  Example: Given a support folder holding a 4 KB catalog and one kept payload, and a reports folder holding two Otos reports, an `OtosHelper` report and a Safari report, the export holds the five entries, the two Otos reports only, the payload, two log lines and the catalog's size; given a second export in the same second, it is `<name> 2` and the first is unchanged; given a log that cannot be read, about.json says `unavailable: …` and there is no `log.ndjson`; given a folder that no longer exists, the outcome reads `The folder “Gone” no longer exists.` and nothing is written; given two keeps of payloads, each is a new file.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/DiagnosticsExport.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/DiagnosticsExportController.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MetricKitRecorder.swift`, `OtosKit/OtosApp.swift`

- **APP-057** The Places scope (ADR 0040, `docs/design/places.md`) shall be every asset with a stored primary location across the available fixed libraries — All Media's membership plus one dimension — reached from a `Places` sidebar row that exists whenever one fixed library exists, and presented as a map rather than a view mode. [verified]
  - The map shall show thumbnail stacks on a standard-style map with points of interest excluded, pan and zoom only, never panning or zooming past 75° north or south, or past the farthest located item plus 2° when one lies beyond, nor past the map's own edge (a camera that settles past the band eases back to its edge at the same zoom, DES-104) while wrapping east–west in both directions, the zoom stepper and scale as its chrome, and no user-location control or permission prompt.
  - The filter shall narrow the map; the sort shall decide which photo fronts a stack (the first member in display order).
  - A bar above the map, fixed at 52 pt, shall state the L1 fact `N items on the map` (DES-105), tabular, and no count of the items it leaves off.
  - On entry, the camera shall center on the located items' average location (the circular mean of longitude) and reach every item, so MapKit's zoom-out clamp keeps where most items are in view.
  - When the filter changes, the map shall re-fit; when the user returns from an area, the map shall restore the viewport the user left; ⌘= / ⌘− and the toolbar zoom shall step the camera.
  - Stacks shall be the ViewModel's cluster lane's output for the reported viewport: one 64 pt cell per stack, stacks within a cell of each other merged so none overlap, only cells touching the padded viewport, members in display order.
  - Camera requests shall be map-point bounds, so a fit across more than 180° of longitude holds both ends; stacks and the fit check shall take each item's copy in a viewport across the antimeridian; and a fit whose own settled viewport leaves an item outside (MapKit's zoom-out limit) shall be asked once more without margin at the camera the map showed, then left.
  - An area (`SidebarSelection.mapArea`) shall hold the located items inside its box, and its reads — order, types, places, count — shall seek the located rows of the map cells the box covers through `idx_asset_metadata_place_cell`, testing each by its coordinate, never walking every row.
  - Places' reads shall take each located row's coordinate from `idx_asset_metadata_location`, never from the `asset_metadata` row that holds its metadata record.
  - While Places is empty with reachable libraries, it shall say `No Located Media`.
  - When the last fixed library is removed, Otos shall resolve Places to the default like a removed library.
  Example: Given a library with located and unlocated items, `.places` orders only the located ones, each with its coordinate, under the descriptor's sort, and counts and filter options agree; given the Places scope publishes, `CatalogWindowState.map` pairs the scoped IDs with their coordinates; given the user is in the loupe and selects Places, the mode returns to grid, and until the map reports a viewport the picker is disabled and `setViewMode` changes nothing; given the map reports a viewport, items at one coordinate form one exact stack fronted by the first in order, nearby items merge at country zoom and split at street zoom, and a stack outside the viewport is not emitted; given entry, the camera request centers on the located items' average location and holds them all; given ⌘= on the map, the next camera request halves the span; given no fixed library, Places resolves to the default; given no located item, the empty state is `.noLocatedMedia`.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/CatalogModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/MapModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSQLTypes.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/RootRegistry.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogQueryGateway.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSchema.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/CatalogWindowPolicy.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/SidebarSelectionPolicy.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/CatalogProjectionWindowStateBuild.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MapClusterPolicy.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelMap.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/PlacesMapView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/SidebarView.swift`, `OtosKit/ContentView.swift`
  Not covered by tests: the drawn stacks themselves, which the window's self-capture (script/smoke-run.sh --verify places, whose op=map.cluster line proves the camera report and the cluster pass on the real library) does not render

- **APP-058** When a stack on the Places map (DES-103) is clicked, the map shall zoom the camera into its extent (never wider than a quarter of the viewport) when its members sit at several coordinates, and open the stack when they share one; when it is double-clicked, the map shall open it: `SidebarSelection.mapStack`, exactly the items it counted, by identity, as the grid. [verified]
  - A double-click shall be one gesture by AppKit's click count (DES-103): its second click is taken before MapKit or any stack hit-tests it and opens the stack the first click acted on, however far that click's flight moved it, never a stack the flight slid under the pointer nor MapKit's own double-click zoom.
  - On a stack the first click opened, the double-click shall open nothing again; a click counted as one shall start a new gesture.
  - The map shall have no selection.
  - On the map, the view-mode picker shall select no segment, and choosing Grid, Loupe or Compare — the picker, ⌘1 / ⌘2 / ⌘3 or the View menu — shall open the area the map shows in that mode: `SidebarSelection.mapArea`, the items whose stored location lies in the viewport's latitude/longitude box (a box across the antimeridian holds both sides).
  - Loupe and Compare shall start on the area's first items (Photos' Grid, `docs/design/places.md`).
  - Until the area is published, the pane shall be the area's, waiting (APP-084), so neither the map nor Places' items are drawn on the way into it, and Back shall show Places waiting until Places is published.
  - Neither an area nor a stack shall be a sidebar row: the Places row stays highlighted, the title stays `Places`, and a leading `Back to Map` toolbar item returns to the map at the viewport the user left — taken when they leave, and for a stack a double-click opened, the viewport its first click zoomed from, however far that flight got.
  - The area shall be saved and restored with the browser place (APP-043; an opened stack reopens the map).
  - A stack's thumbnail shall be the representative's, decoded off the main actor through the grid's loader into the shared tile store, a placeholder only before its first decode, and kept for as long as the stack's representative is the same item.
  - VoiceOver shall name a stack by its count then its coordinates.
  Example: Given an exact stack is clicked, the descriptor's scope is that stack's members, the sidebar row is Places and Back is offered, and the pane is still the map until the stack's state is applied, then the grid; given a viewport, Loupe opens the viewport's area, a late Places state does not undo the mode, and once the area publishes the focus is its first item; given Grid with nothing selected, the area opens as the grid with no selection; given a city viewport, its area holds the city and not the next one; given a viewport across the antimeridian, its area holds both sides; given a world-wide viewport, every longitude; given the map, the drawn segmented control selects no segment and choosing one delivers that mode; given an area box, the store returns exactly the items inside it, across the antimeridian too; given Back, the scope is `.places` and the camera request is the viewport the user left, not a re-fit; given a splittable stack is clicked, the scope is unchanged and the camera request narrows, and a double-click then opens exactly its members, and Back after the zoom settled returns to the viewport before the click; given an off-centre stack clicked and its flight under way, the second click is taken and opens that stack, a third does nothing more, and a second click after a fresh single click is not taken; given an exact stack double-clicked, it opens once; given a stack's members, the store returns exactly them in the scope's order; given a saved area scope, the browser place round-trips it; given a stack of two, its label is `2 items, 60.17 north, 24.94 east`.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelMap.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelViewMode.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelSelection.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/PlacesMapView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/CatalogToolbarControls.swift`, `OtosKit/ContentView.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/BrowserPlace.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/MapModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogQueryGateway.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MapClusterPolicy.swift`
  Not covered by tests: the thumbnail's persistence across camera changes rests on SwiftUI view identity, and the map's local event monitor registration and a stack's tap reading the current event are AppKit event plumbing in the view (the decision the monitor hands on, MapMouseDown, is tested); both are exercised only by running the app

- **APP-059** Otos shall show a Live Photo as one item (ADR 0041): a still and a movie that share a library root, a folder and Apple's content identifier, and are the only still and the only movie there that do, show as the still alone in the grid, the filmstrip, counts, day sections, filters and import batches. [verified]
  - Any other count shall pair nothing, and every file shall show.
  - Move to Trash on the still shall take the movie as a member (`.livePhotoMotion`), and undo shall put both back.
  - In the loupe, a Live Photo shall offer its motion: a `Live` button at the pane's top trailing edge and View ▸ Play Live Photo play the movie in place, once, and the still returns when it ends (DES-106).
  - Trim Video shall not be offered for a Live Photo.
  - Copy, Share, Open, drag and Export shall hand off the still only (ADR 0037, ADR 0038).
  Example: Given `IMG_1.HEIC` and `IMG_1.MOV` with one identifier, the grid shows `IMG_1.HEIC` and a Videos filter shows nothing; given a second movie with that identifier, or the movie in another folder, or an unrelated `IMG_2.JPG` beside a pair, nothing extra pairs; given Move to Trash on the still, its members are the still and the movie; given the loupe on the still, Play Live Photo is enabled and Trim Video is not.
