- **APP-060** While any group of identical copies is active (and while it is selected), a Duplicates sidebar row shall exist, alone under a Utilities header, the last section of the sidebar, counting photos with duplicates (DES-107, ADR 0042). [verified]
  - Its list shall show groups of visible fixed-library copies whose current digests match — in one library or account unless the user compares across them (APP-083) — read through the digest index alone, never grouping every row of the scope (DES-107, ADR 0042).
  - Every group shall have a default: each library keeps its best copy (companions; for same photo the camera original, most pixels, largest file; an edit; a choice; no Finder/browser/import copy suffix; earliest birth; path order), and only a library's copies with work that cannot be combined — two edits, two with companions, two different choices — wait.
  - Moving a copy shall carry its choice and edit onto the kept copy that lacks them, in the write that hides it, and undo shall take them back.
  - Each folder set that holds exact copies — two folders that mirror each other, or one folder with files saved twice — shall be one header whose rule decides every exact-copy group under it (keep the copies in one folder, or the original name, the oldest, the largest) and no file-of-one-photo group.
  - A click on a photo or a name shall keep that copy and outrank the rule; a group whose work cannot be combined shall be decided only by a click.
  - Keep All Copies shall be remembered per pair of paths (`duplicate_kept_pair`, schema v11), carried by Otos moves, purged with either copy, and undoable; a later copy of kept copies is the only one offered.
  - The groups shall have their own owner (`DuplicatesProjection`), asked for a build only after a catalog change: the window publishes without waiting for a build, and a change of scope, filter or sort never starts one.
  - The list shall always show every active group: one found while the user looks joins the list and the count at once.
  - A file of zero bytes shall never be a copy.
  - What each group keeps shall be one computation (`DuplicateResolution`): the click, else the folder rule for exact copies, else the default.
  - The bar's `Move N Copies to Trash` shall move exactly the copies tagged Trash through the ordinary Move to Trash path, asking nothing; a group always keeps one copy.
  - An Immich account's copy shall never be tagged Trash (ADR 0046 §8): whatever a click, rule or default chooses, it stays and only library copies move, the header ends `Otos never removes copies in “Family”` (alone when nothing moves), its Move to Trash Now is disabled, an account counts as one folder named by the account, and a folder header never offers to keep a library's side over an account's, its line saying why.
  - Return and double-click shall open a group in Compare or Loupe (`.duplicateGroup`), and Back to Duplicates shall return.
  - Photo ▸ Keep This Copy / Keep All Copies / Review Again / Move Duplicates to Trash shall act on the selected photos, and a photo's context menu shall carry the ordinary Move to Trash for that copy.
  - The list shall be three sections by the attention a group needs (`DuplicatesLayout`, published with the groups): groups whose work cannot be combined, then files of one photo, each newest first by capture date, then exact copies under their folder set's header — the set with the most groups first, three or more folders last, largest first inside each, a header closed until clicked unless it is the only one and opened by a selection made elsewhere.
  - A group's section shall follow its copies, so a click, a rule or Keep All Copies never moves it.
  - A folder's header shall count its groups, the bytes they would move and their reasons.
  - Each group's default shall record why it was chosen — the keeper criterion that separated the kept copy from the copy it beat last (birth times at whole seconds), or that each library keeps its own — or which work conflicts and in which library.
  - A waiting group's header shall name the conflict in words (DES-107), and the kind noun (`videos`, `photos`, `items`) shall follow what the groups hold.
  - A kept group shall stay in place as one quiet header whose `Review Again` forgets its pairs, undoably by its own name.
  - In a pair, unkeeping the kept copy shall keep the other.
  - ⌘⌫ on the list shall move only the selected groups' extra copies, never a kept one.
  - After the act, the bar shall show its receipt with Undo until a click or rule changes or the user leaves the list.
  - The bar's L1 shall be what will happen (`221 copies go to the Trash · 53.9 GB`) over L3 the count of items with duplicates, and shall be that count only while nothing would move (DES-107).
  - The bar's button shall carry the count (`Move 221 Copies to Trash`).
  - Files of one photo shall rank by pixels and bytes whether or not they name a capture, each group laid out as Photos lays it out, its date and why it keeps what it keeps over its copies' photos, each tagged Keep or Trash with its size and, under it, what else differs.
  - An exact-copy group shall be one picture beside its date, the size its copies share and why, over one line per copy marked by a checkmark or a trash can and named by what tells it apart.
  - A group's photos shall take its own aspect from the copies' upright pixel size.
  - A closed folder header shall preview its first five groups (its file name, or its folder when the names are the same).
  - The arrow keys shall walk the copies on screen, and Space shall keep the selected one.
  - Return, Space and double-click shall open a group.
  - The window shall show no `Filtered` subtitle under the list.
  Example: Given a copy-suffixed and a plain copy in one library, the plain one is kept; given an authored copy, it is kept whatever its name; given a pick on one copy and an edit on another, the edited copy is kept and the pick carried; given two edits, two sets of companions or two different choices, the group waits; given two libraries, each keeps its copy until their folder rule keeps one side; given a carried choice and edit, undo takes them back; given kept pairs, the group is set aside and a newcomer is the only copy offered; given a group found mid-visit, it joins the count at once; given a folder rule, every group in that folder set keeps what it says, and a clicked group keeps the click; given two empty files with one digest, neither is a duplicate; given the groups' build held at its suspension, the loaded window is already published with groups unknown; given a scope change while the build is held, the chosen scope is published and the groups are built once; given groups not yet built, the selected sidebar row shows without a count and nothing can be moved; given a copy pending trash, its twin is no longer a duplicate; given a library copy chosen over an account's copy, nothing moves and the header says why, while choosing the account's moves the library's; given two copies in one account, nothing moves and their header offers no rule; given a library folder mirroring an account, its header offers only the account and Both; given a conflicting group, a resized file, exact copies in one folder, in two and in three, the list reads in that order and a kept conflicting group stays first; given a folder rule and a file-of-one-photo group in that folder, the group keeps its default; given exact copies in one folder, their lines read their file names, and in two folders their folders.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/DuplicateModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStoreDuplicates.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelDuplicates.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/DuplicatesView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/DuplicatesPresentation.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/DuplicatesProjection.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/CatalogProjection.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelTrash.swift`

- **APP-061** Duplicates shall also list versions of the same photo — different files of one capture, keeping the camera original with the most pixels by default — when their stored-thumbnail render signatures nearly match (difference hash within 10 bits, 32 × 32 grayscale mean difference below 4) and no capture fact sets them apart (ADR 0042). [verified]
  - Two files that both name a camera and an instant shall group only when the model and the whole-second instant agree, the body serial agrees where both carry one, and both records carry the same sub-seconds — a record without sub-seconds proves nothing, so the pair does not group.
  - A file without capture metadata shall join only the one original clearly closer than any other.
  - A RAW shall never group with a processed file.
  - A thumbnail an Immich account drew shall never pair with one Otos rendered.
  - Copies in an identical group shall be left to it.
  - An Immich external library's asset shall never be compared, since it names a file a library holds.
  - Versions in two libraries or accounts shall pair only while the user compares across them (APP-083) (ADR 0042).
  - The compared stills and their record's pairing columns shall be read from two indexes that hold only those (v67), never from the wide rows.
  - A build shall pair again only when the stills, the identical copies or the reach moved, read a still's evidence (render and sub-seconds) only when its stamp moved, and read the copies a group lists for its members every build, so what it publishes is what a build from nothing publishes.
  - A build shall be asked for by rows shown, Live Photo grouping, copies, choices, and a write of the date, kind, record, image or edit of a row Duplicates compares or lists (`DuplicatesHoldSQL`); the same write to a row it cannot see asks for none.
  - Each file shall show as its own photo, tagged Keep or Trash, and Return shall open two side by side in Compare with the group as the filmstrip (DES-107).
  Example: Given two burst frames with identical pixels but different sub-seconds, no group; given same-second frames with identical pixels whose records carry no sub-seconds, or only one of a pair carrying them, no group; given a stripped, resized copy and its source, one group; given a stripped copy equally close to two frames, none; given a RAW and its JPEG, none; given an account's Immich-drawn thumbnail and a library's Otos rendering of one capture, none, while two Immich-drawn ones pair; given an original and an upscaled stripped copy, the original is kept; given a version in each of two libraries, no pair until Duplicates compares across libraries; given an uploaded and an external-library asset, signed, only the uploaded one is compared, and a record arriving for the external one asks Duplicates for no build while one for the uploaded one does; given 400 random stills across two roots, the proposed pairs are each pair of one bucket that passes the column checks, each once, compared within each library and across them; given a build with nothing changed, nothing is paired and no evidence read; given a choice on a member, its copy changes and the pairing is held; given a member's thumbnail redrawn, then signed again, or its camera changed, each build equals one from nothing; given an owner-shaped catalog, the candidate read is two covering-index searches.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/DuplicateModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/RenderSignature.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStoreDuplicates.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/DuplicatesProjection.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/DuplicatesView.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelDuplicates.swift`

- **APP-062** An Immich account shall be a library root of kind `immichAccount` (ADR 0046 §2, §3), named by `immich:<normalized server>#<user id>`. [verified]
  - The account's path shall be an Otos staging folder under Caches (`RemoteStaging` in the build's own container, `StagingContainers`: the one value the Store derives from its catalog location and the camera monitor, sweeps, quit check and import release all read) derived from its identity and re-derived at every load, so a root stored at another build's or the old shared container moves into this build's own with every overlay its assets hold (a camera's root likewise).
  - The account's server address shall be kept in `library_root.remote_base_url` (migration `v15_library_root_remote_base_url`) and shown as its Settings location.
  - The account shall be listed in the sidebar's Libraries section and spanned by All Media, Places and Duplicates.
  - The account shall never be removable, an import destination or source, reorganized, scanned, watched, Erased or moved to the Trash, and enrichment shall never claim its rows to download an original (it reads their metadata in ranges, APP-066).
  - The account's rows shall stay browsable with no server: an account is available at launch from its stored listing.
  - The same account added twice shall be one root.
  - When the account is removed, Otos shall delete its rows and its staging folder and nothing on the server.
  - If a catalog names a root kind this build does not know, then Otos shall refuse it rather than read it as a library.
  Example: Given an account listed with two assets, it is a Libraries-section root whose assets are in All Media and, located, in Places; given an account copy and a library file with one digest, Duplicates groups them; given a relaunch with no listing, the account is available and its rows are in All Media; given the same server typed differently, adding again returns the same root; given removal, its rows and staging folder are gone; given an account stored under another container with a Liked choice, an edit, a named face, a search reading and text, a thumbnail and a journaled erase, a launch moves its root into this build's container keeping every one, renames the thumbnail and moves the journal, and leaves the old folder on disk.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/CatalogModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStorePersistence.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSchema.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/RootRuntimeState.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/StagingLocation.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/SettingsView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/SidebarView.swift`

- **APP-063** An Immich account's rows shall follow its server's sync stream (ADR 0046 §5), read by `ImmichCatalogSync` over `ImmichClient` with a login session whose token lives in the Keychain (`ImmichKeychainSessionStore`), never SQLite. [verified]
  - An image or video in the timeline or archive shall be a row at `<asset id>/<original file name>` with its EXIF size; audio and other types shall never be rows.
  - A Live Photo's motion part (`hidden`) shall be a row in its still's folder, `<still id>/<motion original file name>`, so the pairing rule pairs them (ADR 0041).
  - The motion part shall be listed whenever its still is, from the motion's own line in that pass (with its size when that is there too; a later size reaches it by its ID) or else from the server's `GET /api/assets/{id}`, hidden with its still, refreshed from its own line where it already lives in its still's folder when that line comes without its still's, purged when its still no longer links it or is deleted, and on its own permanent deletion.
  - If the server refuses to describe a motion part (400 or 404), then its still shall be left listed alone, the rows already in the still's folder kept, and the batch acknowledged.
  - When a video once listed in a folder of its own becomes a motion part, it shall leave that folder, whether its `hidden` line comes in a later batch or after its timeline line in the same one, so it is one row, in its still's folder.
  - If an asset line's type or visibility is one this version does not know, then the line shall be ignored, logged and acknowledged, never a pass that cannot be read.
  - Every row shall store the source's own ID for it (`source_item_id`, migration v20), which names every request for its bytes and images and every size and deletion the stream reports apart from its line; a still's folder is never parsed for it.
  - An asset in Immich's trash shall stay as a row hidden by its source (`hidden_by_source`, migration v16; its trash `source_deleted` since v84) with its overlay kept, and show again when restored.
  - An asset the server has not read yet — no size and none of the server's images, which the server's own timeline leaves out — shall be hidden by its source too, and show once the server reports its size (v84).
  - A line without a size shall keep the stored size.
  - A permanent deletion, or a move into the locked folder, shall purge the asset's rows and overlay.
  - Lines shall be applied in batches of 500 under a listing lease and acknowledged only after the Store applied them, so a dropped connection replays what it did not apply.
  - A full resync shall be recorded as owed (`library_root.resync_owed`, migration v41, the revision of the newest request) when the server resets the cursor, before the reset is acknowledged, and when a new session is signed in (APP-064).
  - While a full resync is owed, every pass shall ask the server to discard the cursor (`reset: true`) and read the stream from zero, so a resync that dropped after acknowledging batches is never resumed from them, and the pass that reaches the end shall purge every row it did not list and settle the revision it started from in one Store transaction, leaving a request made meanwhile owed.
  - If a reset comes in a stream read from zero, or a second one comes in a pass, then it shall fail the pass (`docs/research/2026-11-13-immich-sync-reset.md`).
  - If a root cannot get or keep its listing lease, then the pass shall end as such and be retried; only a cancelled loop shall be a stopped pass.
  - A request for a sync shall be answered only by a pass that succeeded (its stream synced, its mirror walk and its walk over the rows owed images did not fail; a floor hold answers it), and a fetch of the rows on screen only when it did not fail or when a later pass that succeeded walked them.
  - If a pass or fetch fails, then it shall leave the sync owing it (`ImmichCatalogSync.holdsWork`, ADR 0032 rule 4), so an account whose server does not answer is never quiescent or caught up until a pass the poll or backoff rings succeeds (ADR 0046 §5).
  - A pass shall be a sync only when its stream ends with `SyncCompleteV1`; a stream cut short keeps what it applied and is not reported as a sync.
  - Server failures shall be classified by URL error code and HTTP status (`unreachable`, `unauthorized`, `refused`), never by text.
  - A folder a batch lists in full shall purge only the rows inside it that the batch does not list, never a sibling folder or file whose name begins the same, and each such lookup shall read only that folder's rows, so a batch's cost does not grow with the account (a 500-folder batch on a 69,000-row account went from about 7 s to milliseconds).
  - Each pass shall receive the stream whole before applying it, so applying it never slows its transfer until the request times out.
  Example: Given a pass with images, a video, audio, an unlinked motion part and a locked asset, only the image and video are rows, with their sizes, and the newest ack of each type is sent once after the apply; given a trashed then restored asset, it is hidden then shown with its curation choice; given a deletion line, its row is gone; given a size in a later pass, the row gets it; given a still and the motion part it links, both are rows in the still's folder, each with its own size and ID; given a still whose motion part's lines came in an earlier pass, the motion is listed from the server's facts, takes a later size by its own ID, and is purged when the still stops linking it or is deleted; given a trashed still, its motion part is hidden with it; given a motion part's own line alone with a later modification time and size, its row takes them and stays hidden with its trashed still; given the motion part deleted alone, the still stays; given the v20 migration, existing account rows take their folder as their ID and no other kind gets one; given a reset, the listing read from zero purges what it no longer names; given a resync that drops after its first batch was acknowledged by a server that resumes from acknowledgements, the next pass reads from zero again and purges only the row the server no longer lists; given a resync requested while one runs, completing the first leaves the second owed; given a root with no lease, the pass says so rather than stopped; given a pass whose server failed, the sync still owes its request after the pass ends and owes nothing once the next pass syncs; given a fetch of the rows on screen the server failed, the sync owes it until a pass whose walk fetched those rows; given a video listed alone that becomes a motion part, in a later batch or the same one, it is one row in its still's folder; given a motion part's line without its size, its still lists it with no request and the size arrives later; given a motion part the server refuses, its still is listed, the batch acknowledged, and the motion row already in its folder keeps its overlay; given an asset line of an unknown type or visibility, it is ignored; given a connection dropped after one batch, only that batch is acknowledged and the next pass applies the rest; given the client, login, refusals, transport failures, the line stream and acks behave as Immich 3.3.6 does; given the Keychain, a token round-trips and is removed.; given rows in `a/`, `a/sub/`, `a-b/`, `a2/` and `a.jpg`, a batch listing `a/` in full with one of its rows purges only the other rows of `a/` and `a/sub/`; given a stream received and its session then cancelled, every line of it is still read, and a refused stream is an error Given an asset listed with no size and no server images, it is hidden and counted as unread, and shown once its size is listed; given a motion part listed before its size, its still shows alone until the size arrives.

- **APP-064** Settings ▸ Libraries shall add an Immich account through one sheet (Add Immich Account…: server address, email, password; DES-041, DES-052, DES-053). [verified]
  - When Add is chosen, the sheet shall check the address, that the server speaks Immich 3, and the login, and say which failed (not an address, nothing answered, not Immich, another major version, wrong email or password) in the sheet's one footnote line, which otherwise says Otos never changes anything on the server, so the sheet keeps one height.
  - An added account's session shall be kept in the Keychain and its sync shall run at once, at each launch, after each pass on a poll while its server answers, and on a growing backoff while it does not (`ImmichAccounts`).
  - Each account's status — connecting, online, offline, signed out — shall be what its server last answered: only a request that reached no server makes it offline, while a server that answered with a failure, or a failure of Otos's own, leaves it standing and retries on the backoff, and a report from a pass whose loop was replaced (signing in again) or stopped is dropped.
  - A pass shall be online as soon as the first line of its stream is read, not only when it ends, so a first sync of a large library is online while it runs, and a batch the catalog refuses shall never show its server as unreachable.
  - Settings' Status column shall say when the account last synced rather than whether it is connected: `Syncing…` while a pass whose server answered applies the stream and then fetches the server's images; `Synced 3m ago` (`just now`, `yesterday`, then the date) since the last pass applied its listing to the stream's end (recorded then, not when its images are all fetched, which on a first sync takes hours); the same with a `wifi.slash` glyph and the tooltip `Can’t reach <host>. Last synced … Otos shows the photos it has stored.` while the server does not answer; `Signed Out`; and before any finished pass `Connecting…` or `Can’t reach server` — redrawn each minute.
  - The last finished pass shall be kept in the catalog (`library_root.last_synced_at`, migration v34), so a launch whose server does not answer still says it.
  - The sidebar shall mark an offline or signed-out account with a glyph and its reason (DES-029).
  - A signed-out account shall sign in again through the same sheet with its server filled in (Sign In…).
  - Every new session, the first or one signed in again, shall owe a full resync (APP-063), because its empty cursor gets no reset from the server and proves nothing about rows it does not list: its first pass reads the stream from zero and purges a row the server no longer lists, such as one deleted while signed out.
  - If a pass's root could not get or keep its listing lease, then the pass shall be retried on the backoff; only a loop torn down shall ring no further pass.
  - While Otos holds an account, signed in or not, it shall claim the account's staging tree (`StagingLocation.claimOwnership(of:)`), so another instance's sweep never deletes its staged originals; forgetting the account shall release it.
  - Staging containers shall be named by the running app's bundle identifier, so a dev build and the installed app never share one (ADR 0030).
  - When an account is removed, Otos shall stop its sync, log its session out when the server answers, and delete its Keychain item before its root is removed.
  Example: Given a server that answers, Add saves the session, adds the account and syncs its asset; given a wrong password, an old major version, an address nothing answers, or a non-http address, no account and no session remain; given the server answering, then not reachable, then answering, then refusing the session, the status is online, offline, online, signed out; given any status, a 5xx, an unreadable line, a reset loop, a catalog failure, a pass with no lease or a stopped pass leaves it as it was; given an account signed in again, its pass asks the server to read from zero, purges a row the server no longer lists, and settles the resync; given an account held signed out, a sweep leaves its staged original, and after forgetting it the sweep collects it; given a stream that fails after its second batch, the pass reports its server answered once, at its first line, before any acknowledgement, and a stream that fails before any line reports nothing; given a launch, an account with a saved session syncs and one without is signed out; given forgetting an account, its session is logged out and deleted.; given a pass, its listing is reported applied before any of its images is fetched and a failed stream reports none; given a pass that applied its listing, its time is kept, and a later launch whose server fails is offline with that same last sync and not syncing; given each status, Settings reads `Connecting…`, `Can’t reach server`, `Syncing…`, `Signed Out`, `Synced just now`, `3m ago`, `yesterday`

- **APP-065** An Immich account's rows that have never been edited, have no Otos rendering and have no source images for their current file shall get them from the server — the rows on screen first, as soon as they are shown and after each listing batch lands, and after each sync pass every other row (ADR 0046 §7, ADR 0035). [verified]
  - Immich's own preview shall be downloaded, once per asset, and Otos's preview (stills only, at the server's size, never upscaled) and 256 px grid thumbnail shall be drawn from it and written through the rendering's staging and promotion, stamped as source products (`source_products_size`/`_mtime`, migration v17) and never as the rendering.
  - The rows owed them shall be found from an index of exactly those rows (v65), so a pass over an account whose images are current reads none.
  - A file that changes shall be owed new images, which replace the shown ones when they arrive.
  - If a preview is no image, then it shall be skipped and asked for again next pass while the rest are still fetched.
  - An asset the server lists without a thumbhash (one whose previews it has not made, and every Live Photo motion part, which Immich never makes previews of) shall not be asked for its images.
  - If the server answers an asset's preview with a 404, its answer that it has none, then the asset shall be skipped and recorded (`source_products_absent`, migration v39), so neither it nor an asset without a thumbhash is asked for them again until a later listing of it carries a thumbhash.
  - If the server answers with any other status (a 5xx, a 429, a refused session), then that shall be treated as the server failing now, not an answer about the asset: it ends the fetch after the page's images already fetched are written, and the rest stay owed.
  - If the server does not answer, or the images' volume is at its free-space floor, then the fetch shall end the same way.
  - An edited still shall always show Otos's rendering, so it is never owed the server's images and none is fetched for it, whatever its file does.
  - The write shall refuse the server's images for any row not owed them.
  Example: Given an account with a still and a video, a pass fetches each preview once: the still gets a preview at the server's size and a 256 px thumbnail, the video only a thumbnail, and neither stamps a rendering; a pass with nothing owed fetches nothing; a changed file is fetched again; given an unreadable preview and a server that stops answering, those rows stay owed; given a still edited after its images arrived, a changed file fetches nothing on that pass or the next; given the live server, every listed asset has a thumbnail after one pass.; given a 404 for one asset's preview, the others are fetched, the next pass asks for none of them, and a later listing of that asset asks for it once more; given an asset listed without a thumbhash and a Live Photo, only the still's preview is asked for; a refused session still ends the fetch and leaves its row owed; given a 429, 500 or 503 for one preview, the preview fetched before it is kept and that row stays owed; given three rows owed images and two on screen, the two are fetched at once in the screen's order and the third waits for the walk; given rows shown with no pass running, their images are fetched
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/ImmichCatalogSync.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/ImmichAccounts.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModel.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/ImmichClient.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSQLTypes.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStorePersistence.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSchema.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/AssetMetadata.swift`

- **APP-066** An Immich account's rows shall get their metadata from MediaMetadata reading the original in byte ranges of the server's `/original` (HTTP `Range`, answered 206) through the account's session, a few 128 KB read-through chunks per file and never a download (ADR 0046 §5, ADR 0039). [verified]
  - Enrichment shall claim them for metadata and a video's faces (read in the same ranges, ADR 0047) only, and only while the account's server answers: `ImmichAccounts` records each account's status in the Store (`setSourceAnswering`), an account starts not answering at launch, and a root that stops answering has its claims released.
  - A range outside the file shall never be asked for.
  - If a read is short, failed or unanswered, then it shall not be a definitive absence: the row is paced by the retry ledger (`EnrichmentPurpose.unanswered`) and not claimed again at once.
  - A ranged read's status shall be judged when its headers arrive: anything but a 206 (a server or proxy that ignores `Range` and sends the whole file as a 200) settles it as refused and cancels the transfer before its body is received.
  - When a read's caller stops waiting for it (the 30 s deadline, AVFoundation cancelling a video's request), the read shall be cancelled, so it stops costing bytes.
  Example: Given a byte source, a range past the end is nil with no request and a short, failed or unanswered read throws, and the unanswered one is cancelled; given a ranged read answered 200, it is refused at its headers and its transfer stopped; given a ranged read cancelled while it runs, its transfer stops and it settles as cancellation; given an account, it is enrichable only while its server is recorded as answering; given an account row served in ranges, enrichment stamps its metadata with the file's capture date in at most four reads and no rendering; given an unanswered row, the ledger holds it for its pacing; given the live server, a ranged read of a real original parses.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/RemoteByteSource.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/RemoteVideoAsset.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MediaEnricher.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinator.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinatorWorkSelection.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinatorCompletion.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentSchedule.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/ImmichAccounts.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/ImmichClient.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/RootRuntimeState.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`

- **APP-067** An Immich account's original shall be downloaded only for the user's work — full-resolution zoom, an edit, export, hand-off (interactive or command materialization) — through the account's session into its staging folder, under a hidden name then renamed over the final one. [verified]
  - The original's locality shall be `fetchableForUserWork`, so background and device work never ask for it (`ensureLocal` answers `userWorkOnly` without a request; enrichment releases the row to its pacing ledger), and the executor shall still refuse such demand at its boundary, so no original is downloaded to render the library (owner decision, 2026-11-09).
  - If the server does not answer, then the download shall be reported unavailable; if the session is refused, then Otos shall sign the account out.
  - A downloaded original shall be offered to enrichment ahead of other work for every product it owes (`originalBecameLocal`), so Otos's rendering — RAW9 for a RAW — replaces the server's images and its digest is taken from that one read.
  - A claim for metadata alone shall still read in ranges.
  Example: Given an account online, background demand for an original is refused and leaves no file; given an account's unstaged original, background work's `ensureLocal` dispatches no request; interactive demand stages the server's bytes at the row's path and the next background claim offers that row first, for a rendering; given an asset the server does not have, the download fails; given ranged-read scopes, only metadata and a provisional thumbnail are readable in ranges; given the live server, an original downloads at the size the sync reported.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/ImmichAccounts.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/ImmichClient.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModel.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/AssetMetadata.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinator.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinatorWorkSelection.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinatorCompletion.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MaterializationCoordinator.swift`

- **APP-068** Keyword search (ADR 0048, DES-108) shall narrow the shown scope, under its filter, to the items `PhotoSearchIndex` matches for the toolbar field's text, in any language. [verified]
  - The text's language shall be recognised among English and the languages Apple Translation can translate on the device now, and translated to English as a photo's caption (`SearchQueryEncoder.photoWords`, so a lone word has context).
  - In a search language Translation does not offer, the words Otos's glossary of it holds shall be read in English (`SearchGlossary`, Estonian), also when the recogniser calls the query English but English spelling rejects a word of it; every other word shall be read as typed by the model's multilingual tower.
  - The glossary shall list each form once, for the sense a photo search means by it, and shall not load with a form listed twice; a form of three letters or fewer shall be read only as a whole word.
  - The query shall be read by its own owner (`SearchQueryReader`, one reading at a time, the newest query next, a reading in flight never cancelled), so the window's publication never waits on Translation or Maps.
  - The projection shall publish which state the query is in (`searchPhase`, DES-108).
  - While the query is being read, the window shall keep what it shows and its subtitle shall read `Searching…` (`Filtered · Searching…` under a filter) until the reader reports the reading ended, with no deadline.
  - When a query's full reading waits on Translation or Maps, the window shall show its reading as typed first, still searching, its matches replaced by the full reading's when it ends and never an empty grid between them, while a Latin-script query English spelling accepts is read as English and not translated.
  - When the query is read, the window shall show the matches.
  - If the query could not be read (a failed reading, read again after a backoff, 2 s doubling to a minute), then the grid shall show none of the scope and the empty state `Couldn’t Search for “…”` over `Otos tries again on its own. The results appear here when it succeeds.`, never the scope unsearched.
  - Ending the search shall publish at once.
  - The cut and the label gate shall follow the best match within the scope searched, not the library's.
  - The results shall be ordered by relevance — best match first, ungrouped — until another sort is chosen.
  - A search shall survive scope changes and shall never be persisted.
  - If the result is empty, then Otos shall show `No Results for “…”` with the count of the scope's items search has not read yet — those the index holds no embedding for, but for those that will get no thumbnail (`PhotoSearchIndex.unreadCount`, one read of `idx_media_asset_no_thumbnail_coming`, `OtosTests/CatalogStoreQueryPlanTests.swift#theRowsWithNoThumbnailComingAreReadFromTheirIndex`) — and read no count of the whole scope (`OtosTests/PhotoSearchIndexTests.swift#anEmptyAnswerReadsNoCountOfTheWholeScope`).
  - If a query's answer cannot be computed for want of an index, then Otos shall publish nothing rather than an empty grid.
  - Only time-independent work shall be kept between readings (a text's translation, a text's embedding, Maps' answer for a name); a date is kept as the rule the query names and resolved when it is answered, and a query searched again after the field was emptied is read again.
  - When the search field takes focus (a click, Tab, Edit ▸ Find), Otos shall ready the first query without publishing: the index starts the text encoder's load and asks the kernel to read the mapped vectors ahead (`PhotoSearchIndex.prepareForSearch`), and reads no scope ahead.
  - A query's scope's slots shall be read with its first match and reused while the scope and the index hold.
  - When a query comes while the text tower is not prepared, its load in flight or not yet started, the query shall first be read without the tower (`SearchQueryEncoder.wordsReading`: its facts, and its picture words matched only against the words read in photos, through the catalog's text index, which no pass loads, so the answer is ready once the catalog is open) and those matches shown at once, still searching (`searchPhase` `refining`), a words reading that matches nothing reading nothing more, while the reader starts the load as one the user waits on and never waits for it.
  - The load's end shall ring the reader, which reads the query with the tower, and the encoder shall load once whoever asked.
  - A query with no first-phase match shall keep what the window shows, still searching.
  - A query read with the tower prepared shall race its words reading and the tower's, and the words answer shall be published only when no tower answer has landed when it is ready.
  - Preparing the tower shall make exactly one warm-up prediction.
  - When the field takes focus with the tower prepared, Otos shall start exactly one warm prediction (`SearchTextTower.warm`, logged `op=search.tower phase=warmed cause=focus` with its page-ins), so pages macOS evicted are read back before the query comes, at most one at a time.
  - A focus while the warm prediction or the tower's load runs shall start none; a query typed while it runs shall show its words answer without waiting on it and its tower answer after exactly one further prediction; it is no load the admission grants and the background room never holds it; and it never loads a tower that is not prepared.
  - If a load fails, then it shall report the query failed and be retried on its backoff.
  - An idle tower shall let its model go under memory pressure.
  - Without that intent, the same preparation shall run after the first grid as the last load the admission grants (`BackgroundLoad.searchPrepare`, behind every other), and withdraw if the intent comes first.
  Example: Given the field reads `a red picture` and the library holds a red and a blue photo with a margin that keeps only the best, the grid shows the red one; given a search and the field's clear button clicked or pressed after the keyboard left the field, or Escape in it, the search ends and the field is empty; given `a blue picture`, the blue one with no day buckets; given an index that matches nothing, the empty state is `noSearchResults("a dog", stillReading: 0)`; given an emptied field, the whole scope; given a query whose reading is held, the red results kept and marked as searching, a filter change while it is held publishing nothing else, an emptied field publishing the whole scope at once, and the answer published when the reading ends; given a reading that fails, `searchFailed("a red picture")` over an empty grid, and the red photo once a retry reads it; given a scope of the blue photo alone, `a red picture` keeps the blue one; given a reading that fails, it is reported failed and read again on its backoff; given a reading held at its suspension, it is being read until it ends; given the reading or read states, the subtitle reads `Searching…` only while reading.; given the intent to search and a query typed while the encoder's load is held, the query is read with the tower only after that load and the encoder loads once; given a query typed while the first grid's readying holds the load, its words reading is published without awaiting the load, a second query typed then too, and the newest read with the tower once the load ends; given the words reading, only the photo showing the words matches and no tower is loaded; given a query typed while the load is held in the window, the photo showing its words is shown at once, still searching, then the picture's answer, never an empty grid between; given a prepared tower that answers first, one publication and the words dropped; given a prepared tower held, the words answer first, still searching, then the tower's once; given a preparation, one warm-up prediction; given a focus on a prepared tower, one warm prediction, and one more for a later focus; given a second focus while it is held, none; given a query typed while it is held, the words answer first and the tower's after one more prediction; given a focus while the load is held, no warm prediction; given a held background room, the warm prediction runs and the admission grants nothing; given a tower not prepared or let go, a warm prediction predicts and loads nothing; given a load that fails, the query is failed and read once a retry loads; given a query with no intent first, it is read as before; given the intent, no scope is read, and a query's words and picture readings over an unchanged scope read its slots once; given a query typed while the tower's load and the index's vector build are both held, one publication shows the photo showing its words, still searching; given a words reading that matches nothing, no pass reads the owed count and the grid keeps what it shows; given an idle encoder under pressure, its model goes and the next reading loads it; given no intent, the first grid's admitted preparation loads the encoder once and a query typed after it finds it loaded; given a shutdown while it waits, nothing starts; given a later People pass, the preparation still waits behind it
