- **APP-069** The On This Day scope (ADR 0049, `docs/design/on-this-day.md`) shall be every row of the available fixed libraries whose day bucket — the day the item was taken on, on the clock where it was taken (the date of `best_capture_local`, ENR-003), as the grid buckets days — has today's month and day in a year before today's. [verified]
  - Only when no such row exists in the scope (its roots and visibility, before any filter, so a filter only narrows and never turns years into months), the scope shall instead be the rows with today's day of the month in a month before today's (owner direction, 2026-11-12).
  - Days, months and years shall be counted on the Gregorian calendar whatever calendar the user reads dates in (`CalendarDay.calendar(in:)`; dates are written in the user's own calendar), exactly (29 February matches nothing in a common year, the 31st nothing in a 30-day month; undated rows never match).
  - The scope shall be a live query: `SidebarSelection.onThisDay(CalendarDay)` resolved by `RootRegistry.scopePredicate` into `CatalogScopePredicate.DateConstraint.anniversary`, which the gateway turns, in the same read, into one predicate over local capture time (`AnniversaryMatch`: the month and day `best_capture_local` shows, stored as the generated `capture_month` and `capture_day` (schema v62), on rows taken before today's year, or, when no row under it is one On This Day would show, the day of the month on rows taken before today's month, every read of the scope seeking the day's rows through `idx_media_asset_capture_day`), logging `op=onThisDay.match` with its unit, years or months, so order, counts and filter options agree however many years the library spans.
  - The scope's reads shall use the scope's own order, newest capture first, whatever the sort says (`effectiveSort`); the sort control and View ▸ Sort are disabled there and `setSortOrder` refuses; the filter applies.
  - The ViewModel shall own today: it reads it at launch and again on `NSCalendarDayChanged` and `NSSystemTimeZoneDidChange` through one newest-wins stream, and a changed day while On This Day is selected re-selects the scope for the new day.
  - The browser place shall save the scope without its day and reopen it on today.
  - A keyword search shall narrow the scope without reordering it: its results keep the scope's order, never relevance (`CatalogQueryDescriptor.effectiveSearch`, ADR 0048).
  - On This Day shall pick its photos (`OnThisDayPicks`, owner direction 2026-11-13, as Apple Photos' Memories): it never holds a Disliked photo — a stack judged by its key (ADR 0052), so a Disliked key leaves its RAW twin and its burst's other frames out with it — or one Vision is confident is a screenshot or a document.
  - The screenshot-or-document rule shall use the photo's stored confident scene labels, sought by the row's key so a read that starts from another table — the filter's place options — never re-reads every left-out row (schema v52), and counted only from the photo's current search row, the one whose source names the thumbnail the photo has now and the model's revision (`OnThisDayPicks.leftOutSQL`, ADR 0048).
  - A photo not yet read for search shall stay, and so shall one whose row was read from an earlier picture — a re-rendered thumbnail, a new file at a deleted photo's path — until it is read again.
  - A search reading that puts a photo in or out of that rule shall be a catalog change, and so shall a new thumbnail, so an open On This Day is read again.
  - The years-or-months match shall read the same rule (`OnThisDayPicks.shows`, judged by stacks the caller holds or, else, only the stacks that hold the rows judged, built in the read's own transaction from those rows' folders and bursts), so a year is offered exactly when its card would hold a photo.
  - The scope's counts, whose one visible use is its empty state, shall count by the same rule, so a scope whose photos are all files of Disliked-key stacks reads `Nothing on This Day`, never no filter matches or an offline library.
  - Each day's Liked photos shall come first, so a day's card shows a Liked photo when it has one.
  - An anniversary shall show at most 25 photos (`OnThisDayPicks.anniversaryCap`, owner 2026-11-12), the first of its run counted after stacks collapse, so the cards, the bar's counts and the story agree.
  Example: Given photos taken on 9 November 2023 and 2020, the day before, the day after and today itself, On This Day on 9 November 2026 holds the 2023 photo then the two 2020 items under a name sort, counts three, offers their kinds, and a daylight-saving day is midnight to midnight of local time; given photos of 9 November 2025, 9 October and 9 August 2026, On This Day on 9 November 2026 holds only the 2025 photo (and a video filter shows nothing), while on 9 December 2026 it holds the October, August and November 2025 items newest first, 2, 4 and 13 months ago; given the day report arrives with 10 November, the scope becomes 10 November's; given a place saved on 9 November is reopened on 10 November, it shows 10 November's. Given a search for `beach` on On This Day, the matches keep their years' order. Given a day in 2023 with a Liked morning photo, a screenshot, a noon and an evening photo, and a Disliked photo on the day in 2022, On This Day holds the morning, evening and noon photos in that order and no 2022 anniversary, and the screenshot's label write notified a catalog change; given a screenshot, a document, a photo of people and one never read on that day, it holds the people photo and the unread one, and a screenshot whose thumbnail is rendered again comes back until its new thumbnail is read as a screenshot, which is a catalog change; given a day of 30 photos, its anniversary shows its first 25. Given an earlier year whose only photo is a RAW+JPEG pair with a Disliked JPEG key, On This Day falls back to the months and its counts read the months too; once the key is Liked, the year is offered; the year's rows are sought through the day's index, and the scope's order, types, places and count start from it; given only a pair with a Disliked key under a RAW filter, every count is zero and the empty state is `Nothing on This Day`.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/CalendarDay.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/AnniversaryMatch.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSQLTypes.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/RootRegistry.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogQueryGateway.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/CatalogModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/CatalogQueryModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/BrowserPlace.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/CatalogWindowPolicy.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelStory.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/SidebarView.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/CatalogQueryModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/CatalogProjectionWindowStateBuild.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/OnThisDayPicks.swift`

- **APP-070** On This Day's pane shall be one photo card per earlier anniversary that holds a match — a year, or a month when the scope fell back to months (APP-069) — newest first (DES-112). [verified]
  - Projection shall publish `CatalogWindowState.onThisDay`, each anniversary a contiguous run of the scope's ordered IDs with its key item (the run's first) and count (`OnThisDayAnniversaryPolicy`), its distance `N years ago` when its month is today's and `N months ago` otherwise (`OnThisDayInterval`), under a 52 pt bar naming the day and how many items and years (or months) it holds.
  - While the scope shows, the covers shall be pinned in the shared tile store (as the grid pins its visible tiles) and read from it while rendering, so a painted cover never returns to the placeholder.
  - An empty scope shall show `Nothing on This Day` with the day in its message, or the shared filter, checking and offline states.
  - When the user clicks a card, or presses Return or Space on the focused card, the pane shall play that anniversary's story.
  - The arrows shall move focus.
  - View ▸ Play Story (⌘Return) shall play from the newest anniversary and shall be disabled where there is nothing to play.
  - The view-mode picker shall show no segment and shall be disabled there (DES-053).
  Example: Given matches in 2025 and 2023, two cards read `1 year ago` and `3 years ago` with `9 Nov 2025 · 2` and `9 Nov 2023 · 3`; given a click on the 2023 card, the story starts on 2023's first item; given ⌘Return, it starts on 2025's; given matches only on 12 October and 12 August, two cards read `1 month ago` and `3 months ago` and the bar `… from 2 months`.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/OnThisDayAnniversaryPolicy.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/OnThisDayModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/CatalogWindowPolicy.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/CatalogProjectionWindowStateBuild.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/OnThisDayView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/OnThisDayPresentation.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/CatalogMediaEmptyStatePresentation.swift`, `OtosKit/ContentView.swift`, `OtosKit/OtosApp.swift`
  Not covered by tests: the cards' keyboard focus movement and click-to-play are SwiftUI view behavior with no unit seam, checked by hand in the smoke run

- **APP-071** The story player (ADR 0049, DES-112) shall play the On This Day scope's items in its order, across anniversaries, with the loupe's own pane, video and materialization (`viewMode` is `.loupe` and the item *is* `focusedAssetID`, its one owner, so Photo commands and the inspector act on it and every focus writer — the story's moves, Move to Trash's successor, a reorganization's remap — moves the story). [verified]
  - Every focus change shall be a visit, and a report made for another visit — an earlier visit of the same item, a cancelled preparation — shall move nothing.
  - A still shall hold 5 s from when its picture is on screen, or once the pane reports it has none (`time: schedule`).
  - A video shall advance on its player's end report, its segment filling over its trimmed range.
  - If a video's player reports a failure, then the video shall hold like a still on its poster.
  - Nothing shall advance while bytes are awaited, and a failed read shall leave it waiting with an `op=story.stalled` line.
  - Space shall pause and play, freezing the hold's remainder and the video.
  - ← and → shall move across anniversaries.
  - Esc or Close shall return to the cards with focus on the anniversary it was on and the item selected.
  - After the last item the story shall stay there, paused, with Next and Play disabled.
  - A removed item shall move the story to the loupe's survivor, and an emptied scope, a scope change or a day change shall close it.
  - Crop, the white-balance picker, zoom, Trim Video and Live Photo motion shall be off while it plays, and a choice made in it shall never advance it.
  - Every start, advance, hold, pause, video, end and close shall log an `op=story.*` line.
  Example: Given a story on a still whose decode is held, the hold has not started and its report moves nothing; given the decode arrives, the hold starts and its report moves to the next item; given Pause, the hold's report does nothing until Play; given a hold report from an earlier visit of the same item, nothing moves; given a video that fails to play, it holds on its poster; given the last item's video ends, the story stays on it, paused; given the current item is moved to the Trash, the story shows the next one; given a reorganization renames it, the story stays on it and Next goes on from there.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/StoryPlaybackPolicy.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelStory.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelViewMode.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/EditSession.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/StoryPlayerView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/LoupeVideoPlayerView.swift`
  Not covered by tests: a real video reaching its end through AVPlayer (the end report is delivered directly in tests; the failure path runs through AVPlayer)

- **APP-072** People (ADR 0047, `docs/design/people.md`, DES-109–DES-111, DES-113) shall be one sidebar row, under On This Day and before Places, whenever a fixed library exists, opening the People gallery. [verified]
  - The People gallery shall show everyone with a face or something the user said about them, as their key photo (else newest face) cut from its stored preview through the edit that preview shows (DES-110; `person.slash` when the crop leaves the face out, the placeholder while a newer edit's rendering is owed), at the cell's backing size, drawn from the uncached preview so no decode outlives the draw (a JPEG's tile peaks at 1 MB, a HEIC's at most at one whole preview's decode), each tile holding its own pixels and no more, shown without a display copy (`DisplayImage`; a screenful of small faces in a Retina window adds at most 64 MB), with their name or a link-style `Add Name…`, and their photo count.
  - The gallery shall order favourites first, then every named person before every unnamed one, each by most photos, then the name; the hidden people shall come last under `Hidden`, dimmed, the only place they appear.
  - The bar's L1 shall be the visible people and its L3 the stills in the available fixed libraries not yet read for faces (an Immich account's: not yet read from its current preview).
  - The gallery shall be the window's own read (`PeopleGallery`, at the window's priority, no heavy load, run once the first grid is published, and again after each publication that took up a change it reads): the people with the photo counts and newest faces the catalog stores for them (`person_summary`, v73) and their covers' tiles, each sought by key, and the faces on the user's word or a seed from their partial index (`idx_face_anchor`), never a walk of every face.
  - A person whose facts are not yet stored shall not be shown, and before any is stored the pane shall claim nothing.
  - A photo's faces shall be read by key for the targets (`CatalogStore.photoFaces(of:)`) and for the Person sheet's row as it opens.
  - `PeopleAssignment` shall run off every actor on its own loop, asked after each publication that took up a change assignment reads, never awaited by the window: each pass reads what the catalog stores without any face's vector or tile, writes each person's gallery facts that moved, computed from scratch (`PeopleSnapshot.computedGalleryFacts`; announced as `.peopleGallery`, which assignment does not read), and only then assigns every face (ENR-025), and only when what assignment reads hashes differently from what the catalog last settled on (`AssignmentInputs`, v67), reading only the vectors it does not hold; its writes are pages of at most 256 rows.
  - A pass after changes that each named their photos and touched only faces or their photos' dates, kinds and images shall read only those photos' faces, by key (`peopleFaces`), laid over the read it holds; the first pass of a launch and any other shall read every face.
  - A launch with nothing changed shall read no vector.
  - Changes made while a pass runs shall cost one pass after it, however many they are.
  - A pass shall be asked for only by a change People reads: of a photo, its date, kind, grouping or images only when it holds a face (`CatalogChangedFacts.faceTiles`), so dates and renders of photos nobody is in wake nothing.
  - In the gallery, Add Name…/Rename…, Set Birthday…, Add to Favorites/Remove from Favorites, Merge Into ▸ (asked first), dragging one person onto another (asked first), Hide and Unhide shall each act through one intent.
  - A hidden person shall open nothing and leave the filter, and shall merge as anyone does by every path — dragged or dropped on, Merge Into among the people selected with them, a person named with their name — the merged person hidden.
  - The acts the window submits — through its intents, the merge question and its undo stack — shall be checked against the reference model of ENR-024 on random walks: each act's catalog state, each undo and redo restored exactly while nothing else wrote, and the rules no act owns.
  - Opening a person shall show their photos as a leaf under People (`SidebarSelection.person`): every fixed library's photos of them (whether a row holds the person is one seek by person and photo, schema v54, as for the People filter and the likeness sort, never a walk of every face of the person), the People row highlighted, titled with their name, keeping the filter, with Back to People (which keeps it, and exists only in that leaf) and a toolbar menu of their settings.
  - Hiding them shall return to the gallery, and merging them shall show the person they became.
  - When the user saves a name another visible person has, the sheet shall keep the birthday typed and ask `Are these the same person?`, merging on Merge.
  - The answer shall submit the merge without judging it again, so the catalog judges it after any act ahead of it in the People stream, and the window's selection, shown person and filter shall follow the merged person only once the catalog confirms it — a People state that no longer lists the person merged away, arriving before that confirmation, leaves them shown and filtered, and the confirmation takes the window to the person merged into (`PendingPeopleActs`).
  - Once People is built with the merge, the gallery shall scroll to the person merged into wherever their new photo count sorts them (`PendingPeopleActs.takeReveal`), whichever of the answer and that state arrives first; a redo of the merge is that merge and lands the same way — and a refusal there shows `Couldn’t Merge These People` with the disabled act's reason.
  - Someone Else… with such a name shall give the faces to that person, and with a new name shall make one person with the name and birthday typed, one act.
  - The sheet's Save shall be disabled with its reason (DES-038) whenever the save could not happen: an empty name, an unreadable birthday, photos from before the birthday, faces named with a hidden person's name, a taken name whose merge or move the hard rules refuse (that act's reason); every count in a refusal says what it holds, photos, videos or photos and videos, each item once (`MediaCount`), or for Someone Else… a birthday other than the named person's.
  - The gallery's context menu, the menu bar's Photo menu items for the selected person (no keys of their own; disabled while no person is selected), the toolbar menu of a person's photos (its Merge Into ▸ offering the named people only) and each cell's VoiceOver actions shall be one `PersonMenu` the view model builds, a cell's built only as its menu or VoiceOver opens it.
  - No cell's rendering shall read more than its own person, so a People update or a selection change re-renders only the cells whose person or selection changed, the published People state carrying the visible and the hidden people, the names the sheet suggests and each person's index, derived off the main actor, and the toolbar menu of a person's photos built once per People state or scope.
  - The gallery shall draw its focus ring, and Up and Down shall keep the column within the visible and the hidden sections' own rows (`PeopleGalleryStep`).
  - The Person sheet shall say who it names and how many photos Save changes over a row of up to eight of the faces, and shall suggest the visible named people as the name is typed.
  - Every count shall say what it holds (`MediaCount`): `photos`, `videos` when all are videos, and `photos and videos` (`Photos & Videos` in a menu) once both are, so a library video with a face counts in its person's total.
  - A video's face shall never be outlined, its box being one frame's, and shall offer no Make Key Photo (ADR 0047).
  - The face acts shall say who and how many (DES-113): the photo menus group the targeted photos' faces under each visible person — a named person's `Sam ▸` (`Sam · 3 Photos ▸` with several photos) holding Change to with the five people the faces most likely are then Someone Else…, This Is Not Sam, This Is Not a Face and with one photo Make Key Photo for Sam; an unnamed person's `Unnamed Person · 14 Photos ▸`, counting every photo of them, holding Name This Person with the five likeliest named people (a merge, asked first) then New Name…, and This Is Not a Face, with Not the Same Person only in that person's own photos — and the unidentified faces under Assign to: one `Unidentified Face ▸` per face of one photo, and across several photos one `Unidentified Faces · 3 Photos ▸`, whose choices give each photo's face most like the person, only when no photo holds more than one.
  - A lone entry's acts shall sit at the top level under a section heading with its title, the list a submenu, wherever the menu opens.
  - When the user highlights a face item in a photo menu, or points at an Info pane row, Otos shall outline the faces it acts on wherever their photos show (the loupe and both compare panes, through the edit their decode shows and whether or not Info is open, and never on a grid or filmstrip thumbnail; DES-113).
  - The Info pane's People section shall render the same groups and items (APP-041), with a link-style Add Name… and every photo of them in place of a missing name; where only text fits, a person without a name shall be `Unnamed Person`.
  - Every act shall be undoable on the window's undo stack, named by its item and the photos it changed (`Undo Name 3 Photos`, `Undo This Is Not Sam`).
  Example: Given Sam with three photos and a favourite with one, the favourite comes first and a hidden person last under Hidden; given a double-click on Sam, her photos show under People titled `Sam` with the filter kept, and Back to People keeps it, and finds the gallery scrolled where it was (as far as it was scrolled when it left, `PeopleSession.galleryOffset`); given another sidebar row chosen from her photos, Back to People is gone and the row is unfiltered; given Sam hidden while her photos show, the gallery returns, she leaves the filter and does not open; given Ian hidden, dragging him onto Sam or her onto him asks nothing, and once unhidden the drag asks; given a photo with Sam's guessed face and an unidentified one, the menu reads `Sam ▸` (Change to Ian, Someone Else…, This Is Not Sam, This Is Not a Face, Make Key Photo for Sam) then `Unidentified Face ▸` (Assign to), still two submenus in her photos, and a photo with her face alone has its acts at the top level under the heading `Sam`; given a person without a name, their entry is `Unnamed Person · 1 Photo ▸` with Name This Person ▸ Sam asking to merge and New Name… opening the Person sheet, and Not the Same Person only in their own photos; given two photos, `Sam · 2 Photos ▸` without Make Key Photo, and no unidentified entry once a photo holds two unidentified faces; given two such entries, highlighting each outlines its own face and closing the menu clears it; given a pane decode with a crop, the outline is carried through it; given a cropped or quarter-turned photo, its face tile shows the face, and a face the crop leaves out has none; given a rename to a taken name, the merge question; given the People list without the person merged away arriving before the catalog confirms the merge, their photos and filter stay until the confirmation shows Sam's, and a refusal hands them back to that list; given a merge, the gallery reveals the person merged into once, when the later of its answer and the state covering it arrives, never on a state covering an older request; given the merge undone, the person merged away opened, and the merge redone, the same; given Assign to undone and redone, the face returns and is given again, under `Undo Name 1 Photo`; given 738 or 5,000 people, opening the gallery, five People updates and three selection changes build no cell's menu.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/PeopleModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Support/DisplayImage.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSchema.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogQueryGateway.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/PeopleAssignment.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/CatalogProjection.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelPeople.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelMediaItemMenu.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/MediaItemMenuModel.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/MediaItemContextMenu.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/PeopleView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/FaceTileView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/PersonSheetView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/InfoPaneView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/LoupeZoomView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/CompareView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/MediaAssetTileView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/MediaGridBridge.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/SidebarView.swift`, `OtosKit/ContentView.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/RootRegistry.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogQueryGateway.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`
  Not covered by tests: the drag gesture, the gallery's key bindings and focus ring, the face outlines drawn on the loupe and compare panes and their absence from thumbnails, the sheet's name suggestions and the toolbar menu are SwiftUI and AppKit view behavior with no unit seam (their intents and geometry are tested)

- **APP-073** When recognising a query's language, Search shall favour the languages the user searches in (Settings ▸ General ▸ Search Languages, DES-108; default the system's preferred languages), ten to one against the others; choosing a language there adds or removes it, and the last one cannot be removed. [verified]
  Example: Given "kot" and Polish chosen, it is read as Polish; given no choice stored, the system's languages stand; given Finnish and English chosen, the setting reads “Finnish and English”, choosing Swedish adds it and English alone cannot be removed.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/SearchLanguages.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/SearchQueryEncoder.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/SettingsView.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModel.swift`

- **APP-074** Search shall read a query's facts and narrow by them (ADR 0048). [verified]
  - Search shall read a date (a month, a year, today, yesterday, this or last week, month, year) from its English reading, a translated month only when the text it was translated from names that month in its language by Foundation's month names (Swedish "tåg", train, translates "March" and is no month), and in a search language no translation reads, a month name typed as a whole word or one of that language's case forms (Estonian "septembris"; "mail", "main", "meisje" are no month).
  - Search shall read a place, a name the English tagger calls a place or a person (it often calls a place a person) that Apple Maps names so (a case ending of up to three letters aside, "San Franciscos") and that covers a town or more, matched by GPS within its radius, read from a query that may be in a language nothing recognises as the run of capitalised words around that name or else the first such run that is not a lone first word, and never a one-word name an ordinary word follows ("Ferris wheel", "Orange cat").
  - Only that name shall be sent to Maps; Maps saying a name is nowhere shall be remembered, and a lookup that failed shall not.
  - Search shall read a person the user named, matched in the typed text before translation.
  - What is left shall search the picture.
  - A query of facts only shall keep the scope's order and day sections.
  - A photo whose recognised text holds the typed words shall match whatever the cut, and shall rank above every picture-only match when the query names no scene label.
  - When a query names a thing the scene classifier knows, Search shall keep only photos the classifier saw it in or that score within 0.02 of the best.
  Example: Given "beach in September", September's photos ranked for "beach"; given "Dog in San Francisco", photos within the city's radius; given "Abby Rose rannalla", Abby Rose's photos for "rannalla"; given "September" alone, September in date order; given "koer San Franciscos" by a user of Estonian, photos within San Francisco's radius for "koer".
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/SearchQueryParser.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/SearchPlaces.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/SearchQueryEncoder.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/SearchLabelGate.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/PhotoSearchIndex.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/CatalogWindowPolicy.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`

- **APP-075** A collapsed stack (ARC-062) shall be one photo in every browser scope but the duplicates list (DES-116). [verified]
  - The projection shall collapse the scoped order, after search, to each stack's key in the key's place — or, when a filter hides the key, its first member present — so the grid, its day counts, On This Day, the Places map and the empty state count it once, and a person's photo count counts it once.
  - Every action that uses the picture shall act on the shown member — Like and Dislike included, so a collapsed stack is Liked or Disliked as its key is (`PhotoStack.keyChoice`), and On This Day, which leaves Disliked photos out, leaves out every file of a stack whose key is Disliked, its RAW twin included (APP-069).
  - Move to Trash and Reveal in Finder shall act on every member of a collapsed stack and on a member of an expanded one alone.
  - Info's File section shall name every file of a pair and the size of a burst (`Stack`: `Burst · 8 photos`).
  - A pair shall be one frame of a burst, so the projection collapses pairs first, then bursts, and a burst is collapsed only while two or more of its frames are present — an expanded pair's two files are one frame.
  - A collapsed burst's Move to Trash shall reach every file of every frame.
  Example: Given a RAW and its JPEG, the order shows the JPEG alone in its place with its badge, Move to Trash on it reaches both files, and on an expanded RAW only the RAW; given a filter showing one member, that member shows as an ordinary photo; given a collapsed burst whose first frame is a pair, one tile reaching every file, and a file it hides goes to that tile; given a scope holding one burst frame, an expanded pair, both its files show and Move to Trash reaches only the file it is on; given a Disliked JPEG key, the pair carries Disliked.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/PhotoStackModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/CatalogProjectionWindowStateBuild.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelTrash.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelHandOff.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/PhotoInfo.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/PeopleAssignment.swift`

- **APP-076** Expanding, collapsing and choosing a stack's key shall each be one intent (DES-116, DES-117). [verified]
  - S, Photo ▸ Expand Stack / Collapse Stack and a click on a tile's stack badge shall toggle expansion, held by the window and sent with the query request outside the descriptor, so the selection and the browser place stay, and the projection re-collapses the order it last read (its day buckets and map coordinates with it) without running the order query again.
  - Make Key Photo (⇧S) on one member of an expanded stack, and the `JPG`/`RAW` switch on a collapsed pair — in the inspector's header, Info's and the edit panel's, never on the loupe (DES-117) — shall make that member the key through one serial lane, undoable.
  - A selection or focus on a member its stack now hides shall move to the member shown in its place.
  - A click on a badge shall expand the stack the tile stands for (a collapsed burst before its pair) or collapse the innermost expanded stack it is in.
  - Make Key Photo shall choose a pair's file or a burst's frame, each in its own column.
  Example: Given a collapsed pair, it carries no badge; expanded, its members read `RAW` and `JPG` with the key marked; given a collapsed burst of two frames, its badge reads `2`; expanded, a pair frame keeps its `RAW+JPG` badge on the burst's band. Given a pair shown, Info and the edit panel offer its switch for the file shown; given a collapsed pair, its tile carries no badge.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelStacks.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModel.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/MediaAssetTilePresentation.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/PairSwitch.swift`, `OtosKit/OtosApp.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/InfoPaneView.swift`, `OtosKit/ContentView.swift`

- **APP-077** A still's Info pane shall open with Description (ADR 0053, DES-088), above People. [verified]
  - The Description section shall show the description Apple's on-device model wrote — one plain, matter-of-fact sentence of what the photo shows, never an age, never the photo's own text copied — selectable, over `Written by Apple Intelligence`.
  - When the model declined, the section shall show `No Description`.
  - While one is owed, the section shall show `Not Written Yet`, `Not Written Yet — Apple Intelligence isn’t ready.`, `Turn on Apple Intelligence in System Settings to describe photos.` or `This Mac can’t describe photos.`, as `SystemLanguageModel` reports.
  - With no single item, the section shall show `—`.
  - A movie shall have no Description section.
  - The description shall be re-read for the item shown whenever the description writer announces a write and after each Info read lands, on the loop that re-reads In This Photo, so a description written while the pane shows its photo appears without reselecting it.
  - The description shall not be editable and shall never be drawn over the photo.
  Example: Given a written description, the section shows it; given a declined one, `No Description`; given one owed with Apple Intelligence off, the sentence that says so; given a movie, no section; given no single item, `—`.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/PhotoInfo.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/InfoPaneView.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelInspector.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/PhotoDescriber.swift`

- **APP-078** Each On This Day anniversary shall show a title written by Apple's on-device model from the descriptions of up to eight of its photos in play order and how long ago it is (ADR 0053, DES-112). [verified]
  - The title shall show on its card, on its own line under the cover above the date and count, the line kept empty until a title arrives so the card's height never changes; in the story bar after `N years ago`; and first in the card's VoiceOver label.
  - An anniversary none of whose photos is described shall have no title; a title shall be written again when those descriptions change, and a declined request shall leave none.
  - The window shall hand the anniversaries it shows to `AnniversaryTitleWriter` through one newest-wins stream and assign the titles it publishes.
  Example: Given an anniversary with a described photo, its title is written from that description; given one with none described, no title; given a title, the card's accessibility label begins with it.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/AnniversaryTitleWriter.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelStory.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/OnThisDayView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/StoryPlayerView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/OnThisDayPresentation.swift`

- **APP-079** Every scope shall be titled by one owner, `CatalogWindowViewModel.windowTitle`, set once for the whole detail pane. [verified]
  - The title shall be the person's name for a person's photos, else the title Projection publishes for the scope shown — `All Media`, `Places` (and an area or stack under it), `On This Day`, `Duplicates` (and a group under it), `People`, a library's, card's, camera's or Immich account's name, an import's date, `All Imports`.
  - No pane shall name itself, so none is titled with the app's name.
  Example: Given each scope, the window's title is its name, never `Otos` and never blank once the catalog has loaded.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModel.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/CatalogWindowPolicy.swift`, `OtosKit/ContentView.swift`

- **APP-080** From the moment the user selects another scope until Projection publishes it, Otos shall let nothing of the scope they left act (DES-040: the operation on screen belongs to the current selection). [verified]
  - The pane shall no longer draw it (APP-084), and this shall hold as a defence for whatever keeps its state.
  - No item shall be a target (`targetIDs`), so Move to Trash, choices, export, share and the photo menus act on nothing.
  - The edit controls and the Photo menu's edit items (`canEdit`), the Info pane's face acts, drag-out, a stack badge and opening the loupe or compare shall do nothing.
  - The duplicates list's acts — Move Copies to Trash, a keep, a rule, Keep All, Review Again, opening a group — and the People gallery's, the menu bar's person items included, shall do nothing and their menu items shall be disabled.
  - The Places map shall open no stack or area.
  - Play Story shall not start.
  Example: Given the duplicates list and a click on All Media, the list is no longer drawn and Move Copies to Trash and ⌘⌫ are unavailable, and All Media's own targets act once it shows; given a loupe and a click on People, the loupe's photo is no target.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModel.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelCuration.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelDuplicates.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelPeople.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelMap.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelTrash.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelStory.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/EditSession.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelMediaItemMenu.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelHandOff.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelStacks.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelViewMode.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/MediaGridSelectionController.swift`

- **APP-081** A scope that spans the libraries and accounts (`SidebarSelection.showsOneCopy`: All Media, Places and its map areas, People and a person, On This Day, All Imports, and search within them) shall show one copy of each set of identical copies — rows with the same current SHA-1, in two libraries, a library and an account, or one folder — and one library's scope, an import batch's, Duplicates and its groups, and a map stack's members shall show every file (ADR 0054, owner 2026-11-14). [verified]
  - Two files with the same digest in one library shall be one photo too: All Media shows the first by path, the library's own view shows both, Duplicates lists them as one group within their library (ADR 0042), and a choice on either is written on both (APP-089).
  - The shown copy shall be a library's before an account's, then the first by root and path, among present copies; `media_asset.shown_copy_root_id` names it and three SQLite triggers keep it in the transaction of every write that moves a digest, its stamps, a file's size or mtime, its presence or its path (v60).
  - A row shall be left out when its shown copy's root is in the scope; a row whose shown copy's root is not (its library not available) shall be left out when a copy ranked before it is present in the scope, read through the digest index for those rows alone, so with three or more copies only the next best reachable copy shows, and the preferred copy shows again when its library returns, with no write on mount or unmount.
  - An uploaded Immich asset's `checksum` (Base64 SHA-1) shall be its row's digest, stamped with the facts its listing wrote, and a size reported later shall fill a stamp listed without one; an external library's asset (`libraryId` set), whose checksum hashes its path, states none of its bytes.
  - An external asset shall instead be one photo with a library file when the account mirrors that library (`immich_mirror`) and the asset is that file's pair (`immich_mirror_pair`, v79).
  - A library shall have a set of server prefixes on an account's server (v80), each learned from one asset's `originalPath`, which its own path checksum must confirm, asked for the first file no prefix explains that has an unpaired asset of the same name, size and whole-second mtime.
  - A round shall ask one path per library and another round shall run only while the last one's prefixes paired files, and a candidate whose path taught nothing (refused, no path, another folder, a checksum that does not confirm, a known prefix) shall be remembered at the asset's path checksum and never asked again, its refusal logged with its reason and the folders compared.
  - At the end of each pass that owes the walk, every file of the library shall be paired with a free asset whose path checksum is the SHA-1 of `path:`, a prefix and the file's relative path in either Unicode normal form (`ImmichPathChecksum.everyForm`), from the listing alone, with no download and no digest, and unpaired from an asset its path no longer names.
  - A pair shall be one photo while its versions agree — the same digest when both rows have one, else the asset listed with the file's current size and whole-second modification time, or with that time alone while the server has not read the asset and reports no size (v83, decided 2026-11-28) — and two photos while they differ (ADR 0054, decided 2026-11-17).
  - Rows shall be copies of one photo when they share `media_asset.copy_key`, which the copy triggers keep: a row's current digest of some bytes, or for an agreeing pair the digest either holds, else the pair's own key (its account and asset path, v80), so two servers with one path for two different files keep two photos.
  - The triggers shall hold under every write that fires them, an upsert's `DO UPDATE` included, so an asset whose size the server reports after its pair was made joins its file (v81).
  - A write shall name `.copies`, which the combined scopes, People and Duplicates are built from, only when a row it wrote joined or left a key another row holds (`DigestStanding`), and the walk shall name it when a pair came or went; a digest no other copy holds names only the row's own detail, and one written again unchanged names nothing.
  Example: Given a digest no other file holds, the write names `.detail` alone; given the second file with a digest, `.detail` and `.copies`; given the same digest written again, no change; given a library file and an account asset listed with its SHA-1, or an external asset at the file's mirrored path with its size and mtime — the file with no digest included — All Media shows the library's file and not the asset, no byte is read and no digest written, the account's own scope shows the asset, and Duplicates shows both for an uploaded asset; given the library unavailable, All Media shows the asset; given a library file and two account copies with the library unavailable, All Media shows and counts only the first account copy, and the library's once it is back; given an external-library asset, it is shown; given two copies in one account and the first hidden by Immich's trash, the second is shown; given a digest listed before its size, it stays current once the size arrives; given two same-bytes files in one library, All Media shows one, the library both, Duplicates both as one digest, and a Like on the second Likes both, the one All Media shows among them; given a library whose folders the server lists under two prefixes, one pass learns both with one request each and pairs every file, a prefix listed later is learned with one request, a candidate that taught nothing is not asked on the next owed walk, either normal form of an accented path pairs, and two accounts on two servers with one path for two different files show two photos.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/CatalogModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSchema.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogQueryGateway.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/RootRegistry.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/ImmichClient.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/ImmichCatalogSync.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStoreMirrors.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/CatalogProjectionBuildTypes.swift`

- **APP-082** Info's File section shall have a `Copies` row (ADR 0054, DES-053) showing the library or account of each other present copy of the item's photo (the rows sharing its copy key, v79), by the name the sidebar shows, in the order copies are shown in, with a count when one holds several (`Family (2 copies) and Pictures`); `None` when it has a digest and no copy; `Not Checked Yet` while it has neither a copy nor a digest. [verified]
  Example: Given a library file with an account copy, the row names the account; given a file with no copy, `None`; given an asset with no digest, `Not Checked Yet`.

- **APP-083** Duplicates shall compare copies within each library or account by default: identical copies and versions of the same photo group only when every copy is in the same library or account, so a mirror — a library and the Immich server that indexes the same folders — lists nothing, and the same digest in two libraries is two groups or none (ADR 0042). [verified]
  - Where Settings ▸ General ▸ `Find duplicates across libraries and accounts` (off by default, remembered as `duplicates.acrossLibraries`) is on, Duplicates shall list copies across them too.
  - An Immich external library's asset (`source_path_sha1` set) shall never be a copy in Duplicates, with either reach: a mirror pair adds only the library file to any group, a file with only its mirror is no group, and so no click, folder rule or default can keep a mirror and move the file it mirrors.
  - `DuplicatesGateway.countsAsCopy` shall be the one rule the copies read, the Duplicates scope and its count share.
  - The Store shall hold the one current reach, which the copies read, the build, the Duplicates scope and a group's scope all use, and a change of it shall regroup at once.
  - The Duplicates scope's reads — order, types, places, count — shall seek its rows through the digest index by the digests its group subquery finds, never testing every row of the catalog.
  Example: Given copies in one library and a mirror of one of them in another, only the library's pair is a group, named by its library, and a mirror alone is no group; given the setting on, one group of every copy; given two libraries in the Store, the copies read, the Duplicates scope and a group's scope hold only the same library's copies until the reach is set across, which announces a change of what Duplicates reads (`.duplicateFacts`); given a library and an account holding one photo, no copies until the reach is across; given a library file and its mirrored external asset, across libraries, no group before or after the pairing and none with the library unavailable, and with a second copy of the file in its library, a group of the two files alone, in the copies read, the scope and its count.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/DuplicateModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStoreDuplicates.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/RootRegistry.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogQueryGateway.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/DuplicatesProjection.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelDuplicates.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/SettingsView.swift`

- **APP-084** When the user clicks a sidebar row, Otos shall change the detail pane in the step that applies it (DES-118): the pane, its title and its surface follow the selected scope at once (`CatalogDetailPane`), never the scope left. [verified]
  - Until the selected scope's state is applied, the pane shall wait: the surface's bar with what the window holds without the scope's read — no count the read decides — over an empty content area, and nothing of the scope left is drawn or acts (APP-080).
  - The one indeterminate indicator shall appear only once the waiting pane reports a presented frame (`waitingFramePresented`), so a scope whose state arrives within the frame of the click shows none; each new scope intent waits for its own frame; VoiceOver names the indicator by the scope's title.
  - Each waiting bar shall hold its loaded bar's height (52 pt, the duplicates bar its own) and On This Day's day title its place.
  - When the state arrives, the pane shall fill in.
  Example: Given a library shown and a click on Places, the pane is Places waiting with no indicator and the title is `Places` before any state arrives; after a presented frame the indicator shows; the published Places state shows the map; given the state applied before the frame, no indicator is ever shown; given a second click, its wait starts without an indicator.

- **APP-085** Move to Trash shall show its result before the Store records it (DES-119). [verified]
  - In the step that applies the intent, the targets shall leave the published window — Projection takes them off the window it last built, with no catalog read (`CatalogProjectionIntent.hide`), out of the grid with their day buckets and stacks — taken off the last publication itself while nothing was built since it, so the grid's order is an edit of the one the window shows, built and committed in the turn that delivers it — the map, On This Day's runs and the duplicate groups — the selection or focus moves to the nearest survivor, `Undo Move to Trash` is registered and the duplicates receipt shown; the Store's mark follows on the trash intent stream.
  - Every later publication shall keep the items off until a pass that read the catalog after the mark landed covers it, so a read from before the mark, a held answer included, never brings them back, and the read of the mark applies nothing again when nothing else moved; the pass that retires the hold reads the mark even when the Store's change for it has not reached Projection yet.
  - An undo, a second Move to Trash or a scope change made before the mark lands shall be applied after it, and an undo shall put back exactly what the mark hid.
  - Every command shall have an identity of its own (`MediaTrashBatchID`), two issued in one millisecond included, so one command's hide, mark and undo never take in another's.
  - If a mark fails, then it shall bring the items back selected under DES-076's failure alert and take its undo entry off the stack; one that hid nothing shall take its entry off too.
  - The large-download question shall wait before the hide only for a command whose targets' reading has not finished, which reads for itself (APP-045).
  - Undo's Put Back shall be the move undone and show its result the same way, before its file work: in the step that applies Undo, Projection puts each item back in the window beside the neighbours its hide recorded — after its predecessor, or else before its successor, whichever is shown or itself put back — with its day and stack (`CatalogProjectionIntent.putBack`), first into the order last published, so the grid takes it in by the inserts and commits it in the turn that delivers it, the returning tiles painted with the thumbnails they held (APP-015); the queue's put-back follows, and every publication until a pass has read it puts the items back the same way.
  - Items the queue could not put back shall leave again once that read covers the hold, under DES-076's put-back alert.
  - An item neither of whose neighbours is shown, and the map, On This Day and the duplicate groups, shall follow the Store's read.
  Example: Given the Store's mark held, the item is gone from the published grid and ⌘Z is offered; given the mark failing, the item returns selected with the alert and no undo; given ⌘Z before the mark lands, the item comes back and nothing is pending; given a second Move to Trash before the first lands, both leave and ⌘Z takes back the second alone; given People and back while the mark is held, the item stays off the held order. Given three hides in a row, each published order is an edit of the one before it. Given ⌘Z with the queue's put-back held, the item is back in the published grid at once and the file is still in the Trash; given the file gone from the Trash at that point, the item leaves again with `This item couldn’t be put back.`; given three hides undone in turn, each Put Back publishes the order its hide was taken from, an edit of the order shown, with no read; given a mark, and then a Put Back's Store work, reported landed before the Store's change for it reaches Projection, the item stays off, and then stays shown, through every publication, the last sharing the grid the Put Back showed.

- **APP-086** A curation choice — Like, Dislike, Mark as Not Rated, from every surface — shall show its result before the Store records it (DES-120). [verified]
  - In the step that applies the intent, the choice shall be held in `MediaChoiceOverlay`, its targets' tiles take it from the tile store and the grid reconfigures the visible ones in place (`TileChoiceChange`, no structural apply), the loupe and Compare panes and the toolbar control show it, Advance After Choice advances, `Undo Like` is registered, and Projection takes a target the shown choice filter excludes off the window with no read (`CatalogProjectionIntent.choose`).
  - The choice shall be held and shown on every identical copy (ADR 0054) of a target that the tile store holds, by the digest each tile's detail read (`MediaTileDetail.copyDigest`), as the catalog's trigger writes it on every copy; a failed write withdraws it from each, and only the targets are selected and counted.
  - Every tile-detail read shall answer through the overlay, so a read that started before the write landed shows the choice, and the first read that started after the landing retires it; Projection keeps a filtered-out item off until a pass that read the landed write covers it.
  - An undo pressed before the write lands shall show the prior values at once — the Store's priors when its answer has come, else what the window showed — and shall be written after the act from the priors the act's write reported; a second choice on the same item wins whatever the first comes to.
  - If a write finds no row, then it shall be withdrawn and its undo taken off the stack.
  - If a write fails, then it shall be withdrawn, its items selected, its undo removed and the failure alert shown — before, a failed write was logged and nothing was shown.
  Example: Given the write held, the tile, toolbar and Undo show the choice and a read answers it; given the write failing, the tile returns selected with `This choice couldn’t be saved.`; given ⌘Z before the write lands, the tile shows Not Rated at once and the store ends Not Rated; given Like then Dislike with the Like failing, Dislike stays; given Liked only and a Dislike, the photo leaves with no read and comes back when withdrawn; given two copies of one photo in the window, both tiles show a Like in the step that applies it, a third undigested file does not, and a failed Dislike returns both while only the target is selected.

- **APP-087** A People act whose result is known without a build — Add Name… / Rename… / Set Birthday…, Add to / Remove from Favorites, Hide / Unhide, Make Key Photo, This Is Not a Face, This Is Not <Person> / Not the Same Person, Assign to / Change to a person — shall show its result in the step that submits it (DES-120). [verified]
  - `PendingPeopleActs`, People's one overlay, shall lay its effect over the published state, the people it edits in the gallery's own order (`CatalogPeopleState.overlaying`, `CatalogPeopleState.galleryOrder`) and the faces it changes over the targets' faces on the photo menus and the Info pane (`PhotoFaces.overlaying`), and its undo shall be on the stack at once (`PeopleUndoRecord`, filled by the store's answer ahead of any undo on the stream).
  - The act shall stay shown until a People state whose read covers the request the write's answer named arrives (`CatalogPeopleState.covers`, `CatalogProjection.requestPeopleBuild`) and, for an act on faces, a read of the targets' faces asked after the answer (`PhotoFaces.landedThrough`), so a read from before the write never takes it back.
  - A covering state that arrives before the answer shall retire it at the answer (`PendingPeopleActs.builtThrough`), and the answer to a landed act on faces shall ask for that read.
  - An undo or redo shall show what it restores at once.
  - If a write is refused or fails, then it shall be withdrawn, its undo removed, and the People alert shall say so.
  - A new person and a merge, and a person's photo count, shall follow the store and the next build.
  Example: Given Add to Favorites with the store unanswered, the person is favourite and first and `Undo Add to Favorites` is offered; given the store refusing, they are back where they were with `This change couldn’t be saved.`; given a state built before the write, the person stays hidden; given This Is Not Sam then ⌘Z before either lands, the face is Sam's at once and in the store after; given a People state read before the write, or one covering the act while its undo is outstanding, arriving before or after the answer, the name, the hide, the face or the undo shown is kept.

- **APP-088** Expanding or collapsing a stack shall be published inside the Projection intent that asks for it (DES-120), from the collapse the last pass made of the order it holds (`showExpansion`, `CatalogProjection.expanding`), with no read and no wait for a pass in flight, which publishes with the expansion asked meanwhile. [verified]
  - Only the stacks whose expansion changed shall be collapsed again (`PhotoStackCollapse.changingExpansion`), the result being the full collapse's, and the grid shall take the new order as an edit of the one it shows, applied as that edit's inserts and deletes.
  - The Places map's located photos shall take the same edit and On This Day's anniversaries shall be counted again, and an expansion that On This Day's cap would cut shall wait for the pass.
  - When the user clicks Import, the bar shall disable its Import in the click's step, as a held click is drawn (`heldImportClicks`), until a coordinator presentation published after it took the click is applied.
  - When the user changes a filter or sort, Otos shall show the new choice in its control and the window subtitle at once and keep the grid, still acting, until its read.
  Example: Given a pair collapsed, the expansion is published before any pass runs and reads no order; given Import clicked, the button is disabled before the coordinator sees it; given Liked only chosen, the subtitle reads `Filtered` while the grid keeps its items.

- **APP-089** A Like, Dislike or named face shall belong to the photo, not the copy (ADR 0054, ADR 0047), over the copies the shown copy is chosen among (the same copy key: the same current SHA-1 in a library or an account, or a mirror pair whose versions agree). [verified]
  - A choice act on any copy shall be written on every copy, whatever its presence, in the same transaction (trigger `media_asset_choice_made`), so every read of a choice — the order under a choices filter, counts, On This Day, the stacks, the tile badge on whichever copy shows, Info — reads the photo's; undo writes the previous choice back as an act.
  - Each act shall take the next order of `choice_sequence` (`media_choice_sequence`, v64); when a copy joins a photo every copy takes the choice of the latest act among them, and choices made before v64 rank before every act, Liked before Disliked before Not Rated.
  - Deleting a copy shall leave the choice on the others.
  - A person's photos, People and the People filter shall find a photo by a face on any present copy (`PhotoFaceSQL`, through `has_copies` and the copy key's index), whether the person's photos are sought or the order walked, and a person's photo count shall count the photo once (`PeopleSnapshot.computedGalleryFacts`).
  - Each copy shall be read for its own faces from its own preview (they are not the same pixels: Otos's 2560 px rendering, Immich's 1440 px preview), and every surface shall resolve a photo's faces through the same copies (`PhotoFaceSQL.presentCopiesOfKey`, asked for each key of a file with a face and a present copy, read with its faces; `PeopleSnapshot.copies`).
  - Info's People section and the photo menus shall list the shown file's own faces, then each visible person named only on another present copy with that copy's face (`PhotoFaces.faces(of:)`), never another copy's unidentified faces.
  - A face shall be outlined only where its own copy shows, and an act on it shall act on its own copy.
  - Several selected copies shall list a face once.
  - The Person sheet's row shall show each photo once.
  - A face's likeness shall be to the person's faces in other photos only.
  - People's unchecked tally shall count a photo once while any present copy of it is unread.
  Example: Given a library file and its account copy, liking the account's copy likes both; given a file and its mirror paired with no digest, a Like or Dislike on either is written on both; a Liked filter finds the library's in All Media and in its library, and undo un-likes both; given the account's copy liked before the library's digest is read, the library's copy is Liked once it is; given a copy disliked later and then found, every copy is Disliked; given a copy deleted, the others keep the choice; given a v62 catalog whose copies disagree, v64 gives Liked and Disliked Liked, Disliked and Not Rated Disliked; given a face only on a copy no combined scope shows, the person (sought and walked), People and the People filter find the photo and count it once; given a stack's JPEG and its account copy, the person's photo is the stack's; given Sam named only on the account's copy, the library's copy lists her with the account's face after its own, not the account's unidentified face, loses her when `This Is Not Sam` is held on that face, and both selected list her once; given a face named on one copy and the other copy read afterwards, the name stays the user's, the other copy lists it before and after its reading and its own face once assignment gives it the person, counted once; given two unread copies, the tally counts one photo; given a copy's identical face, its likeness is to the person's other photos.

- **APP-090** The window shall say what its catalog's open is doing (DES-121). [verified]
  - Store shall apply the pending migrations one at a time and report, as the newest value of one stream (`CatalogStore.openings`), the step running and how many — known before the first runs, the registrations less the identifiers the catalog records — and the libraries and accounts the catalog holds, counted before any migration with SQL every schema version answers; it finishes the stream when the open ends.
  - Until the catalog loads, every scope shall wait (APP-084), titled as it will be once loaded where the scope alone names it (the fixed scopes, an import's date) and `Otos` where only the catalog does (a library, account, device or person), so no title changes to another name at the load.
  - Once a frame has been presented without it, the waiting pane shall show the one indeterminate indicator over `Opening catalog…` or `Updating catalog…` with `Step N of M` when there are several updates, `Optimizing catalog…` while the open compacts the file, or `Couldn’t open the catalog` when the load failed, at one height in each.
  - After the migrations, an open whose file is a tenth or more free pages (and at least 256 MB of them) shall rewrite it without them (`VACUUM`, `CatalogStore.compactIfOwed`) when the volume holds twice the used pages above the free-space floor, else skip it, logged (`op=catalog.compaction phase=skipped reason=freeSpaceFloor`), for a later open, and log each outcome (`notOwed`, `finish` with its milliseconds and the file's bytes before and after).
  - The sidebar shall show On This Day, People and Places for one library or account and All Media for two, from the open's count, and no other row.
  - A click on one while the catalog opens shall select it and keep the rows.
  - Every migration and every other step of the open shall be logged with its cost (`op=catalog.migration`, `op=catalog.database.open`, `op=catalog.start`'s `migrations` and `migrationsMs`).
  - Migrations shall run with foreign keys enforced as they run, with no whole-catalog check after each, but for a table rebuild (v66's `face`, v71's `media_asset`), which runs with them off so the old table's drop cascades nothing, and checks the tables it made and, for `media_asset`, that every key the old table held is in the new one (ADR 0002).
  Example: Given the reference catalog file after v71 (23.6 % of its pages free, 634 MB), the compaction is owed, and a tenth less, or under 256 MB, is not; given a catalog two migrations behind, the open runs them as steps 1 of 2 and 2 of 2 and then none; given a catalog of two libraries and a card, the open reports two and finishes; given an open at step 1 of 2 before a presented frame, nothing is claimed and the pane waits with no indicator; after the frame, `Updating catalog…`, `Step 1 of 2`, the All Media, Places, People and On This Day rows and no Duplicates row; a click on Places waits for its own frame with the rows kept; the load replaces the status and the rows follow the libraries; with one library no All Media row, with none no fixed row, with one update no step; before the load each scope's title is its loaded title, or `Otos` for a library or person; in a hosted window, one spinning indeterminate indicator, drawn light and dark; the status is one height in every state.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStorePersistence.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/CatalogModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/SidebarSelectionPolicy.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModel.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelMap.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/ScopeWaitingView.swift`, `OtosKit/ContentView.swift`
  Not covered by tests: the status's VoiceOver reading is read from code, not from VoiceOver; the reference catalog's next migration is the first to log its cost there

- **APP-091** Every image a layer or SwiftUI's `Image` shows — grid thumbnails, loupe and Compare previews, live edits, face tiles, On This Day covers, Places and Duplicates tiles — shall be a `DisplayImage` whose pixels are drawn off the main actor into an IOSurface of its own when it is made, so the window's commit that first shows it decodes nothing on the main thread (ADR 0016). [verified]

## Media Enrichment

- **ENR-001** Enrichment shall enrich every cataloged image and video in the background with a thumbnail and parsed media metadata, prioritizing what is in the viewport, then connected/removable source work in fallback claiming, and otherwise following the default newest-first browser usefulness order. [verified]
  Example: Given a library of materialized and dataless media, the background coordinator brings every asset to enriched, materializing dataless files first when none are local; given the user scrolls, visible assets are claimed ahead of background work (ENR-018) and priority claiming skips rows already claimed; given no visible priority candidate is available and removable sources are connected, fallback claims offer removable-source assets before fixed-library assets; given fallback claims within the same source class, they prefer newest best-capture-date assets before stable path tie-breakers rather than path order.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinator.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`

- **ENR-002** The grid tile shall render the asset's thumbnail once enriched and overlay only the video duration/fallback badge when applicable, loading and decoding the thumbnail off the main actor and synchronously repainting from TileContentStore when available. [verified]
  Example: Given an asset with a `thumbnailPath`, its tile shows the 256 px JPEG image at its native aspect-fit rect; given re-enrichment writes a new thumbnail path, source size, or source modification stamp, the tile reloads the image and applies only a non-nil decode result; given only `durationSeconds` changes, the video badge text updates without restarting the thumbnail load; given filename or best-capture-date facts exist, the tile does not receive or render them; given no thumbnail has ever decoded for that asset in the current process, a full-square neutral placeholder face shows; given a decoded thumbnail is already in tile store for that asset, the tile paints it synchronously during configuration.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Views/MediaAssetTileView.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/TileContentStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/MediaGridBridge.swift`

- **ENR-003** The timeline shall run on local capture time (ADR 0039). [verified]
  - The generated `best_capture_local`, used by the Date sort, the grid's day sections, On This Day, search's date spans, People's birthday rules, Info's Captured line and enrichment priority, shall be the library's capture wall clock (`CaptureTimestamps.captureWallClock`, stored as `capture_wall_compact`, a video's duration-derived wall included) read as if it were UTC (`CaptureLocalTime`), so an item is ordered by and sits on the day its own clock read, whatever zone the Mac is in.
  - An item whose file records no wall clock shall be placed by its absolute time — the library's capture instant (`captureInstant`, kept as `capture_instant_unix`: the first role in `instantPrecedence` whose time has a UTC instant; a record that is not a valid capture time is left out), else filesystem creation date, else modification date — read with `assumed_utc_offset_seconds`, the offset of the Mac's zone when Otos recorded that time, at that time, kept while the time is unchanged (`CaptureLocalTime.assumedUTCOffset`, written by the scan upsert and `writeMetadataProjection`; migration v23 made it for existing rows in the zone it ran in), taken at the second it falls in, so both readings are whole seconds and a read whose wall clock is the second an unread item was placed by does not move it (migration v56).
  - Grid tiles shall not render it.
  - Camera enumeration shall fill created/modified facts through one owner, `CameraFileDate`, which resolves each independently among the same six ImageCapture date properties (creation, EXIF creation, file creation, modification, file modification, EXIF modification) but in opposite precedence: `captureDate` (creation-first) feeds `createdAt`, `modificationDate` (modification-first) feeds `contentModifiedAt` — the same value `revalidate` compares against.
  - An item with no usable date shall stay cataloged with NULL date columns (blocked at plan time later, never dropped).
  Example: Given a floating Exif time (no offset, no instant) and a file created a year later, the photo is ordered, sectioned and matched by On This Day on the day its clock read; given photos taken at 20:00 at −08:00 and at 08:00 the next day at +11:00, each is on its own clock's day and the California photo sorts first though its instant is later; given an enriched asset has only an absolute timestamp, `best_capture_local` is that instant read in the zone assumed when it was recorded, before filesystem dates; given none, the filesystem date read the same way; given the absolute time is unchanged, a later write in another zone keeps the assumed offset, and given it changes, the offset is assumed again; given migration v23 over rows with a floating wall, an offset wall, only a creation date and no date, each is its wall read as UTC, the creation date read in the migrating zone, or NULL, and every index over the old column is rebuilt under its own name; given migration v56 over an unread item dated 20:16:27.353 UTC with an assumed −07:00, its read twin with the wall clock 13:16:27, and an item dated half a second before 1970, the first two read 13:16:27, the third the second before 1970 in that offset, and every index over the column is rebuilt under its own name; given a camera item with no usable date field, `best_capture_local` remains nil and Store can report it via `assetsMissingBestDateFacts()`; given a camera file has distinct creation and modification dates, the cataloged `createdAt` and `contentModifiedAt` differ and match `CameraFileDate.captureDate`/`.modificationDate` respectively; given a grid tile is configured, the visible tile detail omits `captureLocalTime` and no relative date text is formatted.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/CaptureTimestampProjections.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/CatalogModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSchema.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStorePersistence.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogQueryGateway.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/AnniversaryMatch.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/PhotoInfo.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/CameraSourceMonitor.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModelSourceMonitoring.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/MediaGridSupport.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/MediaAssetTileView.swift`

- **ENR-004** Thumbnails shall be app-owned files under `Application Support/<bundleID>/Thumbnails/` referenced by a stored relative path. [verified]
  - Each thumbnail shall be 256 px long edge, JPEG — for a still drawn from the same `ImageRenderer` development as its stored preview (ADR 0035), for a video or an unreadable still generated from QuickLook with icon fallback rejected (a definitive-but-thumbnail-less outcome stamps the asset and the grid renders a broken-media placeholder).
  - Thumbnails shall be staged in the `Thumbnails/.staging/` subdirectory before Store validation, atomically promoted for accepted results, with decoded images held only in cost-bounded tile store (visible IDs pinned), and deleted when superseded or when the asset row is purged by proven deletion, explicit library-root removal, or app-owned staged-temp startup cleanup.
  - Every derived file (thumbnail, preview, face frames) shall live exactly as long as a row names it: a camera's provisional thumbnail is promoted only inside the write that names it, for a row with no thumbnail.
  - When a launch's previous session did not end clean, or its stored counters are unknown, the launch shall remove every file in the derived folder that no present row names or would write (a clean launch lists nothing; names derive from identity, re-checked inside a write transaction) and log the folder by kind (`op=derived.folder phase=census`).
  - Catalog schema changes shall never purge thumbnails via destructive database reset.
  Example: Given renders, a re-render, a file moved outside Otos and rendered again, a deleted file, a camera thumbnail offered for a rendered row, and leftover unnamed files, the derived folder holds exactly the names the rows hold after the launch's reconciliation, plus a file of a present row's own identity that its column does not name yet; given enrichment, a staged 256 px JPEG is promoted to its final relative name only after `Store.applyEnrichmentBatch` validates the current SQLite source signature; given an accepted result clears or changes the stored thumbnail path, the prior app-owned file is deleted after the catalog write; given a stale result, its staged file is discarded and the previous final thumbnail remains; given a promotion failure in a batch, already accepted rows remain committed and the failed item remains retryable; given the grid decodes a thumbnail, the decoded `CGImage` is owned by tile store keyed by asset identity and image content key, may be budget-dropped only when unpinned, and is never catalog truth or persisted; given app-owned thumbnail storage starts, everything left in `.staging/` is deleted, listing only that subdirectory, while originals and finished thumbnails are untouched; given saved-root normalization changes the asset's deterministic thumbnail name, Store rewrites the catalog path and moves or deletes the old app-owned thumbnail file so no orphan remains; given QuickLook cannot produce a real thumbnail (icon fallback rejected), enrichment still stamps the asset with a null thumbnail path so the grid shows a broken-media placeholder and the asset is not retried until the file changes on disk; given proven deletion or library removal purges catalog references, app-owned thumbnail files are deleted while originals remain untouched; given a future or unrecognized catalog schema, Store refuses to open without erasing the database or purging thumbnails.

- **ENR-005** Parsed metadata shall be persisted in queryable asset_metadata scalar columns plus metadata detail JSON, decoded on demand and never resident in the grid publication path. [verified]
  - If a stored record does not decode, then it shall be an error to its reader, never an asset without metadata (a detail read fails, import overlay reuse misses and logs, the capture-wall backfill skips and logs).
  - Visible tile details shall use MediaTileDetail rows containing only thumbnail path, best kind, duration, and (size, mtime) stamps.
  - A HEIF or AVIF shall take its orientation from its `irot`/`imir` transform properties when it has them (MediaMetadata 0.6.1), so its upright size is right; library HEIF rows cataloged with no orientation before that re-enrich once (migration `v5_heif_orientation_reread`, ADR 0009).
  - Each record shall store what MediaMetadata reports as reported — orientation and stored size from `geometry`, the codec by `VideoCodec.identifier`, timestamps and locations under the library's own role and source names — and its family's `extractionRevision(for:)` at the read (`asset_metadata.extraction_revision`); kind is the library's (bytes, then the extension's type) and the upright size its `PixelGeometry`.
  - Once per launch, `MetadataRefresher` shall re-read library rows whose record is older than its family's current reading (an absent revision counts as 0) and whose enrichment is otherwise current, from originals already local only (never downloading), writing only the metadata and the columns derived from it while the row's file signature is unchanged and its stored record is still older than its own family's current reading — never compared with the new record's revision, as a re-read can change family (MediaMetadata 0.8.0 reads a PNG, WebP or GIF, which 0.7.1 called `unknown`, as its own family).
  - Card and camera rows shall never be refreshed (ADR 0039).
  Example: Given launch, `fetchCatalog` loads roots and counts without decoding metadata; given visible tile details are fetched, `mediaTileDetails(for:)` reads no filename, best capture date, best format, full metadata, or metadata JSON; given video enrichment includes a duration, `mediaTileDetails(for:)` returns that nullable scalar for the visible tile; given metadata is round-tripped, scalar columns and `metadata_extra_json` preserve `AssetMetadata` values; given a HEIC whose rotation is only in `irot`, enrichment records orientation 6; given a catalog at v4 with library HEIF rows with and without orientation, a JPEG and a card HEIF, only the library HEIF without orientation loses its enrichment stamps.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/AssetMetadata.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/CatalogModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStorePersistence.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSchema.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MediaEnricher.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MetadataRefresher.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogQueryGateway.swift`

- **ENR-006** Scan writes shall touch only scan fact columns and enrichment writes only enrichment fact/overlay columns; scan upserts shall preserve enrichment overlay in the same SQLite row rather than replacing the record. [verified]
  Example: Given an enriched asset, a re-scan that changes its facts updates the scan facts and preserves thumbnail_path, timestamp role facts, enriched kind/format, metadata detail rows, and the stamps in the same record.
