- **ENR-007** Each enrichment product's staleness — metadata, rendering (thumbnail and preview), digest — shall depend only on the current `(size, mtime)` source signature (mtime in integer seconds) against that product's own stamp, never on whether products exist (ADR 0009). [verified]
  - A definitive-but-empty outcome shall still stamp, and an mtime-only change shall count as stale.
  - A hash shall be usable only when its canonical BLOB is 16 bytes and both recorded signature fields are present and current.
  - A claim shall cover its row's stale products by the root's claim shape: a fixed library is claimed while any product is stale, a `.removableVolume` card first while its metadata is, and a camera only while its metadata is (ADR 0018/0013).
  - A provisional thumbnail shall leave the rendering stale; an edit shall clear only the rendering stamp.
  Example: Given an unsupported file enriched once, it is stamped and not retried; given a re-scan that changes only mtime, every product becomes stale and the asset re-enriches after the scan-completion wake; given every stamp matches the facts, it is not re-enriched; given a malformed hash BLOB or missing hash signature, it is stale; given a metadata-current card asset with no rendering or digest, the metadata-only claim returns nothing.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/CatalogModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/AssetMetadata.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/EnrichmentClaimGateway.swift`

- **ENR-008** Otos shall eagerly request every dataless media file that owes any product, its digest alone included (ENR-034), for background enrichment through `MaterializationCoordinator`, accepting the disk growth macOS has not yet evicted. [verified]
  - If a background- or device-priority download would leave its volume below `FreeSpaceFloor` (5 GB, checked against its volume's `FreeSpaceLedger`, which counts the downloads already committed there without counting twice the ones the figure shows, and the figure the app's `VolumeCapacityHold` gives: one CacheDelete calculation per volume per 60 s, lowered at once by every byte the volume's free count loses), then it shall not be started and shall finish `insufficientLocalSpace`.
  - A cloud row shall then keep its claim, held by the enrichment scheduler (not outstanding, so it holds back no other row), and be offered again after the 60 s space recheck, and interactive and command downloads shall never wait for the floor.
  - Interactive work shall outrank command work (user-commanded, off-screen: Move to Trash downloads), which outranks device work, which outranks background work.
  - Command work shall run under the bounded background persistence (120 s, fail-fast on provider refusal), be exempt from the background per-root cap, and like interactive stop its transfer when its last waiter leaves.
  - Background work shall use at most 15 global slots and one active transfer per root, while all priorities share the 2 GiB byte budget and two-large-video cap.
  - If a dataless file's provider definitively refuses with `ETIMEDOUT` (domain-qualified `ETIMEDOUT`) twice consecutively on one transfer, then that transfer shall short-circuit in ~2s (`k 2` probes) with outcome `providerUnavailable` distinct from `timedOut`.
  - Two consecutive `providerUnavailable` on one root at device/background priority shall open a 60s per-root `providerBreakerOpen` suppression so the next background candidates on that root are not dispatched (preventing a 32×2s storm behind `backgroundPerRootCap=1`).
  - The breaker shall never suppress interactive demand; an interactive success shall close an open breaker for that root, and an interactive failure shall neither open nor extend one.
  Example: Given eager work from one root, only one physical background transfer is active; given interactive demand, no new lower-priority transfer starts until interactive demand drains; given a background transfer holding a root's cap and queued background work, a command request on that root is admitted next; given a stalled provider request that never returns `ETIMEDOUT`, the one-URL mechanism re-requests once per second within 120 seconds; given a provider that returns `ETIMEDOUT` twice on one file, that transfer finishes in ~2s with `providerUnavailable` and the second `providerUnavailable` on that root suppresses further background dispatch on that root for 60s with `reason=providerBreakerOpen`; given a volume 1,000 bytes above the floor, a 2,000-byte background or device download is not started while a 500-byte background, an interactive and a command download are, and a volume that reports no capacity admits; given a download that found its file already local while another is in flight, its bytes are given back to the ledger and the next download is admitted on them; given a figure that rises while downloads are in flight, the freed space is admitted; given a command whose provider refuses twice, it ends `providerUnavailable` (bounded persistence); given the last waiter of an interactive or a command transfer leaves, the transfer ends and its accounting returns to zero; given a held cloud download, its claim is released and the ledger offers it again after the recheck.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/MaterializationCoordinator.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MaterializationAdmissionPolicy.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinator.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentSchedule.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/FileMaterialization.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/MediaOperationModels.swift`

- **ENR-009** `EnrichmentCoordinator` shall own only cursorless catalog claiming, a bounded 32-item working window per root (`EnrichmentClaimRoom`), eight-worker local enrichment admission, one claim-lifecycle state per identity, and batched result application; `MaterializationCoordinator` shall own the separate bounded 16-transfer physical scheduler. [verified]
  - Enrichment may hand off at most 16 materialization requests globally and two per root (one admitted background transfer plus one look-ahead request).
  - A claim shall hold while handed-off work waits, with no expiry, until released or until the process ends, and loading the catalog shall clear every claim the last process left.
  - If background materialization fails, then it shall retry in-window through a capped exponential schedule and, once that schedule is exhausted, release its claim-window slot and be held, with its claim, for the pacing (900 s) before a fresh set of retries.
  - A claimed row the hand-off has no slot for — whether routed from a claim or found dataless by its read — shall be held the same way and handed off when a slot frees.
  - Every claimed demand waiting for a slot or an in-window retry shall take a place in its root's 32-item claim window as queued work does, so the active demands stay within the roots' windows and the hand-off however many rows wait, and a root whose claims wait on transfers that do not start (held by the machine's memory, the floor or a full hand-off) fills only its own window, never another root's.
  - A demand waiting out the pacing or the free-space recheck shall be parked, read by no pass, and come back its root's room at a time once its deadline passes.
  - Every held demand shall also end when its row is deleted, moved to the Trash or left awaiting presence, when its root is removed or stops being enrichable (its claim given back), or, for a pacing hold, when its source size changes, and a transfer running when its row or root goes shall end withdrawn, neither retried nor held, so no row a full hand-off, the free-space floor or a failing download holds is claimed and given back to rediscover it.
  - A background claim that found nothing shall not be asked again until a Store change, a claim given back or room for a root whose full window kept it from being asked moves its inputs (a change that names no row, a root's order or activity, moves none) (an empty claim is a write transaction).
  - A row whose source sends the original only for the user's work shall be paced through a bounded process-local ledger (identity, earliest reclaim time, attempt count, source-size fragment of the `(size, mtime)` signature) that holds no work payload and resets on success, source-signature change, or process restart.
  - Cancellation shall release a claim without consuming a retry attempt.
  - The video cap (two videos enriching at once) shall hold back background videos only: a video on screen is admitted whatever background videos are reading.
  - Admission diagnostics shall aggregate rejected candidates by reason and emit only on full state change plus one unblocked transition, truthfully reporting a blocked state — never a spurious unblocked — while effective interactive demand defers device/background dispatch.
  - Root drain shall drop work claimed but not started and wait, without a time limit, only for work this coordinator started — enrichment, and every download it handed to the shared coordinator, which counts as running until its result returns — never for unrelated interactive preview traffic under the same root.
  Example: Given 25,000 dataless assets whose provider transfers are held, the enrichment working window stays at or below 32 for their root, its shared-materializer handoff stays at or below 16 globally and two per root, and the physical scheduler keeps one same-root background transfer active; given two background videos enriching, a video on screen is still admitted; given blocked video enrichment, later admissible still work dispatches and unchanged wakes do not repeat the blocked diagnostic; given success, the old claim releases, the identity re-enters through `freshly`, and any ledger entry clears; given a permanently-failing background population, each asset releases its claim-window slot after the in-window retry schedule exhausts and is held, with its claim, for the pacing, so a fresh candidate is still claimable and none is claimed again meanwhile; given a row deleted, or a library removed, while its demand is held for the pacing, no transfer is spent on it once the pacing passes; given a row found dataless at the outstanding limit, it is held, never one more outstanding; given 3,000 rows needing their bytes and transfers that never return, held and outstanding demands stay within the window and the hand-off and a pass with the window full claims nothing; given a cloud library whose held downloads fill its window, a card attached after them has every row claimed and dispatched without any on screen; given a root whose window is full, a claim takes none of its rows and gives the others their own room; given a claim that found nothing, it is asked again only once a root whose window was full has room; given a cancelled (non-shutdown) materialization, the claim releases without scheduling a retry or consuming ledger pacing; given a pending or effectively in-flight interactive waiter defers lower-priority dispatch, the blocked diagnostic is recorded truthfully and does not clear to unblocked while that demand holds; given an eject drain with only unrelated interactive preview traffic active under the root, the drain returns without waiting for it; given running enrichment and queued claims under the root, the drain drops the queued claims and returns when the running enrichment finishes; given shutdown, materializer waiters cancel promptly and all claims release.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinator.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinatorWorkSelection.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinatorCompletion.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentSchedule.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentClaimRoom.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentAdmissionPolicy.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MaterializationCoordinator.swift`

- **ENR-010** Enrichment shall do no media I/O or thumbnail decoding on the MainActor (ADR 0016). [verified]
  - The coordinator and pools shall run off-main.
  - The controller and loupe/compare pane thumbnail fallback shall load and decode store misses off-main.
  - Preheating shall warm decoded thumbnails off-main into tile store.
  - Store hits shall paint synchronously from immutable decoded images, while video-badge formatting stays small and synchronous in tile configuration from a store-cached duration string.
  - Each decoded thumbnail shall be held once: the tile's layer shows the very image the tile store holds, decoded by `DisplayImage` at its stored size, with no image drawn from it and no Core Animation display copy (ADR 0016).
  Example: Given a cold enrichment sweep, no media bytes or store-miss thumbnail decode run on the MainActor; given a tile configures, duration/fallback badge text can update synchronously without restarting image work and filename/date formatting does not run in the tile path; given tile store already holds the incoming image for that asset key, the tile assigns that immutable image synchronously; given the store misses, thumbnail loading/decoding runs in a detached task that applies the decoded image into tile store on Main under a stale-key guard; given preheat warms a thumbnail, decode runs off-main and partially cancelled AppKit IDs are filtered before merge/warm; given tile loads and store hits run, no media I/O or store-miss decode hops onto the MainActor.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinator.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MediaEnricher.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/MediaAssetTileView.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/ThumbnailStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Support/DisplayImage.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/TileContentStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/MediaGridBridge.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/PanePresentation.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModel.swift`

- **ENR-011** Enrichment shall implement connected-source enrichment priority at claim/staging selection time without a durable second queue. [verified]
  - Available removable roots shall be claimed before fixed roots, and the fixed roots shall share what is left of each claim, a share each and then the room a root did not fill to the others in turn, each root only into its own claim window (ENR-009), so a root whose rows wait on downloads (a cloud library's videos) never holds back one whose rows are readable now (an Immich account's, read in ranges, or a card attached while the library's downloads are held), neither within one claim nor across the passes its held downloads span.
  - MTP eager staging shall submit still/image items before videos, then capture date, then path, at shared materializer device priority, and **at most one device transfer runs at a time**.
  - ImageCaptureCore funnels every request through one serial `ICDeviceCommandQueue`, so concurrent device requests are not parallelism — they queue inside a framework Otos cannot observe, time, or cancel, and they cost throughput.
  - The cap shall be per-transport, never global, so cloud and local materialization stay concurrent; and it shall be enforced at the device session as well as in admission, because import copies call the session directly and never pass through the materializer.
  - When the pass ends, readiness deferrals that have elapsed shall be dropped, and every wait the scheduler holds — download retries, ledger pacing, readiness rechecks, the catalog-write retry — shall wake it through one deadline, the earliest still ahead of the instant the pass started from, so a wait no pass reached can never make the scheduler wake itself at zero delay.
  - A device that is proven to answer ranged reads shall **not be staged for enrichment at all**: its rows are enriched from the container's own index, a few kilobytes per item, so transferring them first would move the whole card to read a date that sits a few kilobytes into each file.
  - Capability shall be measured once per attach — one 12-byte read whose magic must identify a family consistent with the item's extension — because `GetPartialObject` is optional in MTP and devices that advertise it are documented failing it.
  - If a device fails that probe, then it shall stage exactly as before: every offered seed is staged, seeds beyond the outstanding-materialization limit are held in memory and admitted as each download completes, never discarded.
  - Staging shall also remain the fallback for a single item a ranged read cannot serve — an unindexable container, a repeatedly failing read, or a loupe open — through the same per-item materialization path.
  - A ranged read that fails shall be transient, never a definitive absence of a capture date, so a detached camera is retried rather than named from its filesystem mtime, and a read that never answers times out rather than parking its thread.
  - A row enriched this way shall take its grid thumbnail from the container's embedded preview when one is declared at grid size or larger — the only point at which it can, since a stamped row is never re-claimed and ImageCaptureCore returns only a 160×120 embedded EXIF thumb; where no usable preview exists the camera's provisional thumbnail is left in place rather than replaced.
  - Viewport priority may still preempt, bounded by viewport size.
  - Root drain/eject shall wait for enrichment-local work, which includes every request already handed to the shared materializer until its result returns.
  Example: Given fixed and removable fallback candidates are both eligible, Store returns the removable-source candidate before a newer fixed-library candidate; given two fixed libraries owing work, a window of two holds one row of each and a wider one every row left; given MTP staging seeds include stills and videos, the coordinator submits still/image requests first and video requests last without enabling cloud eager materialization; given a camera offers more items than the outstanding limit, every seed is either an outstanding demand or queued for one and none is discarded, and completing downloads admit the queued remainder; given low local space, device staging pauses as a distinct retryable condition; given a source is ejected, the drain waits for downloads already handed to the shared materializer, and camera staging is deleted.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinator.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinatorWorkSelection.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentSchedule.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MaterializationCoordinator.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/DeviceStagingRegistry.swift`

- **ENR-012** Enrichment shall persist a capture-wall naming fact (capture_wall_compact / capture_wall_precision) that is MediaMetadata's `CaptureTimestamps.captureWallClock` asked of the stored records — local-authority embedded timestamps only (precision localFloating or localWithOffset), in `wallClockPrecedence` (original → digitized → quickTimeCreation → quickTimeContentCreate → riffRecording; the audio-only roles after it are never stored, MED-004; ADR 0039), with UTC-anchored candidates excluded. [verified]
  - On migration, already-enriched rows shall be backfilled from metadata_extra_json.
  Example: Given an asset with floating EXIF DateTimeOriginal 2026-04-26 14:57:35, enrichment stores compact 20260426145735 with precision localFloating; given only absolute/UTC candidates, both columns stay NULL and naming later falls back to source mtime; given a pre-migration enriched row with metadata JSON, opening the catalog backfills the wall columns without re-enrichment.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/CaptureWallClock.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStorePersistence.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MediaEnricher.swift`

- **ENR-013** Ordinary enrichment shall hash each materialized image/video primary with SHA-1 (CryptoKit `Insecure.SHA1`, ADR 0018) through the existing eight-worker lifecycle and atomically persist its 20 bytes plus the hashed source size/mtime. [verified]
  - The digest shall be current while those stamps name the row's current size and mtime (`ContentDigestSQL.isCurrent`).
  - Each claim shall carry one `EnrichmentScope` the Store decides — the set of the row's stale products the claim covers — and one read shall make all of them: a fixed library's row is read for its stale metadata, rendering and digest together; a row whose only stale product is its digest gets the digest alone, with nothing parsed or rendered again and no planning revision moved, downloaded for it when its original is cloud-only (ENR-034); a `.removableVolume` card is read for metadata first, with a provisional QuickLook thumbnail, and its rows are offered for their renderings and digests together only once no row of that card needs metadata — the card is developed and checksummed while it sits idle, from local bytes, downloading nothing.
  - A camera (`.deviceMTP`) row shall never be hashed or rendered in place.
  - A digest shall be written only while the row's size and mtime are the ones it was read at.
  - If the hash overlay is missing, malformed, or signature-mismatched, then the library or card asset shall be stale; hashing never creates a second queue, materialization lifecycle, identity, sidecar, or UI state.
  - The digest's bytes, and a still's original read for its rendering, shall be read through `FileBytes` (one `read(2)` buffer in a `ScratchBuffer`; a whole read into a mapped region its `Data` owns), so hashing or rendering a file adds one buffer to the footprint and leaves nothing behind, whatever the file's size (`docs/research/2026-11-16-autoreleased-chunks-in-the-footprint.md`).
  Example: Given a materialized asset on a library, one enrichment result contains metadata, a rendering, and a valid hash; given a card, its rows are enriched for metadata with a provisional thumbnail first and for renderings and digests in a second pass; given a missing or malformed hash or changed size/mtime, the asset is claimed again; given a stale result, none of its overlay including the digest is applied. Given a hash-only enrichment of a 300 MB local file in a task-group child, the footprint grows by at most 8 MB; given enrichment started on the owner-shaped catalog's backlog, it holds at most its window and hand-off and adds at most 40 MB.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/ContentHasher.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MediaEnricher.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStorePersistence.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/EnrichmentClaimGateway.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/CatalogModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/AssetMetadata.swift`, `OtosKit/Support/FileBytes.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/ImageRenderer.swift`

- **ENR-014** `EnrichmentCoordinator` shall route Store claims by where each original is, as the shared materializer answers it (`MaterializationRequesting.locality(of:)`, ADR 0046 §4; never the file's `SF_DATALESS` flag read directly), before ready-enrichment admission, after routing rows a source serves in ranges to those reads. [verified]
  - Ready claims, materialization demands/in-flight IDs, and pending-apply claims shall have independent capacities.
  - A fetchable candidate — a dataless folder file, or a staged source's original not yet in its staging folder — shall become a bounded materialization demand without occupying a ready-enrichment slot.
  - An original its source sends only for the user's work (`fetchableForUserWork`, an Immich account's) shall never be demanded: the row is released to the pacing ledger, from routing and from an enrichment that found no local file alike.
  - A completed enrichment shall release ready capacity immediately while retaining its SQLite claim until the batched apply commits.
  - An unreachable original shall use an owned retry signal.
  Example: Given a ready candidate is dataless, it is handed to `MaterializationCoordinator` and later re-enters through `freshly`; given an apply result is pending, another ready candidate can be admitted while the pending identity retains its Store lease; given the original is unreachable, the coordinator retries without byte-reading; given a camera row whose original is not in its staging folder, it is demanded, never handed to the enricher; given a dataless folder file, its locality is fetchable, not local; given a materialization-demand population fills its handoff cap, additional candidates are released rather than over-admitted.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinator.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinatorWorkSelection.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinatorCompletion.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentReadinessRouter.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MaterializationCoordinator.swift`

- **ENR-015** MediaEnricher.live shall use the injectable live readiness boundary independently before each byte-reading phase: SHA-1 hashing, media metadata parsing, and Quick Look thumbnail generation. [verified]
  - If a phase gate fails, then MediaEnricher.live shall return .notMaterialized without persisting readiness or invoking a later phase.
  - The .removableVolume path that skips hashing (ADR 0018) shall use the metadata-parse and thumbnail gates only.
  Example: Given a file is materialized at the first gate but becomes dataless after hashing, metadata and thumbnail readers are not invoked; given it becomes dataless after metadata, Quick Look is not invoked; given it remains materialized on a hash-required root, exactly three gates and one hash/metadata/thumbnail pass run; given it remains materialized on a removable-volume root, exactly two gates and one metadata/thumbnail pass run.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/MediaEnricher.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentReadinessRouter.swift`

- **ENR-016** Every still shall be rendered once by `ImageRenderer` into a stored 2560 px 8-bit Display P3 HEIC preview (`media_asset.preview_path`, ADR 0035) and the 256 px grid thumbnail drawn from it, stamped together as the row's rendering (ADR 0009). [verified]
  - A RAW shall be developed by `CIRAWFilter` at the newest decoder the file supports (RAW9, else RAW8) one at a time per lane, and any other still decoded by Image I/O with orientation applied, as an 8-bit Display P3 bitmap on a background or interactive lane.
  - Image I/O decodes shall run up to four at once on the background lane and two on the interactive lane, both lanes admitted by one budget of the memory each render holds, no more than four HEIC previews together, a render the user waits on granted at once with nothing of enrichment's starting beside it, and a still's preview and thumbnail encoded and staged inside its admission.
  - When a render is asked for that is already asked for on either lane, the ask shall join it, so the pane opening the photo enrichment is rendering is handed that render, and an ask cancelled before its render begins withdraws it before a byte is read.
  - A card's metadata pass shall render nothing: its rows get QuickLook's thumbnail, unstamped, until its rendering pass.
  - An import shall never render, and a copy settled without a rendering shall be rendered by enrichment first after it lands (ARC-053).
  - The preview shall live beside the thumbnail and follow its lifecycle: staged, promoted only after the row validates, superseded files deleted, purged with the row or root, moved with a root's path, and copied by import reuse.
  - Videos shall get no preview.
  - The `v3_media_asset_preview_path` migration cleared library stills' stamps so they re-enriched once; `v13_product_stamps` gives card and camera stills and RAWs without a preview no rendering stamp, so they are rendered again.
  - Both products shall be rendered with the photo's saved edit recipe (ADR 0036); a result records the edit revision it read and is refused when a newer save has replaced it, and an accepted one records that revision as rendered.
  - At launch, and when the edit panel cannot render a save, a photo whose rendered revision lags its edit revision shall lose its rendering stamp so enrichment renders it again; reused products whose edit lags shall be re-rendered for the copy the same way.
  Example: Given a HEIC still, enrichment stages a preview no larger than 2560 px and a thumbnail no larger than 256 px from it; given a card's metadata pass, a still gets a provisional thumbnail and no rendering, and an edited one no provisional thumbnail; given a rendering scope, the still is developed without its metadata being read again; given a video, no preview; given an accepted result with both, both are promoted, the tile detail carries both paths, and removing the library deletes both files; given the v3 migration, library stills lose their stamps while videos and card rows keep theirs; given an 8-bit test image, the staged preview is an 8-bit Display P3 HEIC.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/ImageRenderer.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/DecodeBudget.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MediaEnricher.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/ThumbnailStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStorePersistence.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSchema.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStoreImportJournal.swift`

- **ENR-017** While an import copies from a `.removableVolume` source, enrichment shall not read that card — neither its metadata pass nor its rendering-and-digest pass (ENR-013). [verified]
  - The Store shall stop offering it (`RootRuntimeState.isEnrichable`) and release the claims it handed out, the enrichment coordinator shall drop work it queued there, and work already reading shall finish.
  - `MediaOperationCoordinator` shall bracket every run with `beginImportSourceRead`/`endImportSourceRead`, and the bracket shall close however the run ends.
  - The card's current products shall be reused by the copies and the import shall make the rest from each published copy (ARC-053), so card reads during the copy would only compete with the copier for the card reader.
  - A camera shall keep enriching during its import, because the import copies its enrichment staging (ARC-049).
  Example: Given an import copying from a card, the card is offered no priority or background claim while the fixed library still is; given queued card work when the import begins, it is dropped and never starts, while the running item finishes; given the run fails or ends, the card is offered again and its rows are claimable.

- **ENR-018** Each enrichment pass shall claim the rows the user is looking at (the viewport) and rows a download just made local (the `freshly` hand-off) before it drains queued work, and outside the claim window, so a free slot goes to a visible row ahead of queued background claims. [verified]
  - The window shall bound look-ahead; the priority list's own length shall bound priority claims.
  - A priority row the Store answered owes nothing shall not be asked of again until a Store change names it or names no rows, or the video-faces admission moves (`PriorityAnswers`), so a pass woken by anything else reads none of the rows on screen.
  Example: Given a claim window full of background claims and one running item, when a row not yet claimed becomes visible and the running item finishes, the visible row starts next rather than a queued background row.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinatorWorkSelection.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`

- **ENR-021** `RenderSignatureRefresher` shall be the one producer of render signatures. [verified]
  - Each visible still in a fixed library with a stored thumbnail shall get a difference hash and 32 × 32 grayscale render read from that thumbnail in Application Support — never from the original, so nothing is downloaded — stored only while it is the thumbnail it was read from: the hash on the row, the gray in `render_signature` (v71).
  - The signature shall be written only while the row's thumbnail identity (`StoredThumbnailSQL.identity`, which for a source-drawn thumbnail includes its source-products stamp) is still the one read, and cleared with the gray by the write that changes that identity (`media_asset_render_signature_stale`).
  - A re-rendered thumbnail, or a changed account file's new source images at the same path, shall make it owed, and a thumbnail that does not decode shall be recorded so it is not retried until it changes.
  - `RenderSignatureRefresher` shall run one loop woken by a Store change of a kind its walk reads (ARC-018), never by its own signature writes.
  - A wake shall drain in one walk over each root's key (`CandidateWalk`, ADR 0009) of an index holding exactly the stale stills (v65), so a settled library reads none.
  - MediaMetadata's capture sub-seconds and burst identifier shall be stored as reported; from 0.9.2 the library's family revisions announce new fields, so `MetadataRefresher` backfills them from local bytes only, and HEIF, PNG and WebP records carry the sub-seconds of their embedded Exif (ADR 0039).
  Example: Given a candidate whose thumbnail was replaced since it was read, nothing is written; given its current source, the signature is written and the row is no longer offered; given a record's sub-seconds, they round-trip through the stored record; given an account still's source images replaced for a changed file, and then Otos's rendering, it is offered again after each and its pipeline follows.

- **ENR-019** Enrichment and metadata refresh shall store the content identifier MediaMetadata reports (`contentIdentifier`, 0.8.2) as reported, in `AssetMetadata` and `media_asset.content_identifier`, through `writeMetadataProjection`. [verified]
  - When a write changes it, the write shall report a structural change, because it changes which movies a Live Photo hides.
  - Migration v9 adds the column and the `parent_path` the pair rule groups by, and clears the extraction revision of HEIF, JPEG and ISO BMFF records so `MetadataRefresher` re-reads them from local bytes (ADR 0039, ADR 0041).
  Example: Given a movie read before its still, it shows until the still's identifier is written, which reports `.catalogChanged`; given a v8 catalog, v9 leaves HEIF, JPEG and ISO BMFF records stale and a TIFF record current.

- **ENR-020** A metadata refresh pass shall hand to enrichment, rather than skip, a library row whose original is not local and whose record may be half of a Live Photo — a HEIF, JPEG or ISO BMFF record read before the content identifier (NULL revision) and made by Apple (`camera_make = 'Apple'`) — by clearing its enrichment signature only while the file and record are unchanged (`CatalogStore.requestEnrichmentDownload`). [verified]
  - The rendering stamp is part of what the stored thumbnail is, so the write shall announce an image change naming the rows it handed over (with `.faceTiles` for a photo holding a face and `.duplicateFacts` for one Duplicates holds), and the render signature, preview face, search and description owners shall hear it.
  - Enrichment shall then download it once and write metadata, checksum, thumbnail and preview from that one read; any other cloud-only stale record shall be skipped (ADR 0039, ADR 0041).
  Example: Given a cloud-only Apple HEIC and a cloud-only Canon HEIC with old records, the pass requests one download and skips one; enrichment materializes the Apple file once, reads it once with hashing on, and stores its thumbnail and identifier; the Canon row is untouched.

- **ENR-022** A still's faces shall be read by `FaceAnalyzer` (ADR 0047) from the image `PreviewFaceReader` hands it, the still's stored preview decoded upright (ENR-026), never from its original. [verified]
  - The analysis shall use Vision landmarks and capture quality at revision 3, quality paired to its face by `uuid`.
  - A face shall require quality ≥ 0.28, the preview's equivalent of the original's 0.3.
  - A face shall require box height × alignment scale 50–150.
  - A face shall require a person seen around the face: Vision's image classifier at revision 2, on a square twice the face box kept inside the photo, gives `people`, `adult`, `child`, `baby`, `teen` or `crowd` a confidence ≥ 0.2.
  - The analysis shall use an opaque 112 px crop; AdaFace IR-101 with 8-bit weights on the CPU; analysis revision 3.
  - Each embedding shall be stored as 512 little-endian Float32 values.
  - A still's faces shall not be an enrichment product: no enrichment claim owes them and the enricher reads none, so no still's original is downloaded or decoded for faces (a library video's are, ENR-031).
  - The `v24_raw_faces_reread` migration cleared the faces stamp of every RAW stamped with no faces.
  - The `v29_faces_person_admission` migration clears the faces stamp of every still with a face read under an earlier revision, while a still stamped with no face keeps its stamp.
  - The `v30_faces_from_preview` migration drops the library claim's index pair, which owed faces, and clears every faces stamp, so every still is read again from its preview.
  Example: A library still whose metadata, rendering and digest are current is not claimed under any claim, whether or not its faces were read; three public-domain portraits each read as one admitted face, none turned away for want of a person, one person twelve years apart at or above the suggestion threshold and another person below it less the margin; the classifier sees a person around a portrait's face and none around the same box over a pattern; given the v24 migration, a RAW stamped without faces owes faces again while a RAW with faces, a JPEG and an unread RAW are unchanged; given the v29 migration, a still with a revision-1 face owes faces again while a still stamped with none, one with a revision-2 face and an unread one are unchanged; given the v30 migration, no still keeps a faces stamp, its faces stay, and the library claim's indexes are gone; a card's second pass and a fixed library each walk their own claim's index pair.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/AssetMetadata.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/CatalogModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/EnrichmentClaimGateway.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MediaEnricher.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/FaceAnalyzer.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/FaceAlignment.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/FaceEmbeddingModel.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSchema.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStorePersistence.swift`

- **ENR-023** A still's faces shall be stored in the catalog's `face` table as overlay on its asset — the box and five alignment points as fractions of the upright photo, the capture quality and the analysis revision — and its 512-value embedding as little-endian Float32 in its own `face_embedding` row (v66), numbered by a reading's `id` that is never given twice. [verified]
  - The faces shall be written with the faces stamp, the source stamp of the preview it was read from (ENR-026), only while the row still has that preview, and with the source the faces were read from (`faces_read_source_size`/`_mtime`, v47), which a migration that clears the stamp to owe a re-read leaves as it was.
  - A new reading shall replace the asset's faces and carry the user's acts and the seeds (a person given, a removed name, a key photo, rejections, a seed, and on a reading of the same source "not a face") to the new face each matches — a still's by the overlap of their boxes, a video's by likeness (both recognisable, cosine ≥ 0.434), since a video's boxes belong to different frames.
  - A reading of the same source as the faces were read from shall keep, as they were read, the faces the user named, removed the name of, rejected a person for or marked not a face that it does not find.
  - A reading of a changed file shall keep none of them, also when a migration had cleared the stamp.
  - A stored face row no reading writes (an embedding not of unit length, an assignment no case holds) shall be dropped by the next reading.
  - A move shall carry them and the asset's proven deletion shall purge them (`ON UPDATE`/`ON DELETE CASCADE`), the vector rows with their faces.
  - v66 moves every stored embedding to its own row, keeping each face's person, word, likeness, key photo and rejections.
  - Nothing shall be written into the user's folders.
  Example: Two faces applied then one leave one; a re-reading of the same source that does not find a named face and finds a face marked not a face keeps the named face as read and the mark on the found face, and one that finds neither keeps both, while a reading of the changed file keeps only the named face it finds again; with the stamp cleared, a re-read of the same source keeps the mark and the named face it does not find, and one of a file changed meanwhile keeps neither; a video's named face moves to the face of the same person, not the face now in its box; given v47, a photo's faces record their stamp's source, or the source it would name now where it was cleared; a reading of a preview of other bytes stores none and leaves the row owing faces; a rename seen as file events moves the faces to the new path; a removed file's faces are gone; given v66, each face's vector is its own row in face order, the face row keeps the rest, a move carries the vectors, a deletion takes them and a new one is numbered above every earlier one.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSchema.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStorePersistence.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/FaceStorage.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`

- **ENR-024** Who each face is shall be catalog overlay (ADR 0047). [verified]
  - The overlay shall hold people (a name, optional and one person's; a birthday, optional, either a date or only a year, which counts from 1 January, stored as `YYYY-MM-DD` or `YYYY`; anything else is refused; hidden and favourite marks), and on each face its person and whose word that is — none, Otos's guess, a seed, the user's, or held (the user removed the name) — its likeness, whether it is its person's one key photo, "not this person" rejections and "not a face".
  - The store shall refuse giving a person two faces of one photo or a face in a photo the user already gave them another face of (Otos's guess there makes way), a face from before their birthday, a name another person has, and a birthday after a photo the user gave the person — on Set Birthday and on a merge alike, one rule over both merged people against the merged birthday (Otos's guesses and seeds from before it lose the person).
  - A person shall never be merged into themself (`mergeIntoSelf`).
  - A hidden person shall merge as anyone does.
  - A merge shall keep the target's name, birthday and key photo, taking the other's where it has none, be hidden if either was, and carry rejections to the merged person, leaving no face rejected for its own person (the user gave it one of the two, and the merge says the two are one).
  - A photo both merged people are in shall never refuse a merge: every guess of the person merged away becomes the merged person's seed, and there the face most like the merged person by assignment's likeness to both people's other defining faces stays theirs, on a tie the target's, and the others are held, in the merge's one undoable act, so the next assignment groups none of the merged faces into a new person.
  - Make Key Photo shall make the face its person's on the user's word, so a re-read carries it and assignment never moves it; existing key photos on a guess or a seed become the user's (v49).
  - Every act shall be one transaction whose record undoes it exactly, and a reference model of the acts (`PeopleReferenceModelTests`) predicts each act's catalog state, refusals included, and holds the rules no one act owns after every step of random walks of acts, undos, redos, new readings and assignment passes.
  - Assignment's writes shall land only where a face is still Otos's to decide, and delete a person Otos found once they have no face and nothing the user said.
  - A new reading of a photo shall carry each face's person when it was the user's word or a seed, a held face, its rejections and the key photo (`is_key_photo`) to the new face it matches, and "not a face" on a reading of the same source (ENR-023); Otos's guesses are not carried.
  - Existing catalogs keep a confirmed face as the user's and an ignored face as held (v26); a stored birthday in another spelling of a valid date is rewritten in its form, and one naming no date is cleared and logged (v48).
  - Deleting a person shall open their faces in the same statement (v82 trigger; v82 opens what earlier deletes left), so no row names no one under a guess, a seed or the user's word.
  - The People read shall leave out whole a photo with a face row no reading writes (no vector row, an assignment no case holds, no person under a guess, a seed or the user's word), log it and clear its faces stamp so it is read again.
  - A vector whose bytes hold no embedding shall be found as People reads the vectors, its face left out of assignment and its photo owed again the same way.
  - A person row with a malformed birthday shall still fail the read.
  - Acts and assignment writes shall be catalog changes, and a new reading of a photo holding a person's gallery cover shall name the gallery among its changes, since replacing the face rows clears the cover until People writes it again.
  Example: Given Sam given a face and that face's name removed, it is held with Sam rejected; given Otos's guess for Sam in a photo and the user's face of her there, the guess is gone; given a plan for a face the user moved meanwhile, the user's word stays; given a re-read photo, the user's face, the held face and the key photo carry and the guess does not, and a key photo chosen on a guess is the user's face and carries; given a merge into a person born after the other's seed, the seed loses its person; given two people the user gave a face of one photo merged, the face more like their other photos stays and the other is open, and on a tie the target's stays; given a malformed embedding, its photo is left out, owes faces again and a new reading restores it; given v48, `1985-6-7` reads `1985-06-07`, `+985` `0985`, and `0000` and `2021-02-30` none; given v49, a guessed key photo is the user's; given each act undone, the catalog is as before, and redone as after; given a face the user said is not Sam and then gave Ian, merging Sam into Ian leaves it Ian's with no rejection, while a held face that is not Sam becomes not Ian; given a person merged into themself, the act is refused and nothing changes; given a hidden person merged with a visible one either way, the merged person is hidden; given v82, a seed left with no person is open, a held face stays held, and deleting a person opens their faces and clears the key photo; given six seeded random walks, every act matches the model and no step breaks a rule. A merged unnamed person whose only seeds are in photos the target is already in leaves no new person after the next assignment: its guesses are the target's seeds and those seeds are held.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/PeopleModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSchema.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStorePersistence.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/FaceStorage.swift`

- **ENR-025** `FaceAssignmentPolicy` shall give every face its most likely person (ADR 0047). [verified]
  - From the anchors (the user's faces and the seeds), faces shall be given out strongest first, round by round, each compared with a person's faces — all of them when the person was 18 or older on the face's date, otherwise (and when the age is unknown) those within ± 2 years.
  - A face whose best person reaches 0.434 and beats the runner-up by 0.16 shall join them and be evidence for the next round, so a child is followed year by year.
  - A face within the margin shall go to its best person but be evidence for nobody.
  - A person shall never get a face of a photo they are already in, a face before their birthday, a face rejected for them, or a held face.
  - A seed whose photo's date moved before its person's birthday shall lapse, while the user's face there keeps its person.
  - Faces left over, linked at 0.65 (ten strongest links each, never two faces of one photo), shall become a new person when they span two or more days, and chaining shall run again from scratch; a face like no one, or of one day, shall stay unidentified.
  - A face whose cosine with `FaceRecognisability.direction` (the mean embedding of face crops shrunk to 8 px) reaches 0.2 shall be unrecognisable: it is never guessed, grouped, or compared with, counts toward no likeness or ranking, and, given a person on the user's word, still keeps that person out of the rest of its photo.
  - A person the user said nothing about (no name, birthday, favourite, hidden mark or key photo, and no face on their word) shall keep none of their unrecognisable seeds, and their others only while those span two or more days: otherwise the seeds lapse, are Otos's to decide again, and are released in the catalog only while still that person's seeds, and the person, left with no face, is deleted.
  - Run on its own output, `FaceAssignmentPolicy` shall change nothing.
  - Faces shall be compared at half precision (`FaceVector`), recognisability decided from the stored single-precision values.
  - The vectors shall be held in a mapped file of their own, never on the heap, which only its one non-copyable owner writes (`FaceVectorStore`; readers take the `FaceVectors` view), and read back bit for bit as rounded.
  - The vectors kept shall be those of every face the catalog stores, the faces People leaves out included (a photo in the Trash, a face marked not a face, a Live Photo's movie: `PeopleSnapshot.setAsideVectors`), so a face that comes back (Undo, Put Back) is compared again and the pass after it settles.
  - A page of them shall be written only while the volume keeps the free-space floor (ADR 0027), and at the floor People shall hold "no room": it assigns nothing, reads nothing on a change until its backoff wake finds room, and shows what the catalog holds.
  - Each face with a person shall get its likeness, the best similarity to that person's other faces within reach, written only when it appears, goes, or moves by more than 1e-5 (`FaceAssignmentPlan.likenessPrecision`), so the last-bit rounding Accelerate varies from process to process never makes an assignment of an unchanged catalog write, and People settles.
  - `FaceRanker` shall rank the people a face could be by the same reach and rules.
  Example: Given Sam's face in 2010 and faces like hers every 1.5 years after, all are hers; given a face as like Ian as Sam, it is Sam's but a face reached only through it is not; given two faces alike on two days and matching no one, a new person; given a face before Sam's birthday or rejected for her, never Sam; given Sam's seed and Otos's guess dated before her birthday, the seed lapses and no guess is hers, while her user's face there stays hers; given the vectorised chaining and the rules applied one comparison at a time on random faces, the same guesses; given the kernel and the dense kernel it replaced on generated libraries, the same guesses, new people and lapsed seeds and likenesses within 1e-5; given a bare person's two seeds from one day, both lapse and stay without a person, while a named person's, those of a person with a face on the user's word, and seeds spanning two days stand; a lapsed seed is planned out of its person, and the store releases it only while it is still that person's seed; given faces along the unrecognisable direction, alike to a person and to each other across days, no guess and no new person; given such a face on Sam's word, a face of its photo like Sam is not hers while the same face elsewhere is; given a bare person's unrecognisable seed, it lapses while two recognisable seeds of two days stand, and with one day of them all lapse; a real portrait's embedding is recognisable and the same crop shrunk to 8 px is not; given a copy of the vectors made before a page is appended, the copy reads what it was made with and the owner every vector; given the volume at the floor, a page is refused with what is held unchanged, People's pass holds with one backoff wake, changes rung meanwhile read nothing, the named face still shows, and the wake that finds room assigns; given a face marked not a face and the mark undone, the pass after Undo gives it its person and settles.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/FaceAssignmentPolicy.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/FaceRecognisability.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/PeopleAssignment.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/PeopleModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`

- **ENR-026** Every still in a library or an Immich account (`LibraryRootKind.readsFaces`) shall be read for faces from its stored preview, never its original (ADR 0047), by `PreviewFaceReader`, the one face reader: Otos's 2560 px rendering, or until there is one the source's own preview (Immich's). [verified]
  - A still shall owe faces while its faces stamp does not name the source stamp its preview was made from (the rendering's, else the source preview's); an edit is not a new source and is not read again.
  - The preview shows the photo's saved edit: it shall be read only while its edit is known (rendered unedited, or at the row's current edit revision), so a preview of an edit since replaced waits for its new rendering, and each face read from it shall be carried back through the edit's geometry (`EditGeometry`) onto the upright photo.
  - A write shall land only while the row still has the preview that was read, at the same source stamp and edit revision.
  - Only a preview that decoded and held no face shall be stamped with none.
  - If a preview's file cannot be read or its bytes do not decode, or its analysis failed, then the reader shall write nothing and the still shall stay owed, logged, and not be offered again for 900 s (`PreviewFaceRetries`) unless its preview changes, while the stills after it are read in the same drain, which pages after its last row.
  - If a model cannot be loaded (`FaceAnalysisError.modelUnavailable`, the model loaded before any Vision work and a failed load not kept), then the drain shall stop, and reading shall resume after 900 s, not at a catalog change.
  - The reader shall hold what a drain owes (`PreviewFaceDebt`): the whole walk at the start, after a change that names no rows and when the model's pacing ends, else the stills a change named and those whose pacing ended, each read by key (`PreviewFaceSQL.keyedCandidateSQL`), so a paced still is not read again before its deadline and a ring that brings neither reads nothing.
  - A page from which nothing was read shall open no write.
  - A write that stores any reading shall ring the catalog's change notifications (the grid and People read it, and the reader wakes again).
  - `start` shall make its loop and forwarder with no suspension, so a second call starts none.
  - A video shall never be offered to it (a library video's faces are ENR-031's).
  - The candidates and People's unchecked count shall be read from partial indexes over only the rows that owe faces (v50: stills owed faces, stills waiting for a preview, videos owing faces), so a catalog with nothing owed reads none.
  - People shall count as unchecked a still whose faces are owed or whose preview is still to come, not one rendered without a preview.
  Example: A listed Immich JPEG with stored source images is offered once and, once its faces are written, not again; relisting it at a new size with new source images offers it again; a MOV is never offered; a reading of the older preview is not written after the new one arrives; the reader hands the stored preview to the analyser and stores its faces; a library still is unchecked until its rendering, offered once its preview lands with the unedited recipe, not offered once read, and not again after an edit and its new rendering; a still edited before it is read is offered unedited until the rendering of its edit lands, then with that edit, and a reading of that preview is not written once a newer edit is rendered; a face read through a crop or a quarter turn lands where the edit took it from, and through a tone edit unchanged; with one still per page and the first still's analysis failing, the second is read in the same drain and the first is not read again when a third still's rendering rings the reader; a missing preview and an undecodable one both stay owed and never reach the analyser; a missing model stops the drain at its first still and a later ring reads nothing; two starts analyse a still once; a failed still waits out its pacing unless its preview changes, and a missing model holds every still until its pacing passes; the candidates and the unchecked count are planned on their partial indexes.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/PreviewFaceReader.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/EditGeometry.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/CatalogModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/FaceStorage.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModel.swift`

- **ENR-031** Enrichment shall read for faces a video of a library or an Immich account (`LibraryRootKind.readsVideoFaces`) (ADR 0047). [verified]
  - A library's video shall be read in the same read of its local bytes as its other products, under the library claim (`EnrichmentClaim.library`, its index pair made by `v35_video_faces` from frozen text).
  - An account's video shall be read in byte ranges of its original while its server answers, never a download, AVFoundation asking `RemoteVideoAsset` for each range and each read with the account's `RemoteRangeReader`, under the account claim (`EnrichmentClaim.account`: metadata and a video's faces, its index pair made by `v40_account_video_faces`); if the server does not answer a range, then the read shall be unanswered and the video left owed.
  - A video shall owe faces while its faces stamp does not name its current `(size, mtime)`; a still never does, and a card's and a camera's videos shall not be read.
  - `VideoFaceReader` shall decode the frames at 0, 10, 25, 50, 75 and 90% of the video upright at full resolution with no time tolerance — a sampled frame AVFoundation fails to decode (`decodeFailed`) read at the sync frame after it, else the one before (`VideoFaceReader.frame(at:)`, `syncFrameTime`), and none for a frame that is itself a sync frame or an account video's frame after a range went unanswered (`RemoteVideoAsset.failed`), the samples so read logged as `syncFrames=` on `op=videoFaces.remote` and `videoFacesSyncFrames=` in `op=enrichment.item` — one at a time, each read with `FaceAnalyzer` before the next is decoded and kept only when it holds faces, at the 2560 px it is staged at.
  - `VideoFaceReader` shall keep one face per person: faces taken sharpest first join the person they are most like at or above 0.434 who has no face in that frame; a face of the same frame, or one `FaceRecognisability` cannot tell anyone by, is a person of its own.
  - The kept faces' frames shall be staged as the images of one 2560 px HEIC (`media_asset.face_frames_path`, a derived image deleted and renamed with the row), each face storing its image (`face.frame_index`); its tile is cut from that image, and a face kept from an earlier reading of the same source has none.
  - A video with no face shall be stamped with none and its frames file deleted; a video with no video track shall be stamped with none.
  - If a duration or a frame cannot be read, or the analysis failed, then no faces shall be stamped, so an unread frame is never taken for a frame without faces: the read is its own outcome (`EnrichmentPurpose.facesUnread`), which writes every other product the claim covered (metadata, digest, rendering) and leaves only the faces owed, the row paced on the retry ledger for 900 s, and when the model could not be loaded every video's faces held for 900 s (`EnrichmentSchedule.holdVideoFaces`; the coordinator passes `videoFaces(at:)` to every Store claim, which then claims a library under `EnrichmentClaim.products` and an account under `.metadata` (`EnrichmentClaim.admitting`), so a video owing only its faces is never claimed and one owing more is claimed for the rest), never a transient read or an unreadable source.
  - An empty file shall be stamped with none.
  - A video AVFoundation says it cannot decode — its video track not decodable on this Mac (`AVAssetTrack.isDecodable`, asked before any frame: a VP9 movie, `VideoFaceReaderError.trackNotDecodable`), or by domain and code, its format not recognised, its bytes failing to parse, no decoder for them, no image at a sampled time, or `unknown` over the status a truncated AVI answers, -12344 (`VideoFaceReader.isUndecodable`) — shall be no failed read: its faces are stamped with none and `undecodable_by` names the frame reader's decoder (Otos's video revision and the macOS build, `VideoFaceReader.decoderIdentity`, logged `op=videoFaces.read phase=undecodable` for a local video and `op=videoFaces.remote phase=undecodable` for an account's, one outcome line per read with AVFoundation's error), so it neither owes faces nor counts as unchecked.
  - A video the reader decodes shall clear it; any other error, a failed decode among them, shall stay `facesUnread`.
  - At launch, every video another decoder could not read shall lose its faces stamp and its verdict, so its faces are read again, each kind judged by its own decoder, and the count released shall be logged (`op=catalog.start phase=undecodableReleased videos=`), sought through `idx_media_asset_undecodable`.
  - A Live Photo's movie shall be left out of People, its still holding its faces.
  - People shall count as unchecked a library's or an account's video owing faces.
  Example: Given three frames of two people, one face each, kept by the sharpest reading; two faces of one frame stay two; an unrecognisable face is never joined; the fixture video is read in six frames and its kept frame staged as one image; a video with no face stages nothing; a library video owes faces under the library claim only and a still never; the library claim keeps its indexes' text and walks them; a video's faces stored with two frames show as a video's, the second cut from image 1, the video no longer unchecked or owed, and its frames deleted with its library; a reading with no face deletes the frames; an account's fixture video served in ranges is read in six frames, a server that stops answering makes the read unanswered and one that answers gives its faces without metadata; an account's video owes faces under the account claim, ranged, and a camera's are never claimed for them; given v35 on a catalog before it, the frames columns and the library claim's indexes exist; a library video whose frames fail, or whose model is missing, is `facesUnread` with its metadata, digest and rendering and no faces; a missing model holds every claim of a video's faces until its backoff passes, a held library claiming its other products and a held account its metadata; while it holds, a wake writes no claim and no release for a library video owing only its faces, which an admitted wake claims once for them alone; a library video AVFoundation cannot parse is complete, its faces none and its verdict undecodable by `VideoFaceReader.decoderIdentity`, and once applied owes no faces and is not unchecked, the analyser never seeing a frame; a video whose track has no decoder (the fixture renamed VP9) is complete and undecodable, the analyser never seeing a frame, read locally or in ranges; a frame that fails to decode is read at its sync frame and heard, and with no sync frame, or another error, the read fails; a frame's sync frame is the next, else the last, and a sync frame has none; a video the reader decodes is decoded; only AVFoundation's format, parse, decoder and no-image codes, `unknown` over -12344 and a track with no decoder are a verdict, never another unknown error (over -17761 among them), a failed decode, a network error or a failed analysis; given launch, a video another decoder could not read loses its faces stamp and verdict, one this reader could not read keeps them, and a still's verdict by the video reader releases its rendering.
