- **ENR-027** Every asset with a stored thumbnail, in any available root, shall get a search embedding read from that thumbnail by the bundled SigLIP 2 model (ADR 0048), never from its original, newest capture first. [verified]
  - A pass shall find what is owed in one walk of the window's newest-first date order (`CandidateWalk`, ADR 0009), and a change that names its rows shall be reconciled by key and its rows (with their Live Photo partners) embedded and read first, and the backlog walk shall keep its place across passes, stopping at its next page for a change, so a photo made owed now is embedded before an older backlog without the library being read again.
  - Only the first pass and a change that names no rows shall reconcile the whole library and start the walk from the newest.
  - The embedding shall record the thumbnail and model revision it was read from and be written only if they are still current; a new thumbnail or revision makes it stale and owed again.
  - A thumbnail that does not decode shall be no reading — nothing is recorded, it stays owed, a pass pages past it to the rest and it is tried again after the backoff — and the asset's move or proven deletion shall carry or end it by cascade.
  - If a pass leaves an embedding or a text reading owed that it could not make (a model that would not load, a prediction or a write that failed), then it shall try again after a backoff, 30 s doubling to 15 minutes, with no catalog change.
  - While the model will not load or the vector folder's volume is at the free-space floor, no pass before that retry shall read the backlog or the rows a change named; those rows wait for the retry.
  - The tokenizer shall reproduce the trained one's ids, and the int8 text path shall reproduce the full-precision embeddings.
  - The index shall keep its vectors in one file pair in Application Support, mapped read-only (ADR 0048), as Float16 slots with each slot's loose labels kept as numbered names and their stored confidences, deciding the label gate exactly as the parsed labels would.
  - The catalog shall name the file and how many slots its embeddings commit (v70), in the same commit as the rows.
  - When a launch finds a file that is intact, of the model's revision and holds the committed slots, it shall map it and read no stored vector, cutting off any slot past the count, and any other file (missing, torn, short of the count) shall be rebuilt from the catalog's current embeddings in pages of 2,000, each its own read, while a query sees the empty index.
  - A later slot for an asset shall supersede its earlier one, and once dead slots reach a quarter of the live (and 1,024) the live ones shall be compacted into a new pair the catalog then names.
  - Memory shall take every file, append and dead slot in one step with the revision an answer carries (`SearchIndexMemory`), so a query after a compaction is answered from the new file's slots.
  - The free-space floor shall be asked before the catalog commits slots and never by the write after it.
  - If a write fails after its commit, then memory shall keep answering from its slots until a rebuild completes, and a rebuild shall remove the other files only once the catalog names its own, removing just its own when it fails.
  - The process shall claim the folder (`flock`) before it opens or builds a file, so one writer holds it, and a launch that opens the current file shall remove any other pair a crash left.
  - A reconcile shall seek only the embeddings memory lacks by key and append them, and keep a root's rows while that root is not read.
  Example: Given an image model that cannot load until after the first pass, both thumbnails are embedded with no catalog change; given an embedding written for a stale source, nothing is written; given the current one, the asset is no longer owed and is current; given a new thumbnail, it is owed again and its old vector no longer counts; given two thumbnails of a red and a blue picture, the index embeds both and ranks the red one first for `a red picture`; given a newer third whose thumbnail does not decode and pages of one, the other two are embedded and it stays owed. Given 300 generated vectors and labels appended in pages and opened again, the file holds the same assets in the same slots, the vectors rounded to Float16, scores within 1e-5 of the widened vectors' and the gate decides alike for every query; given an asset appended twice and one removed, compaction keeps only the live slots, bit for bit; given a file with more slots than committed, the extra are cut off, and given fewer, another name or a changed header byte, it does not open; given a second index over a catalog whose stored vectors were zeroed, it maps the file and still ranks red above blue, and given a commit the file never got or a file cut short, it rebuilds a new pair and removes the old; given a settled index and a new thumbnail for one photo, it is embedded again with no whole-library currency read, backlog walk or text currency read, and its new slot supersedes the old; given a query answered while a compaction awaits the catalog's naming of the new file, the next query over the same scope is answered from the new file's slots and carries a new revision; given the disk at the floor after the floor was asked and the catalog committed, the write goes ahead and the file stays current; given a rebuild the floor refuses, the old pair stays on the disk and a later pass builds it; given a pair a crash left beside the current one, the open removes it, and given a second index over a claimed folder, it opens nothing until the first shuts down.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/PhotoSearchIndex.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/SearchVectorFile.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/SearchEmbeddingModel.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/SearchTextTokenizer.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/SearchEmbeddingStorage.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSchema.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStorePersistence.swift`

- **ENR-028** Search shall read two more facts from each picture (ADR 0048): Vision's scene labels from the same stored thumbnail as the embedding, and the text Vision recognises in each still's stored preview. [verified]
  - The scene labels shall be stored with the embedding (`SearchEmbedding.revision` 3) both as the gate's loose reading (every label from 0.05, with its confidence) and as the labels that pass Vision's high-precision test (`hasMinimumRecall(0.01, forPrecision: 0.9)`), most confident first.
  - The text shall be recognised at the accurate level on the CPU (`search_text`, `SearchTextReading.revision` 3, keeping the picture's size and each line's box), every line from 0.3 confidence stored with its confidence, recorded with no lines when there is none and written only for the preview it was read from.
  - If a preview does not decode, or a reading fails, then nothing shall be recorded and the still shall stay owed.
  - Text shall be read in a child process of Otos's own (`Otos --read-text`).
  - If a failure loses Vision's text reader for that process (`CRImageReaderError.e5rtError` with status 13, which macOS's purge of the compiled-model cache under disk pressure causes), then that child shall end — the page ends there, every still not read stays owed, `op=search.text phase=sessionEnded` is logged, and the index's retry after its backoff reads the rest in a new child, without a relaunch.
  - If a child exits while reading a picture, without answering, then that picture shall be left owed and paged past for the pass (`op=search.text phase=sessionExited`), and the rest of the page read by a new child, so one picture that ends every child never stops the others being read.
  - If a child neither answers nor exits within 20 minutes, then it shall be ended and its picture paged past the same way (`phase=sessionOverran`).
  - A child shall exit when its requests pipe ends, mid-reading or not, so it never outlives the Otos that started it — a session ended, a quit, a crash or a kill — and the window's teardown shall end the live child and wait for its exit, leaving the still whose reading it cut short owed.
  - Each child's first reading shall log its wait, whatever it came to (`op=search.text phase=firstReading waitMs=`), since that reading loads the system's text model, cold for minutes after a cache purge, while the window says it is still reading.
  - If a child is started with arguments it does not know, then it shall end at once and read nothing.
  - The empty readings recorded for undecodable previews before that rule are dropped once (schema v53) for every asset with a stored preview, so each is read again from it, never from its original.
  - Search shall match every stored line, through an index in the catalog (schema v74: `search_text_folded` and the FTS5 table `search_text_word`, `SearchTextWords`), written in the transaction that stores each reading and gone with it by cascade, built by v74 from the stored readings.
  - A query's words shall be one read of it on the window lane, each candidate decided by the same match (`SearchTextQuery.matches(folded:)`), a stale reading matching nothing.
  - The index's launch pass shall read no reading; a search shown shall follow readings as they are written.
  - The Info pane shall show the lines at full confidence (APP-041).
  - Text shall be read after every embedding is made.
  Example: Given text drawn in a picture, it is read back with full confidence; given a blank picture, no lines; given a stale source, nothing is written; given a recognition that fails about one picture, that still stays owed while the rest of the page is read and a retry reads it; given the reading process's reader lost, the page ends there, every still not read stays owed, and the next page is read by a new reading process; given the index's pass ends that way, its retry reads the rest; given Otos's reading process, it answers a preview through its pipe, a picture that does not decode as such, and a reading process that is gone ends its session; given one started with unknown arguments, it reads nothing; given the system's text model (a live run), Otos's reading process reads a preview's text as the app's own process did; given a picture whose reading ends its session, it is paged past and the rest of the page is read by a new session; given status 10 of the same case, it fails one reading; given an empty reading of a stored preview from before, it is owed again after v53 while one with lines and one without a preview stay; given the current one, the still is no longer owed and search matches every line while the Info pane reads only the certain one; given a multilingual corpus and every query cut from it, the text index answers exactly what matching every reading answers, reading the index first and each row by key; given a reading replaced, emptied, made stale by a new preview, or its asset proven deleted, the index follows it; given the index's launch pass, it visits no row of the readings or their index; given v74 over stored readings, each that holds a word is indexed; given a picture classified, every confident label is one the gate holds, most confident first. given a child whose requests pipe ends, or whose parent is killed, while its reading thread is blocked, it exits; given the reader's shutdown during a reading, the child is gone when it returns, no other starts, and the still being read stays owed.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/SearchTextReading.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/SearchTextChild.swift`, `Otos/main.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/SearchLabelGate.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/SearchEmbeddingModel.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/PhotoSearchIndex.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/SearchTextStorage.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/SearchEmbeddingStorage.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSchema.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStorePersistence.swift`

- **ENR-029** If the renderer cannot decode a still, then Otos shall record the decoder that could not read it (`undecodable_by`: Otos's decode revision and the macOS build, `ImageRenderer.decoderIdentity`), alongside the rendering that fell back to QuickLook's thumbnail (ENR-016). [verified]
  - A still a decoder reads shall clear it.
  - If a still's bytes cannot be read, then it shall get no verdict and no stamp: the read is transient and retried, never stamped with QuickLook's thumbnail and no preview.
  - At launch, every still another still decoder could not read shall lose its rendering stamp and its verdict, so enrichment renders it again, and the count released shall be logged (`op=catalog.start phase=undecodableReleased stills=`).
  - A video's verdict shall be its frame reader's (ENR-031).
  - The hand-back shall seek those stills through `idx_media_asset_undecodable` rather than scanning every row (`OtosTests/CatalogStoreQueryPlanTests.swift#placeCellAndUndecodableReadsAreIndexDriven`).
  - The `v25_media_asset_undecodable_by` migration marked each still rendered with a stamp and no preview as undecodable by Otos's first decoder, so each is tried once.
  Example: Given bytes no decoder reads, the result names `ImageRenderer.decoderIdentity` as undecodable, with no preview; given a readable JPEG, the verdict is decoded; given a file that cannot be read, the outcome is transient; given launch, a still another decoder could not read loses its rendering stamp and its verdict while one this decoder could not read and a decoded still keep theirs; given the v25 migration, a still stamped with no preview is retried while a still with a preview and a video are not.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/ImageRenderer.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MediaEnricher.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/AssetMetadata.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStorePersistence.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSchema.swift`

- **ENR-030** Otos shall name every located item for the Places filter (ADR 0051). [verified]
  - The map shall be cut into cells 0.02° on a side, generated by SQLite from the stored primary location into indexed columns (`asset_metadata.place_cell_latitude` / `place_cell_longitude`) exactly as Swift computes them, the north pole and the 180th meridian in the cell below them so every cell's center is a valid coordinate.
  - `PlaceNameReader` shall ask Apple Maps once per cell, from the cell's center, never an item's own coordinate, for its country and its town with context, stored with the Mac's language and script (`PlaceNameLanguage`, never its region).
  - The catalog shall keep one language's names, so a drain in a new language forgets the others' and names every cell again, and a change of region alone asks nothing.
  - Names stored under a full locale before v43 are re-keyed to its language by v45, so an upgrade asks nothing already named in the Mac's language.
  - A cell shall be stored with no names only for an answer that there are none (`placemarkNotFound`, no map item, a refused coordinate).
  - If any other outcome occurs, then it shall store nothing, be logged and end the pass: the reader arms a backoff (2 s doubling to 5 min, reset by a named cell) that it reports as its wake, so it is quiet until then and asks Maps nothing before it, and the wake goes on with the cells after the failed one and asks the failed cell again when the walk starts over.
  - The unnamed cells shall be read through the place-cell index at launch, in a new language and after a change that names no rows.
  - A change that names its rows (an enrichment flush) shall read only those rows' cells, by key, never every located record.
  - The filter's places shall walk the scope's rows (a wide scope's date order index), each located row's cell found by its coordinate in `idx_asset_metadata_location`.
  - A naming shall be a catalog change.
  Example: Given located photos in Helsinki, Espoo and Sydney, three cells are each asked from their centers in the Mac's language, a throttled and an unexplained failure each waited out once and asked again, Sydney stored with no names; given every naming failing, no cell is stored, and the reader's pass ends with the reader quiescent until its backoff (`wake=backoff`), its next pass started by that wake; given another region, nothing is asked; given another language, every cell is named again and only the new language's names are offered; given names stored under `en_US` before v43, an English Mac has no unnamed cell after the upgrade and a Finnish one has every cell unnamed; given a photo at 90° N, 180° E, its cell is (4499, 8999) in SQL and Swift; the unnamed cells' read walks `idx_asset_metadata_place_cell` and the options' places read no `asset_metadata` table row; given the owner's shape, the places walk counts what every located row's stored cell names.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Models/PlaceModels.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/PlaceNameReader.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSchema.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogQueryGateway.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModel.swift`

- **ENR-032** Every still of a library or an Immich account shall be described by Apple's on-device language model (ADR 0053), never through Private Cloud Compute. [verified]
  - Once its faces are read from its stored preview and its text and scene labels are current, `PhotoDescriptionWriter` shall hand the model that preview at 1,024 px (never the original, so nothing is downloaded) and `PhotoDescriptionPrompt`'s facts.
  - The facts shall be the capture day and part of day, the map cell's town and country, each named face's position left to right with its person's name and age on that day (whole years, months under two, about from a birth year alone) and whether anyone else in it is unnamed (a face given to no one, an unnamed or a hidden person; faces carried onto the preview through its edit, one the crop leaves out not told).
  - The facts shall come with instructions made for the photo: a photo naming no one is described with instructions that never mention names or ages, one whose faces are all named asks for each by name and no other, and only one with unnamed faces too says everyone else stays unnamed; a named photo with no age given is told to state no age, one with ages to give no other.
  - The facts shall also include the certain text and the confident labels, each only from a reading current for the still's preview and thumbnail as they are now (`PhotoDescriptionStorage.readingsSQL`, ADR 0048).
  - `PhotoDescriptionWriter` shall store its answer in `photo_description` (v58) with a source naming the preview, the faces' stamp, each face's person, name, birthday and hidden flag, the place names, the capture time and the prompt revision, so any change to them owes a new description, and a write shall land only while the source still matches.
  - A declined answer shall be stored as declined.
  - If a preview fails, or a request the model's typed errors attribute to the photo (its context too large, its answer undecodable, its time run out) fails, then nothing shall be written and the still shall be passed over for 15 minutes.
  - If any other request fails, then the failure shall be the model's — not available, lacking a capability every request needs, or failing in its service with no public case (`PhotoDescriber.Failure`) — and shall stop the drain for 15 minutes, and a busy one for a minute (`time: backoff`), so a model that fails costs one request per pacing, never one per owed still.
  - Descriptions shall be made one request at a time, newest capture first: the owed stills are found in one newest-first walk (`CandidateWalk`, ADR 0009), and a change that names no rows (a person, a place name) during it restarts the walk from the newest still at its next page, so an import is described before an older backlog.
  - A change that names its rows shall owe those stills alone, read by key before the walk's next page, and never restart it.
  - A walk shall be owed only by the first subscription, a change of a kind it reads that names no rows and a pacing that ended; a ring that owes none reads nothing, since every still the last finished walk passed over waits on its pacing.
  Example: Given a still whose faces, text and labels are read, it is owed a description; before any of them, it is not; given a person named, renamed, given a birthday or hidden, it is owed again and the model is told the new facts; given an answer for facts since changed, nothing is written; given a declined answer, the Info pane reads declined; given an unavailable model, nothing is written and the still stays owed; given every request failing in the model's service, one request is made and the writer holds; given every request failing over its photo, each owed still is asked once; given a crop, only the faces inside it are told, placed on the cropped picture; given new pictures of a still whose text and labels were read from its old ones, the model is told neither; given a text reading written for one still, that still is read by its key and no walk is read.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/PhotoDescriptionWriter.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/PhotoDescriber.swift`, `OtosKit/Models/PhotoDescriptionPrompt.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/PhotoDescriptionStorage.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStore.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogSchema.swift`, `OtosKit/Stores/CatalogStorePersistence.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModel.swift`

- **ENR-033** Heavy background work shall start only while the machine has room for it (ADR 0057). [verified]
  - One owner, `BackgroundRoom`, shall hold the machine's conditions — the kernel's memory-pressure level (`kern.memorystatus_vm_pressure_level`), read at each decision, the thermal state and Low Power Mode, the last two delivered by `MachineConditionsMonitor`.
  - The memory-pressure source shall be only a doorbell whose report makes the room read the kernel, whose level wins (the report is the level only when the kernel cannot be read).
  - Reopening shall wait on the kernel's level read normal: after a rise the source announced, on the source's return report, with no look on time, and otherwise, while the level held is above normal and only then, the level read again every 5 s.
  - `BackgroundRoom` shall decide per kind of work whether a new unit may start.
  - Under memory `critical`, a `serious` or `critical` thermal state, or Low Power Mode, no description request, page of text reading, background download of a cloud original or background heavy load (a People pass, a duplicates build, the search index's build, a mirror pairing) shall start.
  - Under memory `warning` none of them shall start but a description request, which also starts while Otos is the active app.
  - A description request in flight shall never be stopped by a change of level.
  - A held unit shall start exactly once, in the step that delivers conditions that open its kind, never on a timer.
  - A download, load or render the person waits on, the load the current view needs, and every window read shall never be held.
  - A text-reading helper idle when the room holds text reading shall be let go at once, and one mid-reading only at `critical`, its reading staying owed.
  - A download already running shall not be stopped.
  - An owner waiting shall name the hold in its status (`heldBy`, in `op=owners.status` and `op=owners.pending`), and every change shall log `op=background.room phase=changed` with each kind's hold.
  - Each platform report shall reach the room on the thread it arrives on: the memory-pressure source's handler runs on a global queue and claims no actor, so a report never traps in an isolation check.
  Example: Given `warning`, three text-reading waiters wait and none starts, and a description is open; given a thermal state of `serious` beside `warning`, a description is held for heat; given `normal`, each starts once and a second hold and opening starts none; given a waiter cancelled while held, it is never let start; given `warning`, a description starts at once while text reading is held; given a held room, only the load the view needs is granted and the others start once each, in order, when it opens; given a held room, the text reader starts no helper and stops its page before the first reading, and reads once it opens; given a helper idle between pages, it is let go when the room holds; given a held room, a background download waits with the materializer's status naming the hold while the photo on screen downloads, and the waiting one starts once it opens; given `warning` on the kernel with 20 % available, the writer describes the still with one request and names no hold; given `critical`, it makes no request and names `memoryCritical`, and describes the still once `warning` is delivered; given a request in flight when `critical` is delivered, it is not cancelled and its answer is written; given `critical`, a serious or critical thermal state, or Low Power Mode, a description waits naming the hold and starts once in the step that delivers `warning`; given a description in flight through `warning` and `critical`, it finishes, raises no requirement, and the next one starts at `warning` while a People pass waits; given the kernel at warning and a silent source, every kind but descriptions is held, a waiter waits and a heavy load asked for after the rise is not granted; given the kernel back at normal and a silent source, the held waiter starts at the next look, and a later rise found by a decision arms the look again; given the source and the kernel disagreeing, the kernel's level holds; given a source report with the kernel at normal, the held waiter starts in that step; given an unreadable kernel, the held level stays and the source's report is the level; given the room ended, its waiting look is cancelled; given a rise the source announced, the room does not look again, opens in the step of the source's normal report, and looks again at a later rise it finds itself; given a memory-pressure or power-state report delivered off the main thread, the room holds it and nothing traps.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/BackgroundRoom.swift`, `OtosKit/Support/MachineConditionsMonitor.swift`, `OtosKit/Support/ProcessResources.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/LoadAdmission.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MaterializationCoordinator.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MaterializationAdmissionPolicy.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/PhotoDescriptionWriter.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/PhotoSearchIndex.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/SearchTextReading.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/SearchTextChild.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/OwnerQuiescence.swift`, `OtosKit/ViewModels/CatalogWindowViewModel.swift`, `OtosKit/OtosApp.swift`

- **ENR-034** A cloud-only original whose row owes only its digest shall be downloaded for it like any owed product (ADR 0009 §12), so every photo is checked for identical copies: routed as a background materialization demand under the free-space floor and the background room's holds, and its digest read from the downloaded copy. [verified]
  - A row that owes anything else shall be downloaded once and its digest taken in the same read.
  - Nothing shall hold a row for local bytes.
  Example: Given a dataless row owing only its digest, one download is requested and its digest is read from that copy, with nothing held; given a dataless row owing its rendering, it is downloaded once and read for metadata, rendering and digest together.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinatorWorkSelection.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinatorCompletion.swift`

- **ENR-035** A row the pacing ledger paces — its ranged read unanswered, its original sent only for the user's work, its RAW's camera support not fetched, or its video's faces unread — shall keep its Store claim, held by the ledger (`EnrichmentSchedule.holdPacedClaims`, ADR 0009), so no pass claims it only to give it back: it is claimed at most once per pacing, not once per pass. [verified]
  - The hold shall end when its pacing passes (`releaseDuePacedClaims`, the coordinator's wake), when a `.membership` change names it or names none (its claim goes back once and the ledger compares its source at the next claim, ENR-007), or when its root is no longer offered; shutdown shall give the claim back.
  - A held paced row shall take no place in the claim window.
  - Each paced row shall record why it paces (`RetryLedgerEntry.cause`), and `op=owners.trouble owner=enrichment cause= count=` shall report the paced rows by cause at each status moment.
  Example: Given a paced row, three passes read it once and give back no claim, and the pass at its pacing's end dispatches it; given a membership change naming it, it is claimed again once and held again; given cloud-only rows owing only their digest and a video whose faces went unread, five later passes claim nothing, the video is read once and nothing is downloaded.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentSchedule.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinatorWorkSelection.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinatorCompletion.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinator.swift`, `script/lib/scorecard.py`

- **ENR-036** A RAW shall be developed only once Apple reports its camera's support for the chosen decoder is on the Mac (ADR 0035). [verified]
  - Opening it (`ImageRenderer.openRAW`, for enrichment, the loupe, export and the edit panel) shall ask `CIRAWFilter.downloadResources` and wait for its report, which a present camera answers at once, with a one-minute deadline (ADR 0035).
  - If a fetch fails or times out, then it shall throw `RAWResourcesUnavailable`, classified by its error's domain and code, logged as `op=render.rawResources phase=failed`.
  - It is no verdict on the file, so enrichment shall neither stamp the RAW undecodable nor mark its source unreadable: the row is paced for `rawSupportUnavailable` (ENR-035) and the Activity popover says `Camera support for N items couldn’t be downloaded. Otos will try again.`
  Example: Given a fetch that Core Image reports on another queue after it returns, the development waits for the report; given a timed-out download, the error carries its domain and code and is no `RenderError`; given an enricher that cannot fetch a RAW's support, the row is paced for that cause with its claim held, reported as one `rawSupportUnavailable` item, and not developed again by later passes before its pacing passes.
  Implemented in: `OtosKit/Services/ImageRenderer.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/MediaEnricher.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/EnrichmentCoordinatorCompletion.swift`, `OtosKit/Services/OwnerQuiescence.swift`, `OtosKit/Views/ActivityPresentation.swift`
